### [37] [Neural Estimation of Pairwise Mutual Information in Masked Discrete Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20187)
*Jai Sharma,Yifan Wang,Bryan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种从预训练掩码扩散模型(MDM)隐藏状态直接估计成对条件互信息(MI)的神经框架，实现了3-5倍推理加速，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型主要暴露边缘条件分布，未显式表示变量间依赖关系，而理解变量间依赖对于可解释性和高效生成至关重要。

Method: 构建神经网络框架，利用模型自身条件分布计算的真实互信息作为监督信号，从预训练MDM的隐藏状态直接估计成对条件互信息，单次前向传播预测完整MI矩阵，并基于此识别条件独立变量子集以实现MI引导的并行解码。

Result: 在数独和ESM-C蛋白质序列生成任务上，MI图恢复了已知结构约束；相比顺序解码，推理时前向传播次数减少3-5倍，同时保持生成质量；优于基于熵的并行化方法。

Conclusion: 该方法成功捕获了模型的内部依赖结构信念，实现了高效的并行解码，为掩码扩散模型的依赖建模和加速推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding dependencies between variables is critical for interpretability and efficient generation in masked diffusion models (MDMs), yet these models primarily expose marginal conditional distributions and do not explicitly represent inter-variable dependence. We propose a neural framework for estimating pairwise conditional mutual information (MI) directly from the hidden states of a pretrained MDM, using ground-truth MI computed from the model's own conditional distributions for supervision. The resulting estimator captures the model's internal belief about dependency structure and predicts the full MI matrix in a single forward pass, enabling MI-guided parallel decoding by identifying conditionally independent subsets of variables. We evaluate our approach on Sudoku and protein sequence generation with ESM-C, where the MI maps recover known structural constraints and enable a 3-5x magnitude reduction in inference-time forward passes compared to sequential decoding, while preserving generative quality and outperforming entropy-based parallelization methods.

</details>


### [38] [GraphDiffMed: Knowledge-Constrained Differential Attention with Pharmacological Graph Priors for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20188)
*Krati Saxena,Tomohiro Shibata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraphDiffMed框架，通过双尺度差分注意力机制和药理学知识约束，有效解决了EHR药物推荐中的噪声过滤问题，在推荐质量和安全性之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电子健康记录(EHRs)推荐安全有效的药物组合是一个核心临床AI问题，但由于患者轨迹长、噪声多且临床异质性强，该问题极具挑战性。现有方法通常要么擅长跨就诊的时序建模，要么擅长药理学知识整合(如药物-药物相互作用DDIs)，但很少能同时实现这两点并能稳健地抑制噪声。

Method: 提出了GraphDiffMed框架，这是一个基于双尺度差分注意力v2的知识约束药物推荐框架。在就诊内和就诊间两个层面应用差分注意力机制，分别过滤就诊内部和纵向历史中的虚假信号，同时在学习过程中融入药理学约束。

Result: 在MIMIC-III数据集上的实验和消融研究表明，该设计在推荐质量和排序性能上持续优于强基线方法，同时实现了更优的安全性性能平衡。研究发现，在实验设置下，仅使用人口统计辅助特征的配置表现最佳。

Conclusion: GraphDiffMed证明了将噪声感知注意力与药理学约束相结合，能够产生更可靠且具有临床意义的药物推荐。研究已开源代码。

Abstract: Recommending safe and effective medication combinations from electronic health records (EHRs) is a core clinical AI problem, yet it remains difficult because patient trajectories are long, noisy, and clinically heterogeneous. Existing methods typically excel at either temporal modeling across visits or pharmacological knowledge integration (e.g., drug-drug interactions, DDIs), but rarely achieve both while robustly suppressing noise. We present GraphDiffMed, a knowledge-constrained medication recommendation framework built on dual-scale Differential Attention v2. Differential attention is applied at both intra-visit and inter-visit levels to filter spurious signals within encounters and across longitudinal history, while pharmacological constraints are incorporated during learning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and ablation studies show that this design consistently improves recommendation quality and ranking over strong baselines while achieving a more favorable safety performance balance. We further find that the strongest-performing configuration uses only demographic auxiliary features under our experimental setting. Overall, GraphDiffMed demonstrates that combining noise-aware attention with pharmacological constraints yields more reliable and clinically meaningful medication recommendation. We open-source our code at https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed.

</details>


### [39] [TabPFN-MT: A Natively Multitask In-Context Learner for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20234)
*Cormac Cureton,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TabPFN-MT模型，通过扩展的多目标合成先验训练实现表格数据多任务上下文学习，在中小型数据集上达到最新最优性能，同时将多任务推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的先验数据拟合网络(PFNs)专为单任务推理设计，在上下文中预测多个目标值需要重复前向调用，无法进行任务间信息共享，存在计算效率低和信息利用不充分的问题。

Method: 提出TabPFN-MT模型：1)在扩展的多目标合成先验上进行训练以捕获上下文中的任务间依赖关系；2)使用扩展的y编码器和共享解码器头部实现多任务上下文学习和同时推理；3)基于上下文学习而非传统梯度训练，专门针对中小型数据集优化。

Result: 在344个数据集上的广泛评估表明：1)在小中型数据集(平均少于1000样本)上建立了深度表格多任务学习的新SOTA；2)在多任务数据集上达到4.89的平均准确率排名，是所有测试模型中最高的；3)与最新的单任务集成方法保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: TabPFN-MT成功实现了表格数据多任务学习，在保持高度竞争性能的同时，将T个任务的推理成本从O(T)前向传播减少到O(1)前向传播，为多目标表格应用提供了巨大的计算效率提升。

Abstract: Prior-Data Fitted networks (PFNs) have been very successful in tabular contexts, handling prediction tasks in context. However, they are designed for single-task inference, meaning that predicting several target values within a context requires repeated forward calls and precludes inter-task information sharing. We propose TabPFN-MT, which is trained on an expanded multi-target synthetic prior to capture inter-task dependencies in context. This model uses an expanded $y$-encoder and a shared decoder head to enable multitask in-context learning and simultaneous inference. The model is uniquely specialized for small-to-medium datasets by relying on in-context learning rather than traditional gradient-based training. Within this regime (averaging fewer than 1,000 samples), extensive evaluations across 344 datasets demonstrate that TabPFN-MT establishes a new state-of-the-art for deep tabular multitask learning. Furthermore, despite the inherent compute asymmetry of joint optimization, our model remains highly competitive with the latest state-of-the-art single-task ensembles. Notably, on multitask datasets it achieves an overall Accuracy rank of 4.89, the highest average rank among all models tested. Crucially, TabPFN-MT delivers this highly competitive performance while reducing the inference cost for $T$ tasks from $O(T)$ to $O(1)$ forward passes, offering a massive computational efficiency improvement for multi-target tabular applications.

</details>


### [40] [Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20235)
*Wei Huang,Andi Han,Mingyuan Bai,Huanjian Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Kenji Fukumizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型通过“坍缩-精炼”机制克服维度灾难的原理，提出了Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD)框架，在流形学习和密度估计上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高维数据生成中表现卓越，但其训练如何在高维数据位于低维流形时高效学习得分函数并克服维度灾难，缺乏理论解释。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出“坍缩-精炼”机制：小噪声尺度下得分函数的奇异性驱动去噪映射快速坍缩至数据流形投影；中等噪声尺度下训练精细化流形上的内在密度。基于此原理构建SiLD两阶段框架，仅通过单一去噪得分匹配目标实现流形学习与密度估计，替代VAE的KL正则化。

Result: 理论证明样本复杂度依赖内在维度而非环境维度。在Stacked MNIST、CelebA变体及分子生成基准实验中，SiLD生成质量与VAE-LDM持平或更优，重建性能一致提升。

Conclusion: 本文从几何角度解释了扩散模型如何克服维度灾难，提出的SiLD框架在理论和实验上均验证了该机制的有效性，为生成模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. We identify a collapse-and-refine mechanism driven by the geometry of the score function itself: at small noise scales, the diverging singularity of the score drives a rapid dimensional collapse of the induced denoising map onto the data manifold projection; at moderate noise scales, training refines the intrinsic density on the learned manifold. We instantiate this principle as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD), a two-stage framework in which both manifold learning and density estimation emerge from a single denoising score matching objective, replacing the heuristic KL regularization of VAE-based latent diffusion models. We prove that the resulting sample complexity depends on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. Experiments on Stacked MNIST, CelebA variants, and molecular generation benchmarks show that SiLD matches or outperforms VAE-based LDMs in generation quality and consistently improves reconstruction, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>

Abstract: Battery health diagnostics today rely overwhelmingly on electrochemical signals measured at the cell terminals. The release contains 5,600 PulseBat-conditioned grounded samples, 600 synthetic sensor-anomaly samples derived from clean parents, and 560 low-voltage Regime-B extrapolation samples. A cell-disjoint, parent-child-leakage-free primary benchmark split is verified to contain zero overlapping cells, zero cross-split parent-child pairs, and zero sample-ID overlap. We define three primary benchmark tasks: SOH regression, second-life classification, and anomaly detection, plus an auxiliary anomaly-subtype classification task. A controlled label-shuffle ablation collapses SOH regression from R^2 approximately 0.77 to approximately 0, confirming that the bridge encodes input SOH non-trivially rather than producing label-aligned artifacts. This work provides a public benchmark for magnetic-sensing battery diagnostics while paired magnetic-electrochemical measurements remain scarce.

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geometry-Lite探针方法，通过分解多层安全信号的几何结构，揭示了大语言模型中安全证据主要表现为持久的边界位置几何特征，而非层间运动信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示级安全探针虽然平均检测性能较强，但无法解释安全与不安全提示分离的几何机制，特别是安全证据如何在各层间形成、哪些几何特征支持低误报决策、以及在基准变化时哪些几何偏差保持稳定。

Result: Geometry-Lite性能优于单层探针且接近原始多层分数堆叠；安全证据主要通过持久的边界位置几何特征表达，最终或极端边界和不安全侧层占用主导检测性能；有限差分漂移和结构摘要对AUROC贡献较小，但在低误报率阈值下可提供召回率修正；在基准变化下，类别条件均值几何比优化线性边界更可靠地保持分离。

Conclusion: 提示级安全证据主要不是层间运动信号，而是持久的层间边界几何特征，其有用成分和读出级偏差在决策关键区域中显现，为理解大语言模型安全机制提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Prompt-level safety probes for large language models use hidden-state representations to separate safe from unsafe prompts, but strong average detection performance does not explain the geometry of this separation. In particular, it remains unclear how safety evidence is formed across layers, which aspects of that layer-wise geometry support low-false-positive decisions, and which geometric biases remain stable under benchmark shift. We study this as an empirical decomposition problem and introduce Geometry-Lite, a compact prompt-level probe that maps each layer's final prompt-token representation to signed margins under centroid, local-neighborhood, and supervised linear-boundary readouts, then summarizes the resulting margin profiles by boundary position, layer-to-layer change, and coarse shape. Across nine instruction-tuned backbones ($1.2$B--$70$B) and seven safety benchmarks, Geometry-Lite improves over single-layer probes while remaining close to raw multi-layer score stacking, making it a useful instrument for analyzing the multi-layer safety signal. The decomposition shows that safety evidence is expressed primarily through persistent boundary-position geometry: final or extremal margins and unsafe-side layer occupancy dominate aggregate detection performance. In contrast, finite-difference drift and structural summaries add little to pooled AUROC, although drift can provide small recall-oriented corrections under shifted low-FPR thresholds. Under benchmark shift, optimized linear boundaries are sharp on the training mixture, whereas class-conditional mean geometry retains separation more reliably on a predefined hard held-out subset. Overall, prompt-level safety evidence is not primarily a layer-to-layer motion signal, but a persistent layer-wise margin geometry whose useful components and readout-level biases become visible in decision-critical regimes.

</details>


### [43] [FedCritic: Serverless Federated Critic Learning-based Resource Allocation for Multi-Cell OFDMA in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21418)
*Amin Farajzadeh,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedCritic，一种无服务器的联邦多智能体Actor-Critic框架，用于解决6G超密集网络中强干扰耦合下的分布式下行资源管理问题，在无需中心协调器的情况下实现了更优的网络性能和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G超密集网络中，激进的频率复用放大了小区间干扰（ICI），使得多小区OFDMA调度和功率控制在邻近小区间强耦合。现有的集中训练分布式执行（CTDE）方法需要中心化的Critic学习和联合轨迹聚合，存在协调开销大、扩展性差的问题。因此，需要开发一种能够在干扰耦合和长期QoS约束下实现高效分布式资源管理的方案。

Method: 提出FedCritic框架，使用虚拟队列缺欠权重来强制执行长期QoS约束。该框架采用无服务器的联邦多智能体Actor-Critic架构，通过在干扰图上基于轻量级Gossip的参数平均来联邦化Critic，从而在无需中心协调器的情况下实现稳定的价值估计，同时保持策略的本地性。与CTDE方法不同，FedCritic避免了集中式的Critic学习和联合轨迹聚合。

Result: 在干扰丰富的复用-1场景下的仿真表明，FedCritic相比非协调和CTDE基线方法：（1）提高了平均信干噪比（SINR）和小区边缘速率；（2）增加了全网平均总速率和公平性；（3）实现了更稳定的训练过程；（4）降低了协调开销。

Conclusion: FedCritic成功实现了在无中心协调器情况下的分布式下行资源管理，通过Gossip机制联邦化Critic学习，有效解决了强干扰耦合和长期QoS约束下的资源分配问题，为6G超密集网络提供了一种高效、可扩展的分布式解决方案。

Abstract: In sixth-generation (6G) ultra-dense networks, aggressive frequency reuse amplifies inter-cell interference (ICI), making multi-cell orthogonal frequency-division multiple access (OFDMA) scheduling and power control strongly coupled across neighboring cells. We study distributed downlink resource management -- joint subcarrier scheduling and power allocation -- under interference coupling and long-term per-user quality-of-service (QoS) minimum-rate constraints. By using virtual-queue deficit weights to enforce long-term QoS, we develop FedCritic, a serverless federated multi-agent actor-critic framework with decentralized execution. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches that require centralized critic learning and joint trajectory aggregation, FedCritic federates the critic through lightweight gossip-based parameter averaging over the interference graph, enabling stable value estimation without a central coordinator while keeping policies local. Simulations in an interference-rich reuse-1 setting show that FedCritic improves mean signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and cell-edge rate, increases network-wide average sum-rate and fairness relative to non-coordinated and CTDE baselines, and achieves more stable training with lower coordination overhead.

</details>

*Xin-De Wang,Zhi-Rui Chen,Ze-Feng Gao,Peng-Jie Guo,Cheng Mu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Method: 开发LEAP框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)训练领域专用大语言模型从钙钛矿添加剂文献中提取机制相关知识；(2)通过可解释描述符表示候选分子；(3)将这些描述符集成到贝叶斯优化工作流中，在低数据条件下进行不确定性感知的优先级排序，并结合专家可行性审查形成闭环迭代优化。

Result: 在未见文献的基准测试中，领域专用模型在机制一致性推理方面优于通用模型；实验验证显示，经过三轮筛选，后期轮次筛选的6-CDQ和2-CNA处理器件平均效率分别达到20.13%和20.87%，均优于对照组的19.25%，冠军器件效率达到21.32%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将文献驱动的机制描述符与贝叶斯优化和专家可行性审查相结合，能够有效支持钙钛矿光伏器件中机制感知的添加剂优先级排序，为添加剂发现提供了一种新的高效筛选范式。

Abstract: Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. We develop LEAP(LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), an expert-in-the-loop closed framework that couples a domain-specialized large language model(LLM) with active learning for iterative additive prioritization. The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [45] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GROW框架，通过将轨迹分解为状态-动作样本并计算样本间优势值，解决了GRPO在多轮开放世界VLM智能体中因需要完整轨迹而导致的上下文过长和噪声问题，在800多个Minecraft任务上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界VLM智能体主要依赖监督微调(SFT)，而先进的强化学习算法GRPO在多轮任务中未被有效应用，主要原因是标准GRPO需要完整轨迹作为训练样本，导致上下文过长和引入噪声。

Method: 提出GROW强化学习框架：1) 将收集的轨迹分解为状态-动作样本；2) 计算样本间的优势值而非将完整轨迹视为单一实体；3) 提供代理分析证明在简化假设下，即使分组样本基于不同局部状态而非相同提示上下文，目标函数仍能保持GRPO的核心相对策略优化信号。

Result: 在超过800个Minecraft任务上的实验表明，该方法达到了最先进(SOTA)的性能水平，验证了所提出的强化学习框架对开放世界VLM智能体的有效性。

Conclusion: GROW框架成功将GRPO强化学习算法应用于开放世界VLM智能体的多轮任务训练，通过轨迹分解有效解决了上下文过长和噪声问题，为VLM智能体的强化学习训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

</details>


### [46] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CP-MoE框架，通过引入暂态专家（transient expert）机制，解决了基于LoRA的MoE持续学习方法中知识隔离与灾难性遗忘之间的权衡问题，在单模态和多模态持续学习任务上均取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LoRA的MoE持续学习方法面临一个根本性权衡：过度隔离专家会限制任务间的知识迁移，而允许任务特定更新覆盖重要参数则会导致严重的灾难性遗忘，目前尚无有效方案平衡二者。

Method: 提出CP-MoE框架，核心包含两个组件：（1）一致性保留路由偏置，利用暂态专家估计与稳定专家的表示相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家；（2）暂态专家引导的正则化机制，在合并时选择性保护重要的历史参数。暂态专家负责捕获早期任务特定更新并指导其整合到稳定专家中。

Result: 在SuperNI基准测试（覆盖多样化序列语言任务）上达到SOTA性能并展现更强的零样本迁移能力；在VQA v2数据集上有效扩展至多模态视觉推理，持续降低遗忘程度并优于强大的MoE基线模型。

Conclusion: CP-MoE成功缓解了参数干扰和遗忘问题，同时保持了跨任务知识迁移能力，为大语言模型和视觉-语言模型的持续学习提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Graph Transductive Sharpening: Leveraging Unlabeled Predictions in Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20248)
*Brown Zaz,Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transductive Sharpening (TS)方法，通过最小化未标记节点的预测熵来利用其训练信号，在无需修改骨干架构的情况下提升了半监督节点分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直推式设置中，现有研究主要关注架构创新而忽略了训练目标。观察到未标记节点的预测包含有用的训练信号，但标准监督目标因缺少真实标签而将其丢弃。受交叉熵分解为标签依赖对齐项和标签独立熵项的启发，作者提出利用预测置信度来提取这些信号。

Method: 提出Transductive Sharpening (TS)方法，这是一种损失层面的改进。TS最小化未标记节点的预测熵，同时在标记节点上平衡这种效应。该方法不要求对骨干架构进行任何修改，可以与现有模型直接结合。

Result: 在广泛的节点分类基准测试中，Transductive Sharpening带来了一致的性能提升，无需改变骨干架构。实验验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视训练目标这一正交方向，Transductive Sharpening证明了损失函数设计在直推式学习中的重要性，为半监督节点分类提供了一个简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In the transductive setting, where the full graph is observed but node labels are only partially available, progress in semi-supervised node classification has largely focused on architectural innovation. In this paper, we revisit an orthogonal axis: the training objective. We start from a simple observation: transductive models produce predictions for every node during training, including nodes without labels. These unlabeled-node predictions may contain useful training signal, but standard supervised objectives discard them because no ground-truth labels are available. Inspired by the decomposition of cross-entropy into a label-dependent alignment term and a label-independent entropy term, we propose prediction confidence as a natural way to extract this signal in the absence of labels. This motivates Transductive Sharpening (TS): a loss-level modification that minimizes prediction entropy on unlabeled nodes while counterbalancing this effect on labeled nodes. We evaluate Transductive Sharpening across a wide range of node-classification benchmarks and observe consistent performance improvements without requiring any changes to the backbone architecture. Code is available at https://github.com/transductive-sharpening/tunedGNN.

</details>


### [48] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kernel Discovery框架，利用LLM驱动的进化算法在高维贝叶斯优化中自动发现高斯过程核函数，在5个基准上平均排名1.2/17。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维贝叶斯优化中的核函数设计依赖大量人工工程。现有自动化方法存在两大瓶颈：核搜索空间局限于基核的加法和乘法组合；基于LLM的方法需要以原始观测为条件，受限于上下文长度约束且难以提取有意义的模式。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的进化框架，采用两阶段方法：第一阶段LLM提出新颖的数学形式，第二阶段LLM将形式转换为可验证的可执行代码。框架搜索超越预定义组合规则的更广阔核空间，且无需以观测为条件。提出留一法连续排序概率分数(LOO-CRPS)作为选择标准，惩罚过拟合的核函数。

Result: 在5个高维贝叶斯优化基准测试中，该方法在17个竞争基线中取得1.2的平均排名，显著优于其他方法。进一步分析了发现的核函数，识别出哪些核函数在高维BO中带来改进。

Conclusion: Kernel Discovery框架成功解决了高维贝叶斯优化中核函数自动设计的挑战，通过LLM的两阶段生成策略和LOO-CRPS选择标准，能够发现超越传统组合规则的有效核函数，为高维优化问题提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

</details>


### [49] [LOSCAR-SGD: Local SGD with Communication-Computation Overlap and Delay-Corrected Sparse Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20866)
*Yassine Maziane,Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LOSCAR-SGD算法，首次在理论层面将通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠三种技术结合起来，用于解决分布式学习中的通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式学习（特别是大规模场景和联邦学习环境）中，通信是主要的瓶颈。虽然通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠这三种方法在实践中常被组合使用且效果显著，但目前缺乏将这三者结合的理论分析。

Method: 提出了一种名为LOSCAR-SGD的Local SGD方法，该方法仅通信模型坐标的一个稀疏子集，并在通信传输过程中继续进行优化计算。核心创新在于引入了一种延迟修正合并规则（delay-corrected merge rule），该规则能够在融合延迟同步信息的同时，保留重叠阶段所取得的优化进展。

Result: 在平滑非凸目标函数上给出了收敛性保证，并量化分析了稀疏性、重叠和工作者异构性对收敛速率的影响。实验结果表明，通信-计算重叠有效减少了训练时间，且延迟修正合并策略显著优于简单的覆盖策略。

Conclusion: 据我们所知，这是首个针对通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠三者结合的理论研究，为该组合方法的有效性提供了理论依据和实证支持。

Abstract: Communication is a major bottleneck in distributed learning, especially in large-scale settings and in federated learning environments with slow links. Three standard ways to reduce this cost are communication compression, local training, and communication-computation overlap. Methods that combine these ingredients are used in practice and have been found to be effective for large-scale training, but there is little theory for methods that combine all three. We study a heterogeneous-compute setting in which different workers may take different numbers of local steps, and we propose LOSCAR-SGD, a Local SGD method that communicates only a sparse subset of model coordinates and continues optimizing while communication is in flight. A key ingredient is a delay-corrected merge rule that incorporates delayed synchronized information without discarding the progress made during the overlap phase. We give convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives and show how sparsity, overlap, and worker heterogeneity affect the rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theory for this combination of ingredients. Experiments further show that communication-computation overlap reduces training time and that the delay-corrected merge outperforms naive overwriting.

</details>

*Rafał Topolnicki,Paweł Dłotko,Maciej Matyka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于卷积神经网络的框架，可直接从孔隙几何预测孔隙尺度速度场，并作为Lattice-Boltzmann模拟的初始条件，加速求解器收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔介质中流体流动的精确模拟面临复杂孔隙空间几何和Navier-Stokes方程计算成本高的挑战，尤其在需要重复模拟时，传统数值求解器在复杂多孔域中收敛缓慢。

Method: 采用带有跳跃连接的卷积编码器-解码器架构，通过自定义损失函数（结合速度重建、不可压缩性、固体内无流动条件、周期性约束和全局曲折度一致性）确保物理一致性，评估多种CNN骨干网络以确定最优架构。

Result: 训练模型在分布外样本（包括障碍物几何、边界条件、孔隙度和真实多孔结构变化）上具有良好泛化能力；将预测速度场作为Lattice-Boltzmann模拟的初始条件，在超过90%的测试案例中减少迭代次数。

Conclusion: 该神经网络框架能有效预测孔隙尺度速度场并保持物理一致性，作为数值求解器的热启动策略显著加速收敛，为多孔介质流动模拟提供了高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate simulation of fluid flow in porous media is challenging due to complex pore-space geometries and the computational cost of solving the Navier-Stokes equations. This difficulty is particularly important when repeated simulations are required, as standard numerical solvers may converge slowly in intricate porous domains. We present a neural-network-based framework for predicting pore-scale velocity fields directly from sample geometry. The method uses a convolutional encoder-decoder architecture with skip connections to preserve spatial detail while extracting multi-scale features. Physical consistency is encouraged through a custom loss function combining velocity reconstruction with incompressibility, no-flow conditions inside solids, periodicity constraints, and agreement with the global tortuosity index. We analyze the influence of the corresponding loss weights and quantify the contribution of individual loss components to prediction accuracy. Several CNN backbones are evaluated to identify architectures providing accurate and robust predictions. The generalization ability of the trained model is tested on samples outside the training distribution, including changes in obstacle geometry, boundary conditions, porosity, and realistic porous structures. Finally, we demonstrate a practical use of the predicted velocity fields as initial conditions for Lattice-Boltzmann simulations. This warm-start strategy accelerates solver convergence, reducing the number of iterations in over 90% of tested cases.

</details>

*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）联合训练自动驾驶汽车（SDC）和行人，以生成更真实的交互场景，特别是针对具有潜在人格特质的乱穿马路行为。实验表明，联合训练的SDC在目标达成率和碰撞率上显著优于基于规则的基线方法，且乱穿马路行为是导致碰撞的主要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶汽车仿真测试通常使用脚本化或简化的行人模型，无法捕捉真实人类过马路行为的异质性和不确定性，特别是在涉及乱穿马路的场景中。乱穿马路行为受车辆无法观察到的潜在人格特质支配，这限制了安全评估的真实性。

Method: 构建了一个MARL环境，使用多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）联合训练一辆SDC和12名行人。行人运动遵循脚本化的Dijkstra寻路算法，而强化学习策略控制高层次的通过/等待决策。乱穿马路概率取决于每个行人在情景开始时采样的人格特质，该特质对SDC隐藏。

Result: 在500回合评估中，联合训练的SDC达到78%的目标达成率和14%的碰撞率，而最佳基于规则的基线分别为35%和33%。乱穿马路占过马路事件的13%，但与62%的碰撞相关。与单智能体强化学习相比，MARL联合训练使碰撞减少了30%。

Conclusion: 联合训练自动驾驶汽车和行人的MARL方法能够产生更真实的交互场景，显著提高安全评估的有效性。速度差分指标显示SDC在靠近乱穿马路者时速度比在人行横道用户附近快2.65 m/s，表明乱穿马路遭遇未被预期。行人学会了在SDC高速接近时等待，从而减少了碰撞。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

</details>


### [52] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FBOS-RL框架，通过反馈驱动的双目标协同训练（EPA和ECC），解决了GRPO在任务超出模型能力时训练停滞的问题，实现了更快的训练速度和更高的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及其变体在强化学习训练中采用简单的采样方案，当任务超出策略模型当前能力时，难以产生高质量rollout，导致模型缺乏有效的梯度方向而训练停滞。与监督学习不同，强化学习没有显式的ground-truth目标，需要依赖高质量rollout作为隐式"教师"来指导参数更新。

Method: 提出Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic强化学习框架（FBOS-RL）。核心包括：（1）Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement：基于环境反馈让模型进行反馈引导的探索增强；（2）设计两个相互增强的训练目标：Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment (EPA，利用导向的策略对齐)和Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation (ECC，探索导向的能力培养)，形成正向飞轮效应。

Result: 在相同rollout数量下，FBOS-RL比GRPO和基于反馈的基线方法学习速度显著更快，最终达到更高的性能上限。训练过程中展现出更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数，EPA和ECC相互增强形成正向飞轮效应，显著提升了训练效率和最终性能上限。

Conclusion: FBOS-RL通过反馈驱动的双目标协同机制有效解决了传统GRPO方法的训练停滞问题，为大规模模型的强化学习对齐和推理能力解锁提供了更高效的训练框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

</details>


### [53] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了L-GRACE和L-BGRL两种基于链路表示的自监督学习模型，通过新型社区结构增强策略，显著提升了链路预测任务的性能，特别是在无属性图上表现优异，达到了有监督和自监督最先进方法的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例判别模型在图像和节点分类领域已展现卓越性能，但在链路预测任务上的研究相对较少。现有方法主要关注节点表示学习，而链路预测需要直接优化链路表示，因此需要专门针对该任务设计新的模型架构。

Method: 首先系统评估了现有自监督模型在链路预测任务上的性能，揭示数据增强过程的关键作用；随后提出了基于社区结构的新型结构增强策略；最后设计了L-GRACE和L-BGRL两个新模型，直接基于链路表示而非节点表示进行学习。

Result: 实验结果表明：现有方法的性能严重依赖于增强策略的选择；提出的社区结构增强对链路预测高度相关；L-GRACE和L-BGRL在无属性图上性能提升显著，并在有监督和自监督设置下均达到了最先进方法水平。

Conclusion: 链路预测任务需要专门的链路级表示学习框架，而非简单移植节点级方法。通过合理的结构增强策略和链路级建模，自监督学习可以在链路预测任务上取得与有监督方法相当的性能，为图表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recently, instance discrimination models have emerged as a major solution for self-supervised learning. Having already demonstrated its effectiveness in the image domain, instance discrimination learning is now proving equally convincing in the graph domain, in particular for node classification. However, fewer contributions have tackled the link prediction task. In this contribution, we propose to adapt existing methods to this context. We first provide a rigorous evaluation of existing self-supervised models in the field of link prediction, showing that the main performance depends on the augmentation process (like in computer vision). We then propose a new structural augmentation based on the community structure that is relevant for link prediction. Our main contribution introduces two new models, L-GRACE and L-BGRL, based on link representations instead of node representations, which improve the performance of the existing methods, especially on unattributed graphs, and we show that they perform on par with the state of the art, both in supervised and self-supervised contexts.

</details>


### [54] [It Takes Two: Complementary Self-Distillation for Contextual Integrity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20258)
*Sangwoo Park,Woongyeong Yeo,Seanie Lee,Yumin Choi,Hyomin Lee,Kangsan Kim,Jinheon Baek,Seong Joon Oh,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SELFCI框架，通过自蒸馏方法解耦信息抑制与任务解决，在大语言模型作为个人代理处理敏感工作流时，有效平衡了隐私保护与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型作为个人代理处理敏感工作流的普及，遵循情境完整性理论（Contextual Integrity）对隐私保护至关重要。然而，当前前沿模型在信息披露决策上仍不可靠，且现有缓解策略往往会损害底层任务性能，存在隐私-效用权衡问题。

Method: 提出SELFCI自蒸馏框架，解耦信息抑制与任务解决。该方法联合优化两个独立的逆向KL散度，分别源自反馈得到的两个不同教师分布：一个鼓励保留任务相关信息以维持效用，另一个强制执行最小且适当的信息披露。这种互补形式诱导出专家乘积（Product-of-Experts）目标，使策略与能力和隐私要求的交集对齐。

Result: 实验评估表明，SELFCI在不依赖昂贵外部监督的情况下，持续优于在线强化学习算法（如GRPO）等竞争基线。这些趋势进一步延伸到涉及代理工作流和累积私有上下文的域外设置。

Conclusion: SELFCI为实现情境完整性对齐提供了切实可行的路径，能够在保护隐私的同时维持任务性能，无需昂贵的外部监督，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Contextual Integrity (CI) defines privacy not merely as keeping information hidden, but as governing information flows according to the norms of a given context. As large language models are increasingly deployed as personal agents handling sensitive workflows, adhering to CI becomes critical. However, even frontier models remain unreliable in making disclosure decisions, and existing mitigation strategies often degrade underlying task performance. To overcome this privacy-utility trade-off, we propose SELFCI, a complementary self-distillation framework that decouples information suppression from task resolution. SELFCI jointly optimizes two independent reverse KL divergences over distinct teacher distributions derived from feedback: one encourages preserving task-relevant information for utility, while the other enforces minimal and appropriate disclosure. This complementary formulation induces a Product-of-Experts (PoE) target, aligning the policy with the intersection of capability and privacy requirements. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that SELFCI, without relying on costly external supervision, consistently outperforms competitive baselines such as online reinforcement learning algorithms (e.g., GRPO). These trends further extend to out-of-domain settings involving agentic workflows and accumulated private context, suggesting that SELFCI provides a practical path toward CI alignment.

</details>


### [55] [Residual Paving: Diagnosing the Routing Bottleneck in Selective Refusal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20262)
*Bryce Hinkley,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Residual Paving方法，通过分离路由选择与残差编辑，有效解决了选择性拒绝编辑问题，在Gemma-3-4B-IT上编辑拒绝率从88.6%降至4.0%，同时保持了良性和有害行为分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性拒绝编辑是一个三方面控制问题：需要在指定的编辑提示上诱导非拒绝行为，同时在编辑集之外保持良性行为和有害拒绝。现有方法在编辑成功率与行为保持之间难以平衡，需要一种能够精确控制何时干预以及如何编辑的方法。

Method: 提出Residual Paving方法，这是一种针对冻结指令调优transformer的路由残差编辑方法。方法将路由选择性（是否干预）与残差编辑能力（应用何种编辑）解耦。早期层路由器预测标量门控和专家混合；当激活时，提示条件的瓶颈残差专家在后续层应用残差更新，而保持骨干网络不变。这种分解支持oracle路由诊断，即仅用保留的编辑/保持标签替换学习到的标量门控。

Result: 在Gemma-3-4B-IT保留分割上，Residual Paving将编辑拒绝率从88.6%降至4.0%，良性分布保持率为95.5%，有害分布保持率为87.3%。相比单方向转向控制方法（Edit-target ActAdd为86.8%，DIM风格拒绝转向为78.9%），效果显著更优。跨六个骨干网络的oracle路由在所有报告行上均改善了保持侧诊断分数，中位数增益为+12.9个百分点。轨迹诊断表明存在向编辑目标延续的有向移动，而非通用的拒绝抑制。

Conclusion: 学习到的路由选择性是观察到的主要瓶颈，分离路由选择与残差编辑的设计是有效的。Residual Paving方法成功实现了选择性拒绝编辑，但仍存在非目标有害保持退化问题（有害拒绝率65.3%低于冻结基准81.6%）。

Abstract: We study selective refusal editing as a three-way control problem: induce non-refusal on designated edit prompts while preserving benign behavior and harmful refusals outside the edit set. We introduce Residual Paving, a routed residual editing method for frozen instruction-tuned transformers that separates route selectivity, whether to intervene, from residual-edit capacity, what edit to apply. An early-layer router predicts a scalar gate and expert mixture; when active, prompt-conditioned bottleneck residual experts apply later-layer residual updates while leaving the backbone unchanged. This decomposition supports an oracle-routing diagnostic where only the learned scalar gate is replaced with the held-out edit/keep label, leaving the residual editor and frozen backbone fixed. On the primary Gemma-3-4B-IT held-out split, learned Residual Paving reduces edit refusal from 88.6% to 4.0%, with 95.5% benign distribution preservation and 87.3% harmful distribution preservation. Same-protocol one-direction steering controls are much weaker on edit success, leaving edit refusal at 86.8% for Edit-target ActAdd and 78.9% for DIM-style refusal steering. The remaining failure is off-target harmful-keep degradation: harmful refusal remains below the frozen-base rate, 65.3% vs. 81.6%. Across six backbones, oracle routing improves the keep-side diagnostic score on every reported row, with median gain +12.9 pp, supporting the interpretation that learned route selectivity is the main observed bottleneck. Trajectory diagnostics on two backbones further suggest directed movement toward edit-target continuations rather than generic refusal suppression.

</details>


### [56] [Chronicle: A Multimodal Foundation Model for Joint Language and Time Series Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20268)
*Paul Quinlan,Jeremy Levasseur,Qingguo Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chronicle，首个从头联合预训练文本和时间序列的紧凑多模态模型，在单骨干网络下同时匹配Gemma-3-270M-PT的NLU性能，在时间序列分类上刷新基准，在多模态预测任务上超越所有有监督融合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型仅处理数值序列而忽略文本信息（元数据、描述、新闻、报告等）；现有文本-时间序列多模态模型均采用事后适配预训练语言模型的方式，其表示学习从未接触过时间数据；且评估时仅与多模态基线比较，未与各领域最强的单模态基础模型对比，无法判断联合训练是否必要。

Method: 提出Chronicle，一个324M参数的decoder-only transformer，从头在单一统一架构中联合预训练自然语言和时间序列。两种模态共享相同的transformer块、注意力机制和残差流；预训练主体使用单模态批次，跨模态能力纯粹从共享参数中涌现，辅以短暂的交插对齐阶段。

Result: Chronicle在19个NLU任务上匹配Gemma-3-270M-PT；在24个UCR/UEA数据集上刷新冻结嵌入时间序列分类性能；在Time-MMD多模态预测任务上超越所有有监督融合基线，全部由单一骨干网络实现。

Conclusion: Chronicle证明了从头联合预训练文本和时间序列的可行性与有效性，首次在统一架构下实现了跨模态能力的自然涌现，并在两个模态领域均达到与专用基础模型相当甚至更优的性能，为多模态时间序列建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Real-world time series come with text: metadata, descriptions, news, reports. Yet time series foundation models process numerical sequences in isolation, and the multimodal text-and-time-series models that attempt to bridge the two all adapt a pretrained language model post hoc, inheriting representations shaped without ever seeing temporal data. These models are also evaluated almost exclusively against other multimodal baselines, not against the strongest unimodal foundation models in either domain, leaving open whether joint training is needed at all. We present Chronicle, a compact 324M-parameter decoder-only transformer trained from scratch on natural language and time series within a single unified architecture. Both modalities share the same transformer blocks, attention mechanism, and residual stream; the bulk of pretraining uses unimodal batches so cross-modal capability emerges purely from shared parameters, with a short alignment stage that interleaves the two. To our knowledge, Chronicle is the first model jointly pretrained on text and time series from scratch, and the first multimodal model evaluated against dedicated foundation models in both domains. It matches Gemma-3-270M-PT on 19 NLU tasks, sets a new bar for frozen-embedding time series classification on 24 UCR/UEA datasets, and produces multimodal forecasts on Time-MMD that beat every supervised fusion baseline, all from a single backbone.

</details>


### [57] [Catching a Moving Subspace: Low-Rank Bandits Beyond Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20269)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分段平稳低秩线性上下文bandit问题，提出了SPSC算法，首次在标量反馈下刻画了移动子空间的可辨识边界，并将动态遗憾从环境维度$O(d\sqrt{T})$降至内在秩率$O(r\sqrt{T})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作孤立处理低秩结构或非平稳性：平稳低秩bandit在子空间漂移时失效，非平稳线性bandit虽能适应漂移但需支付环境维度率$\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$。实际应用（推荐系统、临床给药、广告投放）同时具备这两个特性：奖励位于低维潜在子空间且子空间会漂移。

Method: 提出SPSC算法：(1)理论层面，证明在单次播放标量奖励下，当且仅当满足已知噪声方差、有界状态-噪声耦合、全维探测支撑三个条件时，移动子空间通过奖励的二次泛函可恢复；(2)算法层面，SPSC交替执行各向同性探测和在学习的r维子空间内的窗口化投影岭UCB利用，CUSUM变体在线发现分段边界。

Result: (1)识别边界：刻画了问题的可解区域边界，三个条件在无约束二阶矩问题中各自必要且联合充分；(2)动态遗憾：$\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$，用内在秩替代环境维度$d$；(3)实验验证：在合成数据、UCI/MovieLens、半合成临床数据、ZOZOTOWN生产日志等11个基准上，当$d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$时SPSC优于非平稳和低秩基线，与分析交叉点匹配。

Conclusion: 首次在此设定下刻画了识别边界并达到内在秩动态遗憾率，为同时具有低秩结构和子空间漂移的实际bandit部署提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Many bandit deployments (recommendation, clinical dosing, ad targeting) share two facts prior work handles only in isolation: rewards live on a low-dimensional latent subspace, and that subspace drifts. Stationary low-rank bandits exploit rank but break under subspace change; non-stationary linear bandits adapt to drift but pay ambient rate $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$. We study piecewise-stationary low-rank linear contextual bandits with scalar feedback: $θ_t = B_k^\star w_t$ with rank-$r$ factor $B_k^\star\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}$ constant within each of $K$ unknown segments and able to shift at boundaries. Our results are tight along three axes. (i) Identification boundary. With single-play scalar rewards, the moving subspace is recoverable through quadratic functionals of rewards iff three probe-side conditions hold: known noise variance, bounded state-noise coupling, and full-dimensional probe support. Each is necessary in the unrestricted-second-moment problem, and jointly they are sufficient, characterizing the boundary of the solvable region. (ii) Algorithm and dynamic regret. SPSC interleaves isotropic probes with windowed projected ridge-UCB exploitation inside the learned $r$-dimensional subspace; a CUSUM-style variant discovers segment boundaries online. The costed dynamic regret is $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$, replacing the ambient $d\sqrt{T}$ rate with the intrinsic rank. (iii) Empirics. On eleven benchmarks spanning synthetic, UCI/MovieLens, semi-synthetic clinical, and ZOZOTOWN production-log data, SPSC outperforms non-stationary and low-rank baselines whenever $d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$, matching the analytical crossover. To our knowledge, this is the first work to characterize the identification boundary and attain the intrinsic-rank dynamic-regret rate in this setting.

</details>


### [58] [Conformal Selective Acting: Anytime-Valid Risk Control for RLVR-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20270)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CSA（Conformal Selective Acting）方法，这是首个能够在每个部署的每一轮次提供选择性风险安全证书的包装器，解决了现有方法无法在自适应在线更新流上实现路径有效性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管组织中部署本地专业LLM时，需要对每个部署流的每一轮次提供安全证书（误差预算α），而不能跨部署池化或等待长期平均值。现有方法（离线共形风险方法、在线共形方法、非交换性扩展、A-RCPS等）均无法同时满足自适应在线更新、选择性风险控制和路径有效性要求。

Method: 基于（检验统计量、有效性保证、部署规则）框架，CSA作为每轮包装器，在Bonferroni网格上针对每个阈值维护Ville型e-process，并在RLVR过滤下进行评估。该方法在可预测更新和等渗校准单调风险条件下，实现了anytime-pathwise选择性风险界限。

Result: 在8个专业基准（480个流）、16个对抗性分布偏移单元（160个流）和5个实时Expert-Iteration RLVR单元（10,300轮）的实验中，CSA是10种对比方法中唯一在所有单元上满足路径有效性和非拒绝部署的方法。证明了选择性风险界限$R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$、最优认证率匹配$Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$以及与视界无关的释放率差距。

Conclusion: CSA作为部署端补充方案，与模型正交，为无法使用前沿API的操作者提供了实用的安全证书解决方案，填补了安全部署方法的理论空白。

Abstract: A local specialist LLM, fine-tuned with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on operator-local data, is installed in a regulated organization with per-deployment error budget $α$. The operator needs a safety certificate for this deployment's stream at every round: no pooling across deployments, no waiting for a long-run average. Existing wrappers cannot deliver this on adaptive, online-updated streams: offline conformal-risk methods require exchangeability; online-conformal methods bound only long-run averages; non-exchangeable extensions are marginally valid; and the closest anytime wrapper, A-RCPS, controls marginal rather than selective risk. Using a (test statistic, validity guarantee, deployment rule) framework, we identify one empty cell forced by deployment requirements: e-process per threshold, selective risk, anytime-pathwise validity, max-certified-threshold rule. Conformal Selective Acting (CSA) fills it as a per-round wrapper maintaining a Ville-type e-process per threshold on a Bonferroni grid, evaluated against the RLVR filtration. Under predictable updates and isotonic-calibrated monotone risk we prove (i) an anytime-pathwise selective-risk bound $R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$, (ii) rate-optimal certification matching $Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$, and (iii) a horizon-independent release-rate gap. Across eight specialist benchmarks ($480$ streams), sixteen adversarial distribution-shift cells ($160$ streams), and five live Expert-Iteration RLVR cells with online LoRA over four base models in three architecture families ($10{,}300$ rounds), CSA is the only method among ten compared that satisfies pathwise validity and non-refusing deployment on every cell. We do not propose a new LLM, training algorithm, or policy class; CSA is the deployment-side complement, orthogonal to the model, for operators who cannot use a frontier API.

</details>


### [59] [Smaller Abstract State Spaces Enable Cross-Scale Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20272)
*Nasehatul Mustakim,Lucas Lehnert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在强化学习（RL）中实现分布外（OOD）泛化的理论模型。通过扩展部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中的状态抽象框架并引入后继加权模型缩减，本文推导出了OOD测试性能的理论上界。理论表明，限制智能体在一个较小的有限抽象状态空间中运行，对于实现向更复杂任务的泛化至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松地将抽象概念泛化到更复杂或更大规模的任务中，但构建具备同等能力的强化学习系统仍然极具挑战性。现有研究缺乏对强化学习智能体如何实现分布外（OOD）泛化的理论解释，因此亟需建立一个理论模型来阐明在何种条件下这种泛化是可能实现的。

Method: 研究基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）展开。首先，将现有的状态抽象框架及相关证明技术扩展至POMDP场景。其次，提出了一种“后继加权模型缩减”（successor-weighted model reduction）方法，该方法相比以往的定义能够将模型压缩至更小的抽象空间。最后，基于该框架推导了智能体OOD测试性能的上界。

Result: 推导出的性能上界将智能体的性能损失分解为近似误差和估计误差两部分。分析揭示了一个关键机制：缩减智能体的抽象状态空间大小能够有效改善测试性能并促进OOD泛化。结论表明，约束智能体在一个较小的有限抽象状态集上运行，是实现向更复杂任务泛化的必要条件。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计能够跨越不同复杂度任务进行扩展的强化学习架构提供了理论依据。研究指出，未来的工作应致力于探索和学习那些能够在复杂度各异的多个任务间实现有效泛化的RL架构。

Abstract: While humans readily generalize abstract concepts to more complex or larger tasks, building Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems with this ability remains elusive. Here, we present the first theoretical model of how such Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization can be achieved in RL agents. Our approach considers Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) and assumes that an intelligent agent uses an abstraction function to determine which experiences can be treated as equivalent and which must be distinguished. First, we extend the existing state abstraction framework and proof techniques to POMDPs. Then, we define a successor-weighted model reduction, a model reduction variant that enables compression into smaller abstract spaces than prior definitions allow. We derive a bound on the agent's OOD test performance, thereby defining the conditions under which OOD generalization is achievable. This bound decomposes an agent's performance loss into approximation and estimation errors, revealing how reducing an agent's abstract state space size improves test performance and OOD generalization. Our analysis suggests that constraining an agent to operate over a small, finite set of abstract states is necessary for achieving generalization to more complex tasks. Our results motivate further research into learning RL architectures that scale across tasks of varying complexity levels.

</details>


### [60] [Modality-Decoupled Online Recursive Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20273)
*Siyuan Li,Youyuan Zhang,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了M-ORE，一种面向多模态大语言模型的模态解耦在线递归编辑器，通过模态解耦统计和固定正交低秩编辑子空间有效解决了跨模态冲突和编辑间干扰问题，实现了高效的终身模型适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向文本大语言模型的编辑方法在应用于多模态大语言模型时面临两大挑战：一是视觉主导的激活会扭曲更新统计量，导致跨模态冲突；二是序列编辑在共享编辑空间中纠缠，放大长时域干扰，造成编辑间干扰。在线模型编辑需要在严格的计算和内存预算下同化持续的修正流。

Method: 提出M-ORE（模态解耦在线递归编辑器）：1）基于统一近端投影公式推导，采用Sherman-Morrison递归实现闭式更新，保证每编辑恒定开销；2）为文本堆栈和视觉投影器分别维护模块级局部性统计，避免视觉主导的更新塑造；3）在固定正交低秩编辑子空间中通过Sherman-Morrison递归执行持续更新，缓解长时域干扰。

Result: 在多个多模态大语言模型骨干和在线编辑基准上的实验表明，M-ORE方法在可靠性、泛化性和局部性方面始终优于强基线方法，同时实现了良好的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: M-ORE成功实现了多模态大语言模型的终身适应，有效解决了跨模态冲突和编辑间干扰问题，为资源受限场景下的持续模型编辑提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Online model editing for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) requires assimilating a stream of corrections under tight compute and memory budgets. Yet editors developed for text-only LLMs often degrade on MLLMs: visually dominant activations skew the statistics that shape updates, causing cross-modal conflict, while sequential writes become entangled in a shared edit space and amplify long-horizon interference, causing inter-edit interference. To address these, we propose M-ORE, a modality-decoupled online recursive editor for lifelong MLLM adaptation. M-ORE is derived from a unified proximal-projection formulation and admits a closed-form update with a Sherman-Morrison recursion, yielding constant per-edit overhead. It maintains module-wise locality statistics for the text stack and the visual projector to avoid visually dominated update shaping and performs continual updates in a fixed orthogonal low-rank edit subspace via a Sherman-Morrison recursion to mitigate long-horizon interference. Experiments on multiple MLLM backbones and online editing benchmarks show that our M-ORE method consistently improves reliability, generality, and locality over strong baselines, while achieving favorable quality-efficiency scaling. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/lab-klc/M-ORE.

</details>


### [61] [OmniISR: A Unified Framework for Centralized and Federated Learning via Intermediate Supervision and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20276)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Guangxu Zhu,Ming Tang,Chen Zhang,Lisheng Wu,Lei Zhou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniISR统一框架，通过中间监督和正则化信号融合集中式学习(CL)和联邦学习(FL)，解决了异构法律框架下的边缘智能部署问题，理论上证明了收敛性和梯度对齐保证，实验上将CL-FL性能差距缩小22.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球边缘智能部署面临异构法律框架的约束：部分区域允许通过云数据聚合进行集中式学习(CL)，而其他区域实施严格的数据本地化要求，必须采用联邦学习(FL)。这两种优化机制存在根本性不兼容：CL具有无偏全局梯度但伴随内部协变量偏移，FL则产生有偏且易漂移的本地更新，简单集成两者缺乏严格的理论保证。

Method: 提出OmniISR统一框架，在多个隐藏层引入中间监督和正则化(ISR)信号，融合纯CL、纯FL和混合CL-FL训练模式。具体包括：(i)使用互信息(MI)作为中间监督，对齐CL中的内部协变量偏移和FL中的客户端漂移表示；(ii)采用负熵(NE)作为中间正则化器，惩罚过度自信的预测、保持表示不确定性并避免设备特定的坍塌。

Result: 理论方面推导出：(i)统一的、ISR无关的、非渐近O(1/sqrt(T))收敛界，证明ISR不违反标准SGD收敛；(ii)联邦漂移界，量化ISR减少的客户端漂移；(iii)梯度对齐保证，确保在适度偏差下CL和FL更新不冲突；(iv)显式逃逸时间界，表明CL-FL混合增大有效随机性并加速逃离严格鞍点。实验表明OmniISR在集中式和联邦范式中均提升模型性能，将CL-FL差距缩小22.60%，在多种FL算法上获得37/48项成对指标胜利。

Conclusion: OmniISR成功实现了CL和FL的理论统一与实践融合，为异构法律框架下的边缘智能部署提供了可行的解决方案。该框架不仅在理论上保证了收敛性和优化过程的稳定性，还在实验中展现了显著的性能提升，证明了中间监督和正则化策略在弥合不同学习范式差距方面的有效性。

Abstract: The global deployment of edge intelligence operates across heterogeneous legal frameworks. While some regions permit centralized learning (CL) via cloud data aggregation, others enforce strict data localization, necessitating federated learning (FL). This operational dichotomy introduces two incompatible optimization regimes (i.e., unbiased global gradients yet coupled with internal covariate shift in CL versus biased, drift-prone local updates in FL), resulting in that any naive integration of the two lacks rigorous theoretical guarantees. To fill this gap, we propose OmniISR, a unified framework that fuses pure CL, pure FL, and hybrid CL-FL training modes via equipping intermediate supervision and regularization (ISR) signals at multiple hidden layers. Specifically, we propose (i) to use mutual-information (MI) as intermediate supervision to align shifting internal covariate in CL and client-drifting representations in FL, and (ii) to adopt negative-entropy (NE) as intermediate regularizer to penalize overconfident prediction, preserve representational uncertainty, and avoid device-specific collapse. On the theory side, we derive (i) a unified, ISR-agnostic, and non-asymptotic O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence bound that shows the introduced ISR does not violate standard SGD convergence, (ii) a federated drift-bound that quantifies the ISR-reduced client drift, (iii) a gradient-alignment guarantee that ensures non-conflicting CL and FL updates under mild bias, and (iv) an explicit escape-time bound that indicates that CL-FL hybrid mixing enlarges effective stochasticity and accelerates escape from strict saddles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniISR consistently improves model performance in both centralized and federated paradigms, reduces the CL-FL gap by 22.60%, and yields 37/48 paired metric wins across multiple FL algorithms.

</details>


### [62] [ClaimDiff-RL: Fine-Grained Caption Reinforcement Learning through Visual Claim Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20278)
*Tianle Li,Xuyang Shen,Yan Ma,Rongxin Guo,Shaoxiang Chen,Jiacheng Chen,Haochen Wang,Hongyang Tang,Yucong Zhou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClaimDiff-RL框架，通过使用细粒度的原子声明差异作为奖励单元，解决了长篇图像描述强化学习中奖励粒度过粗的问题，实现了幻觉与遗漏事实之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇图像描述的强化学习存在奖励粒度问题：现有方法（成对偏好、参考指标、整体标量奖励）将局部错误（如幻觉或遗漏）压缩为单一序列级信号，掩盖了事实性与覆盖度之间的权衡，无法区分和单独调整幻觉与遗漏显著事实的问题。

Method: 提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，以参考条件化的原子声明差异作为奖励单元。具体流程：给定图像、演员生成的描述和参考描述，多模态评判器枚举视觉基础差异，对照图像验证每个差异，分配开放词汇的错误类型和严重级别，并生成每个差异的统计数据用于奖励组合，使幻觉声明和遗漏显著事实可分别测量和调整。

Result: 在160张人工标注诊断基准、公开图像描述基准和VQA基准上的实验表明：整体标量奖励通过增加遗漏事实来减少幻觉；ClaimDiff-RL暴露了事实性与覆盖度的权衡，实现更平衡的操作点，改进幻觉-遗漏事实平衡，保持通用能力，在物体计数、空间关系和场景识别等细粒度能力维度上甚至超越了Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: 类型化、可验证的声明差异是细粒度、可诊断图像描述强化学习的有效奖励单元，为解决幻觉与遗漏事实的权衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-form image captioning exposes a reward granularity problem in RL: captions are judged as whole sequences, while the important errors occur at the level of individual visual claims. A good dense caption should be both faithful and informative, avoiding hallucination without omitting salient details. Yet pairwise preferences, reference-based metrics, and holistic scalar rewards compress these local errors into a single sequence-level signal, obscuring the tradeoff between factuality and coverage. We introduce ClaimDiff-RL, a framework that uses reference-conditioned atomic claim differences as the reward unit for caption RL. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates visually grounded differences, verifies each difference against the image, assigns open-vocabulary error types and severity levels, and produces per-difference statistics for reward composition. This makes hallucinated claims and omitted salient facts separately measurable and tunable. Experiments show that holistic scalar rewards can reduce hallucination by increasing missing facts, while ClaimDiff-RL exposes this faithfulness and coverage tradeoff and enables more balanced operating points. On a 160-image human-labeled diagnostic benchmark, public captioning benchmarks, and VQA benchmarks, ClaimDiff-RL improves the hallucination--missing-fact balance, preserves general capability, and even surpasses Gemini-3-Pro-Preview on several fine-grained Capability dimensions such as object counting, spatial relations, and scene recognition. These results suggest that typed, verifiable claim differences are an effective reward unit for fine-grained and diagnosable caption RL.

</details>


### [63] [Introspective X Training: Feedback Conditioning Improves Scaling Across all LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20285)
*Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,Jaehun Jung,Syeda Nahida Akter,Hyunwoo Kim,Yuxiao Qu,David Acuna,Shrimai Prabhumoye,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出内省训练方法(IXT)，通过利用后续阶段动态指导早期训练，在7.5-12B LLM上实现最高2.8倍计算效率提升，并在数学和代码领域突破传统训练方法的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练管道包含多个不断增长的阶段，如何在这些阶段间实现更高效的扩展成为一个关键问题。核心直觉在于：训练管道后期阶段（如后训练）的动态信息可以用来指导早期阶段（如预训练），从而实现跨阶段的信息流动与优化。

Method: 提出内省训练（IXT），受离线奖励条件强化学习启发。该方法适用于训练的任何阶段，核心机制包括：(1) 使用思考奖励模型为数据标注基于自然语言批评反馈的质量信息；(2) 从训练管道最早期开始实现质量感知训练；(3) 通过前缀条件化方式将生成反馈与数据结合，确保从训练早期就不平等对待所有token。

Result: 在从头训练的7.5-12B规模Transformer密集LLM上进行全面实验，训练至18万亿token。结果显示：(1) 弯曲缩放曲线，普遍实现最高2.8倍的计算效率提升；(2) 在数学和代码等领域达到传统训练方法无法实现的性能水平。

Conclusion: IXT方法成功实现了跨训练管道阶段的高效信息传递，证明了利用后续训练阶段动态指导早期训练的可行性。该方法不仅在计算效率上取得显著提升，更突破了传统训练范式在特定领域的性能瓶颈，为LLM训练管道的优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We tackle the question of how to scale more efficiently across the many, ever-growing stages of current LLM training pipelines. Our guiding intuition stems from the fact that the dynamics of later stages of the pipeline, e.g. post-training, can be used to inform earlier stages such as pre-training. To this end, we propose Introspective Training (or IXT), inspired by offline reward-conditioned reinforcement learning and applicable to any stage of training. IXT uses a thinking reward model to annotate data with natural language critique based feedback, enabling quality aware training from the earliest stages of the pipeline. Models are then trained by prefix-conditioning the data with the generated feedback -- ensuring that not all tokens are treated equally starting much earlier in training than usual. Comprehensive experiments on 7.5-12B transformer-based dense LLMs trained from scratch all the way up to 18 Trillion tokens seen show that our method: bends scaling curves resulting in up to 2.8x more compute efficiency generally; and reaches performance levels unachievable for models trained otherwise in domains such as math and code.

</details>


### [64] [FusionCell: Cross-Attentive Fusion of Layout Geometry and Netlist Topology for Standard-Cell Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20287)
*Haoyi Zhang,Kairong Guo,Bojie Zhang,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FusionCell，一种融合布局几何与网表拓扑的双模态预测器，在7nm工艺上实现0.92%平均MAPE，大幅加速标准单元特性化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准单元特性化仍依赖慢速仿真扫描，现有快速预测器忽略布局几何，缺失耦合与布局相关效应。核心挑战在于如何联合表征布局几何与网表拓扑，使模型在捕获细粒度空间细节的同时把握结构连接性。

Method: FusionCell采用双模态融合架构：DeiT编码器处理三层路由布局，图变换器建模异构器件/网络图；通过拓扑引导机制实现模态融合——网表作为结构"地图"主动查询布局中相关物理区域，实现联合几何与拓扑推理。基于ASAP7 PDK构建包含149类、超19.5k单元的7nm数据集，针对信号上升/下降延迟、转换及功耗六项指标。

Result: FusionCell实现平均MAPE 0.92%的回归误差，在Spearman/Kendall排序指标上优于基线方法，相比电路仿真将特性化过程加速多个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过显式融合布局几何与网表拓扑，FusionCell有效捕获空间细节与结构连接性，实现了高精度、快速的标准单元性能预测，为芯片设计流程中的特性化环节提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Standard cells form the building blocks of digital circuits, so their delay and power critically influence chip-level performance; yet characterization still relies on slow simulation sweeps, and many fast predictors ignore layout geometry, missing coupling and layout-dependent effects. The challenge is to jointly represent layout geometry and netlist topology so models capture fine-grained spatial details together with structural connectivity for accurate performance prediction. We introduce FusionCell, a dual-modality predictor that treats routed layout geometry and netlist topology as inputs and fuses them explicitly in a unified model. A DeiT encoder processes three-layer routed layouts, while a graph transformer models heterogeneous device/net graphs. The modalities are integrated through a topology-guided mechanism, where the netlist acts as a structural "map" to actively query relevant physical regions in the layout for joint geometric and topological reasoning. We build a 7nm dataset based on the ASAP7 PDK with over 19.5k cells spanning 149 types using automatic tools, targeting six metrics: signal rise/fall delay, transition, and power. Experimental results demonstrate that FusionCell reduces regression error, with an average MAPE of 0.92 percent, and improves Spearman/Kendall ranking over baselines, while accelerating the characterization process by orders of magnitude compared to circuit simulation.

</details>


### [65] [Plug-and-Play Spiking Operators: Breaking the Nonlinearity Bottleneck in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20289)
*Xinzhe Yuan,Xiang Peng,Bin Gu,Huan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个即插即用的框架，通过群体计算和位移缩放实现了Transformer非线性算子的脉冲友好近似，解决了ANN-to-SNN转换中对非线性算子支持有限的问题，在LLM上准确率损失小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ANN-to-SNN转换流程主要关注Transformer线性代数运算的脉冲驱动实现，但对关键的非线性算子支持有限。这些非线性操作（如除法、指数运算、范数计算）难以自然地通过标准的LIF（Leaky Integrate-and-Fire）神经元动力学实现，限制了与神经形态执行约束的兼容性。

Method: 提出了一个即插即用的框架，将Transformer非线性计算分解为三个基本原语：除法、指数运算和L2范数。通过LIF神经元群体实现这些原语的群体计算，并结合轻量级位移缩放避免浮点运算。这些原语作为模块化算子块组合，支持Softmax、SiLU和归一化等常见Transformer非线性操作，无需微调。

Result: 在一系列LLM Transformers上的实验表明，选择性替换目标非线性算子后，所有评估任务的准确率下降均小于1%，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了Transformer非线性算子的脉冲友好近似，能够无缝集成到现有的ANN-to-SNN转换流程中，在不进行微调的情况下保持了高性能，为脉冲大语言模型的实际部署提供了重要支持。

Abstract: ANN-to-SNN conversion offers a practical, training-free route to spiking large language models. However, current pipelines primarily focus on spike-driven realizations for Transformer linear-algebra operations, while providing limited support for key nonlinear operators. This gap limits compatibility with neuromorphic-style execution constraints, where such nonlinearities typically require division, exponentiation, or norm computations that are not naturally supported by standard leaky integrate-and-fire dynamics. To solve this problem, we propose a plug-and-play framework that implements spike-friendly approximations for Transformer nonlinearities and integrates into existing ANN-to-SNN pipelines. Our method decomposes these nonlinear computations into three recurring primitives -- division, exponentiation, and $\ell_2$ norms -- and realizes them via population computation using LIF neuron groups, combined with lightweight bit-shift scaling to avoid floating-point arithmetic. By composing these primitives as modular operator blocks, our framework supports common Transformer nonlinearities (e.g., Softmax, SiLU, and normalization) without any fine-tuning. Experiments on a range of LLMs Transformers show that selectively replacing the targeted nonlinear operators incurs less than a $1\%$ accuracy drop across all evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [66] [Weasel: Out-of-Domain Generalization for Web Agents via Importance-Diversity Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20291)
*Fatemeh Pesaran zadeh,Seyeon Choi,Xing Han Lù,Siva Reddy,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weasel轨迹选择方法，通过平衡重要性和多样性的目标函数选择训练子集，结合目标中心化的AXTree剪枝和风格一致性优化，在提升Web智能体跨域性能的同时实现约9.7-12.5倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特定轨迹和领域微调的Web智能体难以泛化到域外场景；离线训练中存在噪声大、轨迹冗余和AXTree状态过长的问题，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 1) Weasel轨迹选择：通过优化平衡单步重要性与状态、网站、交互模式成对多样性的目标函数，使用贪心算法选择固定预算的轨迹步骤子集；2) 目标中心化AXTree剪枝：仅保留真实动作目标周围的内容；3) 针对推理原生模型，用模型生成的风格一致性推理链替换专家轨迹以缓解风格不匹配问题。

Result: 在AgentTrek和NNetNav数据集、WebArena/WorkArena/MiniWob评测环境、Qwen2.5-7B/Gemma3-4B/Qwen3-8B模型上的实验表明，Weasel提升了域外性能，同时实现约9.7-12.5倍训练加速。

Conclusion: Weasel通过智能轨迹选择和优化策略，成功解决了Web智能体离线训练中的泛化性和计算效率问题，为构建高效、通用的Web智能体提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled web agents that follow natural language goals through multi-step browser interactions. However, agents fine-tuned on specific trajectories and domain often struggle to generalize out of domain, and offline training can be compute-inefficient due to noisy, redundant trajectories and long accessibility-tree (AXTree) states. To address both issues, we propose Weasel, a trajectory selection method for offline training of web agents. Weasel selects a fixed-budget subset of trajectory steps by optimizing an objective that balances unary importance with pairwise diversity over states, websites, and interaction patterns, solving efficiently with a greedy algorithm. We further improve efficiency with target-centered AXTree pruning that keeps only content around the ground-truth action target, and we mitigate style mismatch for reasoning-native models by replacing expert traces with model-generated, style-consistent rationales. Across AgentTrek and NNetNav training datasets, evaluations in WebArena, WorkArena, and MiniWob, and experiments with Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma3-4B, and Qwen3-8B, Weasel improves out-of-domain performance while reducing training cost, producing roughly 9.7-12.5$\times$ training speedups over standard fine-tuning. We make the code available at https://github.com/fatemehpesaran310/weasel.

</details>


### [67] [TreeText-CTS: Compact, Source-Traceable Tree-Path Evidence for Irregular Clinical Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20292)
*Kwanhyung Lee,Juhwan Choi,Jongheon Kim,Joohyung Lee,Hyeongwon Jang,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TreeText-CTS框架，将不规则EHR时序数据转换为可读、紧凑、可溯源的树路径证据单元，在预测性能上超越现有文本接口，同时保持证据的可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值时序模型处理不规则EHR数据有效但缺乏可读证据；现有文本接口存在冗余或难以溯源的问题，需要一种既可读又可追溯的解决方案。

Method: 通过冻结的XGBoost模型路由多尺度窗口摘要，将激活的树路径转换为确定性的阈值条件证据单元；证据选择器筛选信息性子集，由语言模型编码器整合进行预测。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012死亡率、MIMIC-III死亡率和PhysioNet 2019脓毒症预测任务中，AUROC和AUPRC均为最佳，AUPRC相比最强文本接口提升6.0-9.7个百分点，且与数值模型性能相当。

Conclusion: TreeText-CTS成功实现了预测性能与证据可解释性的平衡，所有传递给语言模型的证据均可检查和溯源，为EHR时序预测提供了可信赖的文本接口方案。

Abstract: Numerical time-series models can effectively process irregular electronic health record (EHR) trajectories, but they do not naturally expose the measurements and temporal patterns supporting each risk estimate as readable evidence. Existing text-based interfaces improve readability, but typically rely on either raw serialization, which is lengthy and redundant, or patient-level free-form summaries, which are difficult to trace to source measurements and time windows. To bridge this gap, we introduce TreeText-CTS (Clinical Time-Series), which converts irregular EHR trajectories into human-readable, compact, source-traceable tree-path evidence units without patient-level summarization or inference-time autoregressive decoding. TreeText-CTS routes multi-scale window summaries through frozen XGBoost models and verbalizes activated tree paths as deterministic, source-traceable evidence units composed of threshold conditions. An evidence selector assembles an informative subset of these units, which a language-model encoder then integrates for prediction. Across PhysioNet 2012 mortality, MIMIC-III mortality, and PhysioNet 2019 sepsis-onset forecasting, TreeText-CTS achieves the best AUROC and AUPRC among evaluated text-based EHR time-series interfaces, improving AUPRC by 6.0 to 9.7 absolute percentage points over the strongest prior text-based interface while remaining competitive with numerical time-series models. Ablations show that tree-path evidence construction, evidence selection, and language-model composition each contribute to performance. Because every span passed to the language-model encoder is constructed from activated tree-path threshold conditions, TreeText-CTS makes the evidence supplied to the final predictor inspectable and source-traceable.

</details>


### [68] [Closed-form predictive coding via hierarchical Gaussian filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20293)
*Aleksandrs Baskakovs,Sylvain Estebe,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Kristoffer Nielbo,Chris Mathys,Nicolas Legrand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度层次高斯滤波器(HGF)的预测编码网络，通过恢复精度加权消息传递，解决了传统PC网络在深层网络中性能退化且训练缓慢的问题，在多项任务上优于反向传播算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的预测编码网络将精度矩阵固定为单位矩阵，丢弃了变分推导所需的精度加权预测误差，导致训练速度慢且网络深度增加时性能急剧下降，无法充分发挥其生物学优势和贝叶斯特性。

Method: 将预测编码网络表示为深度层次高斯滤波器(HGF)，恢复精度加权消息传递机制，实现在单一自由能目标下同时学习激活、权重和精度，采用闭式变分推断和在线精度学习，无需迭代松弛或全局误差信号。

Result: 在FashionMNIST数据集上，该方法在epoch级时间成本上接近反向传播，但收敛所需epoch更少；在在线学习、数据效率和概念漂移任务上均优于反向传播，同时提供动态不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 闭式变分推断结合在线精度学习为深度预测编码网络提供了可行基础，保留了生物学可解释性优势，无需迭代松弛或全局误差信号，为构建生物合理的神经网络训练方法开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Predictive coding (PC) offers a local and biologically grounded alternative to backpropagation in the training of artificial neural networks, yet to date, it remains slower, and performance degrades sharply as network depth increases. We trace both problems to a single simplification: current PC networks fix the precision matrix to the identity, discarding precision-weighted prediction errors that the variational derivation requires to be fast, local, and Bayesian. We close this gap by expressing predictive coding networks as deep hierarchical Gaussian filters (HGFs) and restore precision-weighted message passing, yielding dynamic uncertainty estimates and Hebbian-compatible update rules at every layer. The resulting networks can simultaneously learn activations, weights, and precisions under a single free-energy objective, with no global error signal, and resolve inference without requiring iterations or automatic differentiation. On FashionMNIST, our solution approaches backpropagation in epoch-level wall-clock cost while converging in fewer epochs, and outperforms it on online, data efficiency, and concept-drift tasks. We thus establish that closed-form variational inference with online precision learning provides a tractable foundation for deep predictive coding networks, retaining biological and interpretative advantages, without requiring iterative relaxation or global error signals.

</details>


### [69] [Quant.npu: Enabling Efficient Mobile NPU Inference for on-device LLMs via Fully Static Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20295)
*Jinghe Zhang,Daliang Xu,Chenghua Wang,Weikai Xie,Tao Qi,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Quant.npu，一个针对移动端NPU的整数型全静态量化框架，通过可学习参数、旋转矩阵和选择性优化策略，在保持精度的同时将推理延迟降低高达15.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型后训练量化（PTQ）方法主要依赖动态激活量化，这与移动端神经处理单元（NPU）要求的完全静态量化不兼容，无法在NPU硬件约束下实现最优推理效率。

Method: 提出Quant.npu框架，引入可学习量化参数和旋转矩阵实现低比特激活-权重量化；设计旋转与比特宽度感知的初始化策略以及分布感知的选择性优化两阶段流水线，解决优化不稳定问题；提出敏感性引导的自适应混合精度方案以平衡精度与效率。

Result: 在真实移动端NPU上的大量实验表明，Quant.npu在精度上与最先进方法相当，同时推理延迟降低高达15.1%。

Conclusion: Quant.npu成功填补了高保真PTQ与NPU约束推理之间的空白，通过创新的初始化和优化策略实现了稳定训练，为移动端大语言模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile devices, where Neural Processing Units (NPUs) necessitate fully static quantization for optimal inference efficiency. However, existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods predominantly rely on dynamic activation quantization, rendering them incompatible with NPU hardware constraints. To bridge the gap between high-fidelity PTQ and NPU-constrained inference, we propose Quant.npu, a integer-only fully static quantization framework. It incorporates learnable quantization parameters and rotation matrices, enabling low-bit activation-weight quantization without runtime quantization parameters re-computation. Crucially, we identify that initialization and selective optimization of quantization parameters is pivotal for optimization stability, as improper initialization and naive joint optimization induce gradient instability that disrupts the optimization of rotation matrices. To address this, we propose a rotation-and-bit-width-aware initialization tailored to diverse activation profiles and a distribution-aware selective optimization (two-stage quantization pipeline) tailored to rotated and unrotated tensors. Furthermore, we introduce a sensitivity-guided adaptive mixed-precision scheme to balance accuracy with inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on real-world mobile NPUs demonstrate that Quant.npu achieves comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing inference latency by up to 15.1%.

</details>


### [70] [Spectral Unforgetting: Post-Hoc Recovery of Damaged Capabilities Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20296)
*Aarash Abro,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-Hard方法，通过奇异值分解（SVD）对微调后的模型权重更新矩阵进行谱修复，有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题，在恢复受损能力的同时保持目标任务性能，并能恢复因良性微调而退化的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在针对目标任务微调时，常会出现灾难性遗忘现象，导致原本具备的能力下降。现有评估指标无法准确反映修复效果，要么掩盖了特定基准的失败，要么单纯奖励向基础模型回退的行为。因此，需要一种有效的后修复方法来恢复受损能力，同时保持目标任务增益和有益的保留改进。

Method: 提出DG-Hard方法，一种仅依赖检查点的谱修复技术。该方法将微调权重更新$Δ= W_{\mathrm{ft}} - W_{\mathrm{base}}$视为嵌入在类IID噪声残差中的低秩任务对齐信号，对每个权重增量矩阵应用Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值，保留结构化的高能量部分并移除谱主体。该方法将修复简化为一个闭式SVD滤波步骤，无需依赖数据的调参。同时引入分区条件度量，分别追踪恢复、保持、未损坏和目标任务保留四项指标。

Result: 在14个（模型，任务）设置和9个跨领域保留基准测试中，DG-Hard在后修复基线中实现了最强的平衡修复效果。此外，尽管未使用对齐数据，DG-Hard仍在三个独立安全轴上恢复了因良性微调而退化的安全对齐。

Conclusion: 微调导致的能力损失并非专业化的必然结果，而是权重更新本身中可移除的谱残差。通过谱修复可以有效缓解灾难性遗忘，恢复受损能力和安全对齐。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model for a target task routinely degrades capabilities the training data never explicitly threatened. We study this phenomenon, known as catastrophic forgetting, and propose a post-hoc repair solution that uses only the pretrained checkpoint $W_{\mathrm{base}}$ and its fine-tuned descendant $W_{\mathrm{ft}}$. The goal is not merely to revert the model toward the base checkpoint, but to recover capabilities damaged by fine-tuning while preserving both the target-task gains and any beneficial held-out improvements. We introduce DG-Hard, a checkpoint-only spectral repair method for the fine-tuning update $Δ= W_{\mathrm{ft}} - W_{\mathrm{base}}$. DG-Hard treats $Δ$ as a low-rank task-aligned signal embedded in an IID-like noise residual that gradient descent has no incentive to remove, and applies the Donoho-Gavish hard singular-value threshold to each weight-delta matrix, keeping the structured high-energy part of the update and removing the spectral bulk. This reduces repair to a closed-form SVD filtering step requiring no data-dependent tuning. A central difficulty is evaluation: average accuracy hides per-benchmark failures, while naive recovery scores reward models that simply revert toward the base. We therefore introduce a partition-conditional metric that separately tracks healing, preservation, non-damage, and target-task retention. Across $14$ (model, task) settings and nine cross-domain held-out benchmarks, DG-Hard achieves the strongest balanced repair among post-hoc baselines. DG-Hard also restores safety alignment degraded by benign fine-tuning on three independent safety axes, despite using no alignment data. These results suggest that part of fine-tuning-induced capability loss is not an unavoidable consequence of specialization, but a removable spectral residue in the weight update itself.

</details>


### [71] [Mechanisms of Misgeneralization in Physical Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20299)
*Kento Nishi,Raphael Tang,Karun Kumar,Core Francisco Park,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现深度学习模型在物理域序列规划中存在物理误泛化问题：即使单条轨迹合理，整体物理量分布仍会偏离预期。论文揭示了误差传播机制，提出数据偏差核进行预测，并设计了相应的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练物理域（如机器人、机械仿真）生成式序列模型时，工程师通常通过精心策划示范数据来约束轨迹在特定物理量（如行程距离、机械能）上的分布。然而研究发现，标准深度学习方法会违背这一意图——虽然每条生成的轨迹单独看都合理，但聚合后的物理量分布却是错误的。

Method: 通过受控合成任务进行实验研究，分析物理误泛化的产生机制。提出数据偏差核来估计模型类的局部误差，并利用该核预测物理量分布中哪些部分会增加或减少质量。在合成任务、迷宫导航和双摆运动任务上进行验证，并基于机制解释提出核信息干预策略。

Result: 揭示了物理误泛化的机制：模型类典型的局部误差通过物理测量传播，导致恢复的分布发生偏移。数据偏差核能够有效预测物理量分布的变化方向，成功应用于合成任务、迷宫导航和双摆运动等多种场景。基于机制解释识别出结构上有前景的缓解策略。

Conclusion: 物理误泛化是深度学习在物理域序列建模中的一个系统性问题，根源在于局部误差的传播机制。通过数据偏差核可以准确预测和解释这一现象，为设计针对性的缓解策略提供了理论基础，核信息干预是一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative sequence models are often trained to plan motion in physical domains, from robotics to mechanical simulations. When constructing a dataset to train such a model, engineers may curate demonstrations to specify how trajectories should be distributed over a physical quantity like travel distance or mechanical energy. For example, a roboticist building a maze navigation agent might choose demonstrations whose travel distances cover a fixed range uniformly, hoping to constrain the agent's expected power usage. We find that standard deep learning can violate this intent: each generated trajectory can seem plausible on its own, but the aggregate distribution over the physical quantity is wrong. We call this failure physical misgeneralization, and develop an account of its mechanism. Using controlled synthetic tasks, we show that physical misgeneralization arises when local errors typical of the model class propagate through the physical measurement to shift the recovered distribution.
