We instantiate this idea via Vector Quantization (VQ), which induces a structured partition of the representation space, and an indexed parameterization of Dirichlet concentrations that enables parameter sharing across regions. Our approach learns heterogeneous calibration maps that generalize well even to sparse regions of the latent space. Experiments on benchmark datasets show significant improvements in local calibration while maintaining competitive global calibration and predictive performance.

</details>


### [144] [ShapeBench: A Scalable Benchmark and Diagnostic Suite for Standardized Evaluation in Aerodynamic Shape Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20763)
*Shaghayegh Fazliani,Krissh Chawla,Jack Guo,Yiren Shen,Matthias Ihme,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ShapeBench，一个涵盖103个任务和8种形状类别的气动外形优化开源基准测试平台，揭示了优化器排名在不同任务间存在巨大差异（Spearman ρ=0.013），表明单一任务结论难以泛化，且基准远未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动外形优化（ASO）领域的快速发展缺乏标准化的评估框架，导致公平比较困难。现有的研究难以在不同形状类别、目标 formulations 和预算条件下对最先进方法进行统一评价，亟需一个全面、可复现的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建了ShapeBench开源基准测试平台，包含统一API接口，涵盖8种形状类别、103个任务及多种优化机制。每个任务配备经过验证的代理模型以加速搜索，并在可行时提供高保真CFD管道进行最终验证。建立了可复现的协议和配置良好的基线（包括经典方法和LLM驱动方法），提出了一种新的领域专用进化LLM基线ShapeEvolve，并采用一致的预算指标进行公平比较。

Result: 实验结果显示优化器排名在不同形状类别和问题 formulations 间存在显著差异，平均成对Spearman相关系数仅为0.013，说明单任务结论无法可靠泛化到其他问题类别。基准测试远未达到饱和状态，经典方法很少能适用于所有形状类别和任务，凸显了对更通用优化方法的需求。

Conclusion: ShapeBench为气动外形优化领域提供了首个大规模、标准化的基准测试平台，填补了评估框架的空白。研究结果揭示了当前优化方法的局限性，强调了开发更通用优化器的必要性，为该领域的未来发展指明了方向。该平台将促进经典方法与LLM驱动方法的公平比较与协同发展。

Abstract: Rapid progress in aerodynamic shape optimization (ASO) has outpaced currently-available standardized evaluation frameworks. Fair comparison requires a unified benchmark spanning diverse shape classes, objective formulations, and matched-budget state-of-the-art baselines. We introduce ShapeBench, an open-source ASO benchmark with a unified API spanning 103 tasks across eight shape categories and multiple optimization regimes. Each ShapeBench task includes a validated surrogate for fast search; when feasible, a high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) pipeline for final verification is available, enabling systematic fidelity-gap analysis. ShapeBench provides a reproducible protocol with well-configured baselines to compare fairly using a consistent budget metric, allowing for comparison among both classical and LLM-driven methods, including general-purpose optimizers and a new domain-specialized evolutionary LLM baseline, ShapeEvolve. Results on ShapeBench demonstrate substantial variance in optimizer rankings across shape categories and problem formulations, with mean pairwise Spearman $ρ= 0.013$, so single-task conclusions do not reliably generalize across problem classes. The benchmark is also far from saturation; classical methods are rarely applicable across all shape categories and tasks, further highlighting the need for more general-purpose approaches.

</details>


### [145] [Cumulative Meta-Learning from Active Learning Queries for Robustness to Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20771)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAML框架，通过元学习利用查询样本学习先验（归纳偏置），有效缓解深度主动学习中的虚假相关性问题，在多个基准测试上显著提升了少数群体的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集中的虚假相关性会导致模型依赖无关模式，降低可靠性、泛化能力和公平性。虽然主动学习可以通过查询有信息量的样本来解决此问题，但在深度学习场景下，标准方法仅将查询样本加入标注集（只更新似然项），导致这些样本的影响被大量已标注数据稀释，且易被过参数化模型记忆。因此需要一种机制，使查询样本能更有效地引导模型区分核心特征与虚假特征。

Method: 提出累积主动元学习（CAML）框架，将每轮主动学习转化为元学习任务：当前标注集作为元训练数据用于适应，新查询批次作为元测试数据评估泛化能力。CAML维护一个累积的归纳偏置（先验），在各轮次中逐步精炼，利用轮次间的序列依赖性。理论上证明了该累积形式引入了交互项，耦合了早期元学习的归纳偏置与后续查询目标，捕捉了标准元学习缺失的依赖关系。

Result: 在多个虚假相关性基准测试中，CAML显著提升了少数群体的准确率：Dominoes提升27.8%，Waterbirds提升29.9%，SpuCo提升14.3%，CivilComments提升24.0%，且在不同采集策略下均有改善。

Conclusion: CAML通过将主动学习与元学习结合，利用查询样本元学习先验而非简单扩充标注集，有效解决了深度学习中虚假相关性问题，为提升模型的泛化能力和公平性提供了新的思路和理论支撑。

Abstract: Spurious correlations in real-world datasets cause machine learning models to rely on irrelevant patterns, undermining reliability, generalization, and fairness. Active learning offers a promising way to address this failure mode by querying informative samples that distinguish core features from spurious ones. However, standard active-learning methods simply append queried examples to the labeled set, effectively updating only the likelihood term. In deep learning regimes, the influence of these informative samples can be diluted by the larger labeled set and memorized by overparameterized models. We propose Cumulative Active Meta-Learning (CAML), an active-learning framework that uses queried examples to meta-learn the prior, or inductive bias, governing how the model adapts. CAML casts each active-learning round as a meta-learning task: the current labeled set serves as meta-train data for adaptation, while the newly queried batch serves as meta-test data for evaluating generalization. Unlike conventional meta-learning, which treats tasks as independent and identically distributed, CAML exploits the sequential dependence between active-learning rounds by maintaining a cumulative inductive bias that is progressively refined. Theoretically, we show that this cumulative formulation introduces interaction terms that couple earlier meta-learned inductive biases with later query-induced objectives, capturing dependencies absent from standard meta-learning. Empirically, CAML improves minority-group accuracy across spurious-correlation benchmarks and acquisition strategies, with gains of up to 27.8% on Dominoes, 29.9% on Waterbirds, 14.3% on SpuCo, and 24.0% on CivilComments.

</details>


### [146] [Efficient Learning of Deep State Space Models via Importance Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21108)
*John-Joseph Brady,Nikolas Nusken,Yunpeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并行变分蒙特卡洛(PVMC)方法，统一了深度状态空间模型(DSSM)的两种训练范式，在基线实验上达到最优结果且训练速度比最快的SMC方法快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在状态空间系统在统计建模中普遍存在，但深度状态空间模型(DSSM)的大规模训练仍然困难。现有两种训练策略相互独立：变分自编码方法仅适用于生成任务，而基于序贯蒙特卡洛(SMC)的方法虽然支持判别和生成任务，但由于前向传播的顺序性，在现代硬件上扩展性差。

Method: 提出并行变分蒙特卡洛(PVMC)训练方法，通过桥接自编码变分方法和SMC反向传播方法之间的差距，实现对DSSM的并行训练，使其能够同时支持判别任务和生成任务的鲁棒训练。

Result: 在基线实验集上达到或超越当前最优结果，训练速度比最快的竞争SMC方法快10倍。

Conclusion: PVMC方法成功统一了DSSM的两种训练范式，解决了SMC方法的可扩展性问题，同时保持了在判别和生成任务上的优异性能，为大规模训练DSSM提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Latent state space systems are ubiquitous in statistical modelling, arising naturally when a time series is observed through a noisy measurement function, however training deep state space models (DSSM) at scale remains difficult. Two largely distinct strategies and literatures have developed around the training of DSSMs. Firstly, auto-encoding DSSMs train generative DSSMs by optimising a variational lower bound. Secondly, DSSMs trained by back-propagating the outputs of a classical sequential Monte Carlo algorithm (SMC). Such approaches can train DSSMs for discriminative as well as generative tasks, however, due to the sequentiality of their forward pass, scale poorly on modern hardware. We propose a new training method \emph{parallel variational Monte Carlo} (PVMC) that bridges the gap between the paradigms, and can be used robustly to train DSSMs for both discriminative and generative tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art or better results on a set of baseline experiments and trains $10\times$ faster than the fastest competing SMC approach.

</details>


### [147] [Learning to Think in Physics: Breaking Shortcut Learning in Scientific Diffusion via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20780)
*Haozhe Jia,Pengyu Yin,Wenshuo Chen,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Bowen Tian,Xiucheng Wang,Nanqian Jia,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了REPA-P框架，通过对扩散模型中间层特征进行物理约束，显著提升了物理信息生成的收敛速度、精度和泛化能力，且推理时零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息扩散模型通常仅在最终输出端施加PDE约束，导致中间层表示不受约束。在边界条件发生偏移时，模型容易陷入捷径学习，影响泛化性能和物理一致性。

Method: 提出了REPA-P框架，这是一种免教师、架构无关的方法。它在选定的网络层附加轻量级1×1投影头，将隐藏激活解码为物理量，并在训练过程中施加PDE残差损失。这些投影头在推理阶段被丢弃，从而实现零推理开销。该方法兼容U-Net和Diffusion Transformer等主流骨干网络。

Result: 在四个PDE任务（达西流、拓扑优化、静电势、湍流通道流）上，REPA-P将收敛速度提升至多2倍，物理残差降低至多66.4%，分布外鲁棒性提升至多49.3%。消融实验表明，监督少量中间层即可获得主要收益，并与输出层物理损失形成互补。

Conclusion: REPA-P通过简单有效的中间层监督机制，成功解决了物理信息扩散模型的捷径学习问题，在不增加推理成本的前提下，显著提升了模型的收敛效率、物理精度和泛化鲁棒性，具有良好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Physics-informed diffusion models typically enforce PDE constraints only on final outputs, leaving intermediate representations unconstrained and prone to shortcut learning under shifted boundary conditions. We introduce **REPA-P**, a teacher-free, architecture-agnostic framework that aligns intermediate features with physical states using first-principles residuals. REPA-P attaches lightweight $1{\times}1$ projection heads to selected layers, decodes hidden activations into physical quantities, and applies PDE residual losses during training. These heads are discarded at inference, introducing **zero overhead**. Across four PDE tasks, including Darcy flow, topology optimization, electrostatic potential, and turbulent channel flow, REPA-P accelerates convergence by up to $2{\times}$, reduces physics residuals by up to $66.4\%$, and improves out-of-distribution robustness by up to $49.3\%$, with consistent gains on both U-Net and Diffusion Transformer backbones. Ablations show that supervising a small set of intermediate layers captures most benefits and complements output-level physics losses. Code is available at [https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P](https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P).

</details>


### [148] [Behavior-Consistent Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21214)
*Marcel Hussing,Liv G. d'Aliberti,Claas Voelcker,Benjamin Eysenbach,Eric Eaton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QED方法，通过Q值分歧自适应调整温度参数，在保持性能的同时将强化学习跨运行策略差异降低两个数量级，显著减少回报方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在不同训练运行间表现出高方差，导致性能不可靠，阻碍了其在现实场景中的部署。跨运行策略发散问题亟需解决，以获得既高性能又在分布上相似的策略。

Method: 1. 形式化行为一致性强化学习问题，目标为获得高性能且跨运行分布相似的策略
2. 证明最大熵RL通过锚定到共同先验来控制行为差异，对于Boltzmann策略，温度与Q函数分歧成正比时可约束策略间KL散度
3. 指出简单增加熵会损害策略优化并放大离策略误差
4. 提出Q值期望分歧(QED)：状态依赖的温度调度，使用双评论家分歧作为跨运行分歧的单运行代理

Result: 在18个连续控制任务上的实验表明，QED将跨运行分歧降低两个数量级，同时不牺牲性能，在适度样本效率成本下显著减少回报方差。

Conclusion: QED为强化学习的可重复性和可靠性提供了有效解决方案，通过理论指导的温度调度实现了性能与一致性之间的良好平衡，推动了RL在现实应用中的部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) often exhibits high variance across training runs, leading to unreliable performance and posing a major challenge to deployment in real-world domains. In this work, we address the challenge of cross-run policy divergence by formalizing the problem of behavior-consistent RL, where the objective is to obtain policies that are both high-performing and distributionally similar across training runs. Our key observation is that maximum-entropy RL provides a direct mechanism for controlling behavioral divergence by anchoring runs to a common (uniform) prior. We prove that, for Boltzmann policies, choosing the temperature proportional to $Q$-function disagreement bounds the pairwise KL divergence between the induced policies. However, we also show that naïvely increasing entropy might impair policy optimization while amplifying off-policy error. Building upon these observations, we propose $Q$-value Expectile Disagreement (QED), a state-dependent temperature schedule that uses double-critic disagreement as a single-run proxy for cross-run disagreement. Empirically, we demonstrate that across 18 continuous-control tasks, QED reduces across-run divergence by two orders of magnitude without sacrificing performance, resulting in a considerable reduction in return variance at modest sample-efficiency costs.

</details>


### [149] [Causal Machine Learning Is Not a Panacea: A Roadmap for Observational Causal Inference in Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20782)
*Donna Tjandra,Trenton Chang,Sonali Parbhoo,Rajesh Ranganath,Andre Kurepa Waschka,William Mitchell,Maggie Makar,Shalmali Joshi,Finale Doshi-Velez,Leo Anthony Celi,Jenna Wiens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为临床专家和机器学习从业者提供了因果机器学习在观察性数据中应用的路线图，强调验证因果假设和规范建模选择对于避免偏倚结果的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模观察性临床数据集的日益可用以及随机对照试验开展的挑战，研究者对利用因果机器学习进行因果推断的热情高涨，但该方法的局限性在跨学科领域中被严重低估，可能影响研究结果的有效性。

Method: 作者提出了一个应用路线图，强调需在现有数据中评估有效性假设，并指导临床专家和缺乏临床专业知识的机器学习从业者负责任地应用因果机器学习方法。

Result: 指出了因果机器学习局限性被低估的知识鸿沟，并识别出若因果假设不满足或建模选择缺乏论证，将产生偏倚或误导性结果的风险。

Conclusion: 因果机器学习是生成因果假设的有力工具，本文提供了加强因果分析严谨性和可解释性的模板。

Abstract: Objective: The growing availability of large-scale observational clinical datasets and challenges in conducting randomized controlled trials have spurred enthusiasm in using causal machine learning (ML) for causal inference in observational data. We present a roadmap for applying causal ML to observational data. Materials and methods: We outline the importance of assessing validity assumptions within available data and applying causal ML responsibly for clinical experts using causal ML and ML practitioners with limited clinical expertise. Observations: Despite advances in causal ML, its limitations remain largely under-appreciated across disciplines. This gap in shared knowledge may impact the validity of findings. Discussion: Causal assumptions must be satisfied and modeling choices justified. Otherwise, these approaches risk producing biased or misleading results, with consequences for clinical research and patient care. Conclusion: Causal ML can be a powerful tool for generating causal hypotheses. We provide a template to strengthen the rigor and interpretability of causal analyses.

</details>


### [150] [PREFINE: Preference-Based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21225)
*Richa Verma,Bavish Kulur,Sanjay Chawla,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PREFINE方法，通过偏好数据对预训练强化学习策略进行安全对齐微调，无需从头训练，在保持高回报的同时降低约束违规率超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练强化学习策略缺乏安全意识，而传统安全强化学习需要从头训练成本高昂。本文旨在解决如何在不重新训练的前提下，利用偏好反馈将预训练策略适配到安全约束环境中，实现高效的安全对齐。

Method: 提出PREFINE方法，将大语言模型中广泛使用的直接偏好优化(DPO)适配到序列决策场景。该方法构建策略采样的反事实轨迹以建立有意义的偏好对比，联合优化回报保持和安全对齐两个目标，利用包含偏好(低代价)和非偏好(高代价)轨迹的小数据集进行微调。

Result: 实验表明PREFINE将约束违规和灾难性失败降低超过60%，同时保持原始回报行为。与传统离线强化学习或模仿学习相比，该方法在数据和计算效率方面显著提升，成功生成了低代价、高回报的策略。

Conclusion: PREFINE成功将偏好对齐与安全策略适配在连续控制领域进行桥接，为预训练强化学习策略的安全微调提供了一种高效可行的解决方案，在保持性能的同时大幅提升了安全性。

Abstract: We address the problem of making a pre-trained reinforcement learning (RL) policy safety-aware by incorporating cost constraints without retraining it from scratch. While costs could be numerically encoded, we assume a more general setting is when costs are provided as preferences. Given a reward-optimized policy and a small dataset of preferred (low-cost) and dispreferred (high-cost) trajectories, our goal is to fine-tune the policy to generate low-cost behaviors while retaining high rewards. Unlike standard RLHF in language models, where preferences are defined over responses to the same prompt, our setting involves trajectory-level preferences in continuous control environments. We introduce PREFINE: Preference-based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment which is a preference-based fine-tuning method that adapts Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), which is now widely used for LLM fine-tuning, to the sequential decision making setting. PREFINE constructs policy-sampled counterfactual trajectories to establish meaningful preference contrasts and jointly optimizes for reward retention and safety alignment. Empirically, PREFINE reduces constraint violations and catastrophic failures by over 60% while maintaining original reward behavior. PREFINE produces policies that achieve low-cost, high-reward performance with significantly improved data and computational efficiency compared to full offline RL or imitation learning, bridging preference alignment and safe policy adaptation in continuous domains.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Numerical Features: CNN-Driven Algorithm Selection via Contour Plots for Continuous Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20797)
*Yiliang Yuan,Xiang Shi,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等高线图可视化图像的算法选择方法，利用CNN预测求解器性能，在黑箱优化中表现优异且无需人工设计特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续优化算法选择方法主要依赖数值描述符（如ELA特征或Deep-ELA嵌入）。本文旨在探索一种互补的表示方法——利用探测景观的等高线图可视化，以验证视觉模型能否直接利用景观的空间结构进行算法选择，从而避免手工特征工程的复杂性。

Method: 该方法通过生成优化问题的探测景观等高线图作为输入表示，使用卷积神经网络（CNN）回归器预测固定求解器组合中每个求解器的性能。网络接收多个实例特定的等高线视图（通过堆叠或逐视图编码后聚合），最终选择预测性能最优的求解器。

Result: 在标准的BBOB 2009单目标测试协议上，该选择器显著优于单一最优求解器（SBS），并与基于特征的基线方法具有竞争力。在DeepELA设置下的双目标评估中，使用窗口化等高线视图的图像方法同样表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，简单的视觉模型能够有效利用探测景观中的空间结构进行算法选择，无需依赖人工设计的ELA特征，为基于图像表示的算法选择提供了可行的新方向。

Abstract: The present paper introduces a new representation-driven approach to per-instance algorithm selection, applied to black-box optimization, for automatically choosing the most promising solver from a fixed portfolio. Prior work in continuous optimization largely relies on numerical descriptors, including Exploratory Landscape Analysis features and learned embeddings such as Deep-ELA. This work studies a complementary representation: contour-map visualizations of probed landscapes. A CNN regressor takes multiple instance-specific contour views (stacked or encoded per view and aggregated) and predicts per-solver performance, enabling selection by the predicted best value. On the standard BBOB 2009 single-objective protocol, the resulting selectors significantly outperform the single best solver (SBS) and are competitive with feature-based baselines. A subsequent bi-objective evaluation under the DeepELA setting further indicates that the same image-based principle can be competitive when using windowed contour views. Overall, the results suggest that simple vision models can exploit spatial structure in probed landscapes for algorithm selection without handcrafted ELA features.

</details>


### [152] [OCTOPUS: Optimized KV Cache for Transformers via Octahedral Parametrization Under optimal Squared error quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21226)
*Mark Boss,Vikram Voleti,Simon Donné,Shimon Vainer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OCTOPUS提出了一种基于旋转预条件的KV缓存压缩新方法，通过对旋转坐标三元组进行联合量化，在文本、视频和音频任务上均超越现有方法，且不增加解码延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文自回归推理中，KV缓存主导了内存带宽和占用。现有的旋转预条件编解码器（如TurboQuant、PolarQuant）已展现出良好效果，但仍有优化空间。本文旨在进一步提升KV压缩性能。

Method: OCTOPUS对旋转坐标三元组进行联合量化：采用八面体参数化将每个三元组的方向映射到正方形，对生成的两个坐标和三元组范数使用Lloyd-Max量化。通过优化每三元组平方误差，实现了严格非均匀的比特分配。该方法数据无关、在线、确定性，并实现了融合Triton实现以避免物化解压缩的键。

Result: 在文本、视频和音频多种模态上，OCTOPUS在所有报告的位宽和指标上均匹配或超越所有先前的旋转编解码器。在极低比特压缩场景下优势更加明显。融合Triton实现在不物化解压缩键的情况下即时重建键，不增加解码时带宽或延迟。

Conclusion: OCTOPUS通过三元组联合量化成功推进了旋转预条件编解码器的性能边界，在保持零额外开销的同时实现了更优的压缩效果，特别适合极端压缩场景。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory bandwidth and footprint in long-context autoregressive inference. Recent rotation-preconditioned codecs (TurboQuant, PolarQuant) show that a structured random rotation followed by a per-coordinate scalar quantizer matched to an analytically tractable marginal is a near-optimal recipe for KV compression. OCTOPUS advances this paradigm through joint quantization of rotated coordinate triplets. Each triplet's direction is mapped to a square via an octahedral parameterization, and the two resulting coordinates and the triplet norm are Lloyd-Max quantized against implementation-matched marginals. Optimizing the per-triplet squared error gives a strictly non-uniform bit allocation depending only on the total dimensionality of the keys. We find the finite-dimensional quality optimum with sweeps to be constant on every real decoder we test. The codec is data-oblivious, online, and deterministic given a seed. Across text, video, and audio, OCTOPUS matches or beats every prior rotation codec at every reported bit width and metric, with a lead that grows as bits drop for extreme compression. Furthermore, a fused Triton implementation reconstructs keys on the fly without materializing the uncompressed key, so the codec adds no decode-time bandwidth or latency over the existing dequantization. Project Page: https://octopus-quant.github.io/

</details>


### [153] [Most Transformer Modifications Still Do Not Transfer at 1-3B: A 2020-2026 Update to Narang et al. (2021) with Downstream Evaluation and a Noise Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20798)
*Yang Zhao,Jiahao Lu,Bin Huang,Guhua Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在大规模（1-3B参数）下复现了“大多数Transformer修改无效”的结论：20个修改中仅2个显著有效，其中一个在更大规模下训练不稳定；下游任务评估揭示了预训练损失无法反映的显著性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Narang等人2021年的研究结论需要更新：当前主流模型参数规模已从T5-base扩展至1-3B，评估方式从预训练困惑度转向下游任务性能，且涌现出一批新的Transformer修改方案。作者旨在严格控制的条件下重新审视“大多数修改是否有效”这一核心问题。

Method: 选取20个2021年后提出的Transformer修改，在1.2B和3B参数规模下进行测试。严格控制变量：iso-data（等数据）、iso-compute（等计算量）、iso-recipe（等训练配方）。设立多种子的基线噪声基准，并以CLIMB-12下游任务评估作为主要度量指标，同时进行Bonferroni校正以确保统计显著性。

Result: 核心发现复现了Narang等人的结论：大多数修改在大规模下无法有效迁移。20个修改中仅2个在1.2B规模下通过Bonferroni校正，其中1个在3B规模下训练不稳定。此外，注意力输出类修改存在严重的“损失-下游性能差距”：即使验证损失仅比基线差2-3%，下游任务性能却下降6-16个CLIMB分。

Conclusion: 当前1-3B参数规模的架构比较研究必须包含三个前提条件：噪声基准报告、下游任务评估以及跨规模稳定性测试。仅依赖预训练损失会掩盖修改在下游任务上的真实性能缺陷。

Abstract: Narang et al. (2021) evaluated 40+ Transformer modifications at T5-base scale and concluded that most did not transfer. Five years later, the typical working regime has moved to 1-3B parameters, downstream evaluation has replaced pretraining perplexity, and a substantially different catalogue of modifications has emerged. We revisit their question by testing 20 post-2021 Transformer modifications at 1.2B and 3B under strict iso-data, iso-compute, iso-recipe control, with a multi-seed baseline noise floor and CLIMB-12 downstream evaluation as the primary metric. The central finding reproduces theirs at this curated set: most modifications do not transfer. Of the 20 modifications, only two clear Bonferroni correction at 1.2B; one of those two further fails to train stably at 3B under the shared recipe. We also find that the loss-downstream gap reported by Tay et al. (2023) enlarges several-fold for attention-output modifications: two significant failures converge to within 2-3% of baseline validation loss yet drop 6-16 CLIMB-points. We conclude that noise-floor reporting, downstream evaluation, and cross-scale stability testing are now prerequisites for architecture comparisons at 1-3B.

</details>


### [154] [APEX: Autonomous Policy Exploration for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21240)
*Yibo Li,Jiashuo Yang,Zhi Zheng,Zhiyuan Hu,Yuan Sui,Shizun Wang,Yufei He,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了APEX框架，通过构建策略地图（有向无环图）来解决自演化智能体中的探索崩溃问题，在文本冒险游戏和WebArena基准上取得了优于所有基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体虽然能在测试时通过累积记忆和反思进行学习，但会遭遇探索崩溃问题：随着记忆增长，行为会集中在熟悉的高回报例程上，降低发现更优替代方案的机会。

Method: 提出了自主策略探索（APEX）框架，通过策略地图（带有先决条件依赖边的里程碑有向无环图）显式构建和维护策略空间。包含两个核心组件：Fork Discovery通过证据支撑的未探索方向扩展地图，Policy Selection在规划时平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在九个Jericho文本冒险游戏和WebArena现实网页交互基准上进行评估，APEX超越了所有基线方法。广泛的消融实验验证了各组件的贡献，并证明了在不同设置下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: APEX有效解决了自演化智能体的持续探索问题，通过显式维护策略空间避免了探索崩溃，为需要长视野决策的交互环境中的智能体学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents have shown strong performance across a wide range of complex tasks, including interactive environments that require long-horizon decision making. But these agents cannot learn on the fly at test time. Self-evolving agents address this by accumulating memory and reflection across episodes rather than requiring model-weight updates. However, these agents often suffer from exploration collapse: as memory grows, behavior concentrates around familiar high-reward routines, reducing the chance of discovering better alternatives. To address this problem, we propose Autonomous Policy EXploration (APEX), which builds and maintains an explicit strategy space through a strategy map-a directed acyclic graph of milestones with prerequisite dependency edges. In APEX, Fork Discovery expands the map with evidence-grounded unexplored directions, while Policy Selection balances exploration and exploitation during planning. Evaluated on nine Jericho text-adventure games and WebArena, a realistic web interaction benchmark, APEX outperforms all baselines. Extensive ablations validate each component's contribution and demonstrate robustness across diverse settings, demonstrating APEX's effectiveness for sustained exploration in self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [155] [How Much Online RL is Enough? Informative Rollouts for Offline Preference Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21266)
*Richa Verma,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G2D方法通过短暂GRPO预热+离线DPO训练，在大幅降低计算成本的同时性能优于纯GRPO。在Qwen2.5-7B上，G2D在MATH-500达到62.4%（比GRPO高10.8%，计算成本降低约4倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO作为RLVR的主要方法需要持续在线rollout生成，计算开销大且难以扩展；而DPO作为离线替代方案，在冷启动SFT策略上性能通常不及在线RL方法。研究者希望找到兼具两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出G2D（GRPO to DPO）三阶段流程：(1)短时GRPO预热；(2)构建静态偏好数据集；(3)使用DPO离线微调模型。核心思想是通过适度的在线预热生成信息量更高的偏好数据。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上，K=150时G2D在MATH-500达到62.4%，超越GRPO（51.6%）10.8%，计算成本降低约4倍。在Llama-3.1-8B上，K=500时G2D达到49.4%，同样超越GRPO。性能提升不依赖于偏好对数量，而取决于数据的信息性——适度预热产生校准的不确定性，提供更强的对比信号。

Conclusion: RLVR中的离线-在线差距主要是数据信息性问题。适度在线RL预热配合适当难度校准的微调数据集，是替代纯在线RL的高效计算方案，重新定义了离线方法在RLVR中的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning in language models, with GRPO as its primary example. However, GRPO requires continuous online rollout generation, making it computationally expensive and difficult to scale. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a stable and efficient offline alternative, it is typically expected to underperform w.r.t. online RL methods such as GRPO when trained on rollouts from a cold supervised fine-tuned (SFT) policy. We introduce G2D (GRPO to DPO)}, a three-stage pipeline that performs a short GRPO warm-up, constructs a static preference dataset, and fine-tunes a model offline with DPO. Across a set of values of the number of online steps (K) in GRPO on Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, we find that offline DPO with moderate warm-up matches or outperforms GRPO at substantially lower compute cost in our setting. On Qwen2.5-7B, G2D at K=150 achieves 62.4% on MATH-500, outperforming GRPO (51.6%) by 10.8% at ~4x lower compute. On Llama-3.1-8B, G2D at K=500 achieves 49.4%, surpassing GRPO in our experimental setting. We show that performance is not governed by the number of preference pairs, which does not vary much w.r.t. K, but by their informativeness. Moderate warm-up produces rollouts with calibrated uncertainty, yielding stronger contrastive signal, while excessive warm-up leads to overconfident policies and less informative data. Our results recast the offline-online gap in RLVR as primarily a data informativeness problem, and identify short online RL warm-up with appropriate difficulty calibration of the fine-tuning dataset as a compute-efficient alternative to online RL.

</details>


### [156] [Markovian Circuit Tracing for Transformer State Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20824)
*Abdullah X*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了马尔可夫电路追踪（MCT）诊断管道，用于测试Transformer激活是否包含粗粒度状态转换结构。在合成HMM任务上，小型因果Transformer学习到了接近贝叶斯最优的预测器，残差激活中包含部分贝叶斯信念信息，状态强制干预实验证明了模型内部存在可恢复的状态转换结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多序列计算通过内部状态转移比孤立局部电路更容易研究。当前缺乏对Transformer激活中是否包含粗粒度状态转换结构的诊断方法，需要可控的合成基准来精确评估模型内部状态动力学。

Method: 引入马尔可夫电路追踪（MCT）诊断管道：1）构建合成隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）任务，其中潜在状态、转移矩阵、贝叶斯信念向量、贝叶斯最优预测和强制状态反事实目标均已知；2）在六种HMM家族上训练小型因果Transformer；3）从残差激活中提取状态抽象；4）通过状态强制干预（patching恢复的状态质心）验证状态结构。

Result: 小型因果Transformer学习到接近贝叶斯最优的预测器（平均超出贝叶斯损失仅0.0138）。残差激活包含部分贝叶斯信念信息，在持久性和低状态机制中最强，在模糊发射和六状态机制中较弱。状态强制干预效果显著：将恢复的状态质心patch到模型后，与HMM反事实目标的KL散度从0.1957降至0.0532，优于所有控制基线。

Conclusion: MCT作为简单的参考管道，提供了可控的基准和评估框架用于Transformer状态动力学可解释性研究。研究表明Transformer内部确实存在可恢复的粗粒度状态转换结构，为序列计算的状态转移视角提供了支持。

Abstract: Many sequence computations are easier to study as movement through internal states than as isolated local circuits. We introduce Markovian Circuit Tracing (MCT), a diagnostic pipeline for testing whether transformer activations contain coarse state-transition structure. The benchmark uses synthetic Hidden Markov Model (HMM) tasks where latent states, transition matrices, Bayesian belief vectors, Bayes-optimal predictions, and forced-state counterfactual targets are known exactly. Across six HMM families and three seeds per family, tiny causal transformers learn near-Bayes next-token predictors, with mean excess loss over Bayes of 0.0138. Residual activations contain partial Bayesian belief information in this controlled synthetic benchmark. State abstractions extracted from these activations recover coarse transition signal, strongest in persistent and lower-state regimes, and weaker in ambiguous-emission and six-state regimes. The clearest result comes from state forcing. Patching a recovered-state centroid reduces KL to the exact HMM counterfactual target from 0.1957 in the unpatched model to 0.0532 on average, beating wrong-state, mean-activation, random-activation, and shuffled-label controls. The contribution is a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework for transformer state-dynamics interpretability, with MCT as a simple reference pipeline

</details>


### [157] [\textit{Stochastic} MeanFlow Policies: One-Step Generative Control with Entropic Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21282)
*Zeyuan Wang,Da Li,Yulin Chen,Yuehu Gong,Yanming Guo,Ye Shi,Liang Bai,Tianyuan Yu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机均值流策略（SMFP），一种单步生成式策略类。它通过均值流变换将高斯噪声映射为动作，结合了熵正则化和镜像下降约束，在保持单步推理效率的同时，解决了高斯策略无法处理多模态分布和生成式策略推理慢或熵不可追踪的问题，在MuJoCo基准上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线离策略强化学习中存在两个核心耦合问题：策略类的选择和更新规则的选择。高斯策略速度快且熵易处理，但难以建模多模态动作分布；生成式策略表达能力强，但通常需要迭代采样或缺乏可处理的熵估计。在优化方面，SAC风格的软策略改进和镜像下降（MD）最小化不同的KL散度，前者使策略趋向价值诱导的玻尔兹曼分布，后者对每次更新进行正则化。将熵正则化与MD约束结合虽有吸引力（支持探索并稳定策略改进），但产生的目标分布是多模态的，与单模态高斯策略不匹配。

Method: 提出随机均值流策略（SMFP），这是一种单步生成式策略类。该方法通过均值流变换将高斯噪声映射到动作空间，实现随机重参数化。这种设计产生了可处理的熵代理，使得均值流策略能够在离策略镜像下降框架下，使用统一的目标函数进行训练，从而实现既具探索性又稳定的策略改进。

Result: 在七个MuJoCo基准测试中，SMFP方法在性能上优于高斯策略基线和生成式策略基线，同时保持了单步推理效率。这证明了该方法在处理多模态分布、支持探索和维持推理速度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: SMFP成功地将熵正则化与镜像下降约束结合，弥合了策略表达能力与优化稳定性之间的差距。该方法为离策略强化学习提供了一个统一的目标框架，在保持计算效率的同时实现了更强的策略表达能力，为未来强化学习算法的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is shaped by two coupled choices: the policy class and the update rule. Gaussian policies are fast and have tractable entropy, but struggle with multimodal action distributions. Generative policies are more expressive, but often require iterative sampling or lack tractable entropy estimates. On the optimisation side, SAC-style soft policy improvement and mirror descent (MD) can be viewed as minimising different KL divergences: the former moves the policy towards a value-induced Boltzmann distribution, while the latter regularises each update against the previous policy. Combining entropy regularisation with an MD constraint is therefore attractive, as it supports exploration while stabilising policy improvement; however, the resulting target can be multimodal and is poorly matched by unimodal Gaussian policies. We propose Stochastic MeanFlow Policies (SMFP), a one-step generative policy class that maps Gaussian noise to actions through a MeanFlow transformation. This stochastic reparameterisation yields a tractable entropy surrogate and allows MeanFlow policies to be trained within off-policy mirror descent under a unified objective for exploratory yet stable improvement. Across seven MuJoCo benchmarks, SMFP improves over Gaussian and generative baselines while retaining single-step inference efficiency.

</details>


### [158] [Finite-Time Regret Analysis of Retry-Aware Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20854)
*Bingkui Tong,Junpei Komiyama,Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为随机Bandit算法ReMax在M=2情况下证明了次线性遗憾界，揭示了该算法特有的“低估效应”如何导致其比汤普森采样更具利用性，并提出了缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReMax算法源于重试感知目标（如pass@$k$和max@$k$），旨在最大化多次尝试中的最佳结果。尽管该机制在强化学习中作为探索机制被提出，其在Bandit问题中的遗憾性质一直不清楚，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 针对高斯奖励和M=2这一非平凡情况，通过期望改进平衡条件刻画最优ReMax分布；在分析中，将次优臂的饱和行为与ReMax特有的低估效应（最优臂因不利估计而被采样过少）进行分离研究。

Result: 首次证明了ReMax的次线性遗憾界；理论分析揭示了ReMax比汤普森采样更具利用性的原因；实验表明在轻度低估情况下ReMax优于KL-UCB和汤普森采样，后验方差缩放可缓解严重低估问题。

Conclusion: ReMax算法在特定条件下具有理论保证的次线性遗憾性能，其独特的低估效应机制既解释了其潜在优势也指出了潜在风险，为实际应用中的参数调整（如后验方差缩放）提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a stochastic bandit algorithm motivated by retry-aware objectives that value the best outcome among multiple attempts, such as pass@$k$ and max@$k$. Given a posterior over arm values, ReMax chooses a sampling distribution that maximizes the posterior expected maximum reward over $M$ virtual draws. Although this objective was introduced in reinforcement learning as an exploration mechanism under uncertainty, its regret properties in bandit problems have remained unclear. For Gaussian rewards and the first nontrivial case $M=2$, we characterize the optimal ReMax distribution through an expected-improvement balance condition and prove the first sublinear regret bound for ReMax. Our analysis separates the usual saturation behavior of suboptimal arms from a ReMax-specific underestimation effect, in which the optimal arm may be sampled too rarely after an unfavorable estimate. This explains why ReMax can be more exploitative than Thompson sampling (TS) and why its regret analysis is technically delicate. Experiments support this picture: ReMax often outperforms KL-UCB and Thompson sampling under mild underestimation, while posterior-variance scaling empirically mitigates severe underestimation.

</details>


### [159] [TimeSRL: Generalizable Time-Series Behavioral Modeling via Semantic RL-Tuned LLMs -- A Case Study in Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21295)
*Yuang Fan,Lilin Xu,Millie Wu,Jingping Nie,Qingyu Chen,Yuzhe Yang,Zhuo Zhang,Xin Liu,Subigya Nepal,Xiaofan Jiang,Xuhai "Orson" Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeSRL框架，通过语义抽象解决跨数据集分布偏移问题，在心理健康预测任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向被动感知在连续健康预测中面临跨数据集分布偏移的挑战。传统机器学习方法容易过拟合队列特定特征，而大语言模型在处理长且异构的时间序列数据时推理能力不足，亟需一种能够提高泛化能力的新方法。

Method: 提出TimeSRL两阶段大语言模型框架，通过显式语义瓶颈进行预测。首先将原始信号抽象为高层自然语言描述，然后仅基于这些抽象进行行为结果预测。使用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）和可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）进行端到端优化，在无需金标准中间标注的情况下学习与结果对齐的抽象表示。

Result: 在心理健康预测基准测试中，采用严格的留一数据集验证（LOSO）协议评估跨队列泛化能力。TimeSRL在焦虑预测上将平均绝对误差（MAE）相比强非LLM机器学习基线降低3.1-10.1%，相比LLM基线降低9.5-44.1%；在抑郁预测上分别降低3.2-9.6%和27.4-57.6%（所有p<0.05）。在跨基准迁移测试中，TimeSRL无需目标域微调即可达到接近域内性能的表现。

Conclusion: 语义抽象具有良好的可重用性，为通过强化学习调优的大语言模型实现可泛化的行为建模提供了新方向。该方法证明了强制模型在语义层面推理能够有效提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Longitudinal passive sensing enables continuous health prediction, yet models often fail under cross-dataset distribution shifts. Traditional ML overfits cohort-specific artifacts, while Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to reason reliably over long, heterogeneous time-series. We introduce TimeSRL, a two-stage LLM framework that routes predictions through an explicit semantic bottleneck. The model first abstracts raw signals into high-level natural language, then predicts behavioral outcomes from these abstractions alone. This forces the model to reason over semantic concepts that we argue generalize better than raw numbers. We optimize this process end-to-end using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), learning outcome-aligned abstractions without gold intermediate annotations. Instantiated on mental-health prediction, TimeSRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on a benchmark designed to stress-test cross-cohort generalization under a rigorous leave-one-dataset-out (LOSO) protocol, reducing mean absolute error (MAE) over strong non-LLM ML and LLM baselines by 3.1--10.1% and 9.5--44.1% for anxiety, and 3.2--9.6% and 27.4--57.6% for depression (all $p$s<0.05). TimeSRL significantly outperforms prior methods in cross-benchmark transfer across different sensing pipelines, rivaling its own within-domain performance without target-domain fine-tuning. These results demonstrate that semantic abstractions are reusable and point to a new direction for generalizable behavior modeling via RL-tuned LLMs.

</details>


### [160] [From Circuit Evidence to Mechanistic Theory: An Inductive Logic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21303)
*Nura Aljaafari,Danilo S. Carvalho,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化框架，将神经网络的电路解释视为归纳理论构建过程，通过因果功能签名（CFS）和架构签名（τ_arch）两个层次来表征电路，实现了跨模型规模和架构家族的机制发现的可比性与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机制可解释性研究发现的电路往往是孤立的实验产物，缺乏共享的形式化表示来描述电路的计算内容、相互关系，以及判断不同发现是否支持同一机制，这阻碍了机制科学知识的累积与传承。

Method: 提出双层表征框架：（1）因果功能签名（CFS），基于因果归因证据和token角色档案来刻画组件行为；（2）架构签名（τ_arch），通过归纳逻辑编程（ILP）从尺度不变的结构谓词中学习。两者共同构成形式化连贯层，使机制主张显式化并可通过θ-包含关系进行比较。

Result: CFS揭示了不同任务类型间定性差异化的计算策略（如注意力介导的复制 vs. MLP介导的绑定）；ILP签名在结构分离性上显著优于图核和特征向量基线方法，并支持跨模型规模和架构家族的原则性迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作为累积性机制科学提供了形式化基础设施，使电路发现不再是孤立产物，而是可比较、可组合、可迁移的形式化知识，推动了机制可解释性从实验性发现向系统性科学的转变。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability produces circuit-level causal analyses of neural network behaviour, but discovered circuits often remain isolated experimental artefacts: there is no shared formal representation for what circuits compute, how they relate, or when two findings provide evidence for the same mechanism. This work provides a formal infrastructure for cumulative mechanistic science by treating circuit interpretation as inductive theory construction. Each circuit is characterised at two levels: a Causal Functional Signature (CFS), which grounds component behaviour in causal attribution evidence and token role profiles, and an architectural signature $τ_{\mathrm{arch}}$, learned by inductive logic programming (ILP) from scale-invariant structural predicates. Together, these constitute a formal coherence layer that makes mechanistic claims explicit, comparable via $θ$-subsumption, and portable across model scales. CFS reveals qualitatively distinct computational strategies across task types, including attention-mediated copying versus MLP-mediated binding. ILP signatures achieve substantially better structural separation than graph kernel and feature-vector baselines, and support principled transfer across model scales and architecture families.

</details>


### [161] [DeCoR: Design and Control Co-Optimization for Urban Streets Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21311)
*Bibek Poudel,Lei Zhu,Kevin Heaslip,Sai Swaminathan,Weizi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeCoR，一个两阶段强化学习框架，联合优化人行横道布局和信号控制，在真实城市场景中显著降低了行人和车辆的延误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉系统已能大规模检测和预测城市交通参与者，但将感知输出转化为城市设计方案的能力仍然有限。现有方法难以有效衔接感知数据与基础设施设计优化。

Method: 提出DeCoR两阶段强化学习框架：设计阶段将行人网络编码为图，学习生成策略以高斯混合模型参数化人行横道位置和宽度；控制阶段为每个布局学习自适应信号配时策略，联合最小化行人和车辆延误。

Result: 在750米真实城市走廊测试中，DeCoR学习的人行横道布局使行人到达最近人行横道的时间减少23%，且使用更少的人行横道；控制方面，行人和车辆等待时间分别降低79%和65%；控制策略对训练外需求具有泛化性，且对布局变化无需重新训练即具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DeCoR框架成功实现了从感知数据到城市设计优化的转化，通过联合优化布局与控制，在真实场景中显著提升了交通效率，证明了强化学习在基础设施设计中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Modern vision systems can detect, track, and forecast urban actors at scale, yet translating perception outputs to urban design remains limited. We introduce DeCoR, a two-stage reinforcement learning framework that leverages flow observations to co-optimize crosswalk layout and network-level signal control. The design stage encodes the pedestrian network as a graph and learns a generative policy that parameterizes a Gaussian mixture model over crosswalk location and width, from which new crosswalks are sampled. For each layout, a shared control policy learns adaptive signal timings to minimize joint pedestrian and vehicle delay. On a 750 m real-world urban corridor with demand sensed from video and Wi-Fi logs, DeCoR learns a layout that reduces pedestrian arrival time to their nearest crosswalk by 23% while using fewer crosswalks than existing configurations. On the control side, DeCoR reduces pedestrian and vehicle wait time by 79% and 65%, respectively, relative to fixed-time signalization. Further, the control policy generalizes to demands outside of training and is robust to layout changes without retraining.

</details>


### [162] [Data-Efficient Neural Operator Training via Physics-Based Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21348)
*Alicja Polanska,Lorenzo Zanisi,Vignesh Gopakumar,Stanislas Pamela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的主动学习算法，利用偏微分方程残差指导数据选择，在显著降低训练数据需求的同时，达到了最先进的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用神经算子求解偏微分方程虽然能大幅降低计算成本，但面临训练数据需求量大的瓶颈。主动学习可以通过迭代方式选择性获取最有信息量的样本来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于物理信息的主动学习算法。该方法利用偏微分方程残差来指导数据选择过程，将物理归纳偏置注入训练中，确保模拟计算资源被用于模型物理理解最薄弱的区域。

Result: 通过1D Burgers方程和2D可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的数值实验验证，该方法在实验中持续优于随机采样策略，并在数据效率方面达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: 基于物理信息的获取策略不仅匹配了最先进的数据效率，还具有将物理归纳偏置注入训练过程的独特优势，为神经算子求解偏微分方程提供了一种高效的数据选择方法。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations with neural operators significantly reduces computational costs but remains bottlenecked by high training data requirements. Active learning offers a natural framework to mitigate this by selectively acquiring the most informative samples in an iterative manner. We introduce physics-based acquisition - a novel physics-informed active learning algorithm that leverages the partial differential equation residual to guide data selection. We validate the method by presenting numerical experiments for the 1D Burgers equation and the 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations. We show that, in our experiments, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and matches the state of the art in data efficiency. At the same time, it has the unique advantage of injecting a physics inductive bias into the training process, ensuring that simulation cost is spent where the model's physical understanding is weakest.

</details>


### [163] [CIG: Exploration via Conditional Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20878)
*Tim Joseph,Marcus Fechner,Philipp Stegmaier,Karam Daaboul,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件信息增益（CIG）奖励作为探索的内在奖励信号，通过集成分歧核的对数行列式目标和Cholesky分解，实现了同时条件于回放缓冲区和轨迹前缀的可扩展奖励，在高维状态空间中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索内在奖励方法存在固有局限：终身奖励忽略轨迹内冗余，情节奖励丢弃终身进度，混合方法依赖启发式权重或不可扩展的高斯过程动态；轨迹级信息增益虽理想但在深度模型中不可计算。

Method: 推导条件信息增益（CIG）奖励作为可处理的替代目标：基于集成分歧核的对数行列式目标，利用Cholesky分解产生因果性每步奖励，同时条件于回放缓冲区和轨迹前缀，并扩展至高维状态空间；在模型based设置中实例化。

Result: 在涵盖离散（MiniGrid）和连续控制（OGBench）的12个任务上，在clean和随机干扰设置下，CIG优于或匹配先前的探索方法，同时对随机干扰器保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CIG奖励成功解决了内在奖励需要同时考虑累积经验和轨迹内信息的挑战，提供了可扩展、鲁棒且高效的探索机制，为深度强化学习中的探索问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Intrinsic rewards for exploration in reinforcement learning condition on different contexts: lifelong rewards score each transition against accumulated experience but ignore within-rollout redundancy; episodic rewards penalize intra-trajectory repetition but discard lifetime progress. Hybrid methods combine both signals through heuristic weights or require Gaussian-process dynamics that do not scale beyond low-dimensional state spaces. Trajectory-level information gain decomposes into per-step terms that condition on the replay buffer and rollout prefix simultaneously, but remains intractable for deep models. We derive the Conditional Information Gain (CIG) reward as a tractable surrogate: a log-determinant objective over an ensemble disagreement kernel whose Cholesky factorization yields causal per-step rewards that retain both conditioning sets while scaling to high-dimensional state spaces. We instantiate CIG in a model-based setting, where rollouts are short and within-rollout corrections remain largely unexplored. Across twelve tasks spanning discrete (MiniGrid) and continuous control (OGBench), in both clean and stochastic-distractor settings, CIG outperforms or matches prior exploration methods while remaining robust to stochastic distractors.

</details>


### [164] [On the Regularity and Generalization of One-Step Wasserstein-guided Generative Models for PDE-Induced Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21388)
*Likun Lin,Zhongjian Wang,Jack Xin,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为学习PDE诱导概率分布的一步生成模型提供了理论保证，证明了最优传输映射具有Hölder连续性，并导出了超额风险界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在实际应用中取得了显著成功，但关于其在科学计算中统计精度的现有理论大多较为悲观。本文旨在为基于PDE的概率测度的一步Wasserstein引导生成模型建立正则性和泛化性质的理论框架。

Method: 研究线性椭圆和抛物方程及扩散和Fokker-Planck方程诱导的目标测度，证明其满足倍增条件。结合倍增测度间最优传输的正则性理论，证明从均匀源测度到目标测度的最优传输映射具有Hölder连续性。以DeepParticle为例，分析一步生成模型的超额风险。

Result: 证明了目标测度满足倍增条件，最优传输映射具有Hölder连续性，为一步生成模型提供了逼近论依据。推导了刻画学习映射与总体最优映射之间差异的超额风险界限，并建立了目标偏移下的鲁棒性估计。实验结果支持了推导的理论速率。

Conclusion: 本文为理解PDE诱导分布的一步生成模型的正则性和泛化性质建立了理论基础，证明了最优传输映射的Hölder连续性，为这类模型的逼近论合理性提供了严格证明，并通过理论分析和实验验证了结果的有效性。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of generative models, the available theory on their statistical accuracy in scientific computing remains largely pessimistic. This paper develops a theoretical framework for understanding the regularity of transport maps and the generalization properties of one-step Wasserstein-guided generative models for PDE-induced probability measures. We consider normalized target densities associated with linear elliptic and parabolic equations on bounded domains, as well as diffusion and Fokker--Planck equations on the torus. Under standard structural assumptions, we prove that these target measures satisfy doubling conditions. By combining this fact with regularity theory for optimal transport between doubling measures, we show that the optimal transport map from a uniform source measure to the target measure is Hölder continuous. This regularity yields an approximation-theoretic justification for one-step generative models that learn PDE-induced distributions via a single pushforward map. As a representative instance, we study DeepParticle and derive excess-risk bounds characterizing the discrepancy between the learned map and the population-optimal map. We also establish a robustness estimate under target shift and illustrate the theory with experiments which support the derived rates.

</details>


### [165] [NeighborDiv: Training-free Zero-shot Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Neighbor Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20879)
*Kaifeng Wei,Teng Liu,Liang Dong,Xiubo Liang,Yuke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeighborDiv，一个无需训练的通用图异常检测框架，通过"邻居间多样性范式"替代传统的"节点-邻居一致性范式"，实现了更高效且性能更优的跨域异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用图异常检测(GGAD)方法主要依赖节点-邻居一致性原则，存在训练流程复杂、对训练数据依赖重、计算成本高以及跨域泛化不稳定等局限性。

Method: 提出NeighborDiv框架，采用"邻居间多样性范式"，通过计算邻居间特征相似度的方差来量化邻居多样性，捕捉节点如何组织其局部图环境，作为独立的异常判别信号。该方法无需训练过程。

Result: 在两种标准GGAD评估范式下取得最优性能：在单域独立训练(SDIT)下，AUC相对提升10.25%，AP提升17.78%；在统一多域训练(UMDT)下，AUC/AP分别提升6.89%/9.58%。所有数据集上性能波动为零。

Conclusion: NeighborDiv成功摆脱了对训练集的依赖，建立了一个轻量级且高度实用的GGAD框架，为零训练开销的跨域图异常检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is increasingly shifting to Generalist GAD (GGAD) for cross-domain "one-for-all" detection, but existing GGAD methods predominantly rely on the neighbor consistency principle, falling into the \textbf{Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm} for anomaly quantification. These methods suffer from complex training pipelines, heavy training data dependency, high computational costs, and unstable cross-domain generalization. To address these limitations, we propose NeighborDiv, a training-free generalist graph anomaly detection framework based on neighbor diversity. Departing from the dominant Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm, we shift the focus to the \textbf{Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm}, and uncover that the internal structural dispersion of a node's neighbor set is a powerful, independently discriminative anomaly signal. We quantify neighbor diversity via the variance of inter-neighbor feature similarities, which captures how a node organizes its local graph environment, and operates independently of conventional node-to-neighbor consistency frameworks. Extensive experiments under two standard GGAD evaluation paradigms show NeighborDiv achieves state-of-the-art performance, with relative gains of 10.25% in average AUC and 17.78% in average AP over the second-best baseline under Single-Domain Independent Training (SDIT), and 6.89%/9.58% in AUC/AP under Unified Multi-Domain Training (UMDT), respectively. Notably, NeighborDiv yields zero performance volatility across all datasets, eliminating training-set dependency and establishing a lightweight and highly practical GGAD framework.

</details>


### [166] [Learning fMRI activations dictionaries across individual geometries via optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20883)
*Sonia Mazelet,Rémi Flamary,Bertrand Thirion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) 距离的新型字典学习方法，用于解决fMRI数据分析中跨个体脑几何变异的挑战，并通过 amortized optimization 显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的fMRI字典学习方法通常将个体脑几何投影到通用模板上以解决几何变异问题，但这会丢失受试者特定的信息。为了在保留个体特征的同时处理跨个体脑几何差异，需要一种能够显式处理这种几何变异的新方法。

Method: 1. 使用基于最优传输的Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) 距离来比较具有不同几何结构和特征的图结构。\n2. 针对 fMRI 大规模图结构计算多次 FGW 距离的计算挑战，采用 amortized optimization 训练神经网络来预测最优传输计划的近似解，大幅降低计算成本。\n3. 学习依赖于 FGW 权衡参数的字典原子，该参数控制特征对齐与结构一致性之间的平衡。

Result: 在 HCP (Human Connectome Project) 数据集上的数值实验表明，该方法能够捕获数据中不同层级的几何变异性，并生成能够保留关键信息的表征。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于 FGW 的字典学习方法成功地在处理跨个体脑几何变异的同时保留了重要的受试者特定信息，为 fMRI 数据分析提供了一种更优的表征学习途径。

Abstract: Dictionary learning is a powerful tool for creating interpretable representations. When applied to functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, the resulting patterns of brain activity can be used for various downstream tasks, such as brain state classification or population-level analysis. However, a major challenge is the variability in brain geometry across individuals. This is usually addressed by projecting each individual brain geometry onto a common template, which removes subject-specific information. In this work, we introduce a novel approach to dictionary learning on fMRI data that explicitly accounts for this variability. We use the optimal transport-based Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) distance to compare graphs with different geometries and features. To address the challenge of computing multiple FGW distances for large graphs such as those arising from fMRI data, we rely on amortized optimization to learn a neural network that predicts an approximation of the optimal transport plans, which substantially reduces the computational cost. Additionally, we learn dictionary atoms that depend on the FGW trade-off parameter, which controls the balance between feature alignment and structural consistency. Numerical experiments on the HCP dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach captures different levels of geometric variability in the data and provides representations that preserve essential information.

</details>


### [167] [HiRes: Inspectable Precedent Memory for Reaction Condition Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21420)
*Shreyas Vinaya Sathyanarayana,Raja Sekhar Pappala,Deepak Warrier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HiRes，一种检索增强的反应条件推荐系统，在USPTO-Condition数据集上达到最优性能，同时提供了可解释的先例支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应条件推荐需要在逆合成断键选择后进行，化学家不仅要求预测准确，还需要能够证明预测合理性的先例。现有方法在预测准确性与化学可解释性之间存在差距。

Method: HiRes（分层反应表示）系统结合了图编码器、变换感知交叉注意力机制、多流反应融合和k近邻检索层。其学习的反应空间同时作为分类器特征和可检查的先例记忆，将检索与学习的条件头相结合。

Result: 在USPTO-Condition数据集上达到最先进性能，催化剂、溶剂和试剂的top-1准确率分别达到0.929、0.534和0.530。在催化剂任务上与最佳基线持平，在溶剂和试剂任务上优于REACON等模型。配对自助法分析表明，集成检索方法在溶剂和试剂选择上相比纯参数方法具有统计显著性提升。

Conclusion: HiRes成功弥合了预测准确性与化学可解释性之间的差距，提供了单一表示方法，既能给出有竞争力的条件推荐，又能提供合成规划实践所需的具体化学先例。

Abstract: Reaction condition recommendation sits immediately after retrosynthetic disconnection selection, and in practice, chemists require both accurate predictions and the precedents that justify them. We present HiRes (Hierarchical Reaction Representations), a retrieval-augmented condition recommendation system whose learned reaction space serves as both a classifier feature and an inspectable precedent memory. The model combines a graph encoder, transformation-aware cross-attention, multi-stream reaction fusion, and a k-NN retrieval layer. HiRes achieves state-of-the-art performance among primary-slot USPTO-Condition models, reaching Catalyst, Solvent, and Reagent top-1 accuracies (Acc@1) of 0.929, 0.534, and 0.530 respectively. It ties the best reported baseline on Catalyst while outperforming models such as REACON on Solvent and Reagent. Furthermore, paired bootstrap analysis demonstrates that integrating retrieval with learned condition heads provides statistically significant gains for solvent and reagent selection over purely parametric approaches. Ultimately, HiRes bridges the gap between predictive accuracy and chemical interpretability, offering a single representation that supplies both competitive recommendations and the concrete chemical precedents necessary for practical synthesis planning.

</details>


### [168] [Training distribution determines the ceiling of drug-blind cancer sensitivity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20885)
*Taekyung Heo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 药物盲敏感性预测的性能瓶颈源于评估指标缺陷和训练策略不当，而非药物表征能力不足。通过机制分层训练和响应匹配可恢复预测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要从分子图谱预测特定肿瘤的药物敏感性，但药物盲敏感性预测方法已进入瓶颈期。尽管药物表征日益复杂，性能却停滞不前，需要探究其根本原因。

Method: 1. 揭示全局Pearson r指标的缺陷：该指标被药物间效力差异主导，仅靠药物均值预测器即可捕获，无需细胞特异性学习；2. 采用药物内Pearson r指标隔离药物内细胞排序；3. 设计对照实验，将作用机制作为药物特征或训练分布约束，分析其影响；4. 在四个独立数据集上验证药物编码相比仅细胞特征无改进。

Result: 1. 全局Pearson r指标存在人为假象，不能真实反映预测能力；2. 药物内Pearson r显示药物编码无法改进仅细胞特征的预测效果；3. 将作用机制作为特征供给几乎无益，但用于训练分层可大幅提高靶向激酶抑制剂的预测性能；4. 泛癌共训练会抑制通路特异性敏感性信号。

Conclusion: 药物盲敏感性预测的停滞反映了评估指标缺陷而非表征瓶颈。机制分层训练和基于试点观察的响应匹配是两种可部署策略，二者结合可恢复药物盲敏感性预测的主要预测增益来源。

Abstract: Precision oncology requires predicting which drugs will suppress a specific tumor from its molecular profile, but drug-blind sensitivity prediction has plateaued despite increasingly complex drug representations. Here we show that this stagnation reflects a metric artifact rather than a representational bottleneck. The standard benchmark, global Pearson r, is dominated by between-drug potency differences that a trivial drug-mean predictor captures without any cell-specific learning. Per-drug Pearson r, which isolates within-drug cell ranking, reveals that no drug encoding improves over cell-only features across four independent datasets. A controlled experiment channeling mechanism-of-action identity as either a drug feature or a training-distribution constraint identifies the cause. Supplying MoA as a feature yields negligible benefit, whereas using it to stratify training raises per-drug r substantially for targeted kinase inhibitors, because pan-cancer co-training suppresses pathway-specific sensitivity signals. Mechanism-stratified training and response matching from pilot observations provide two deployable strategies that together recover the principal sources of predictive gain in drug-blind sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [169] [torchtune: PyTorch native post-training library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21442)
*Mark Obozov,Maxime Griot,Joseph Cummings,Evan Smothers,Felipe Mello,Rafi Ayub,Philip John Bontrager,Salman Mohammadi,Ariel Kwiatkowski,Nathan Azrak,Mircea Mironenco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍了torchtune，一个PyTorch原生的LLM后训练库，强调模块化、可修改性和对底层组件的直接访问，在性能和内存效率上表现优异，同时保持足够的灵活性以支持快速研究迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM需要多阶段训练流程，后训练是适配开源模型的主要接口。现有的微调框架往往在易用性、专用配方或硬件效率方面进行优化，但以牺牲透明度和可扩展性为代价。因此需要一个新的库来平衡这些需求。

Method: 设计了一个PyTorch原生库torchtune，核心设计原则包括模块化、可修改性和对底层PyTorch组件的直接访问。通过模型构建器、训练配方和分布式训练栈来实现这些原则。与流行的微调框架（Axolotl和Unsloth）进行对比评估。

Result: torchtune在多种后训练设置中表现出强劲的性能和内存效率，同时保持足够的灵活性以支持快速研究迭代。与现有框架相比，成功平衡了性能、效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: torchtune作为一个实用的基础平台，为可重现的LLM后训练研究提供了支持，成功填补了现有框架在透明度和可扩展性方面的不足。

Abstract: Modern LLMs typically require multistage training pipelines to achieve strong downstream performance, with post-training serving as the main interface for adapting open-weight models. We introduce torchtune, a PyTorch-native library designed to streamline the post-training lifecycle of LLMs, enabling efficient fine-tuning, experimentation, and deployment-oriented workflows. Unlike many existing fine-tuning frameworks, which often optimize for ease of use, specialized recipes, or hardware efficiency at the cost of transparency and extensibility, torchtune emphasizes modularity, hackability, and direct access to the underlying PyTorch components. In this paper, we present the design principles behind torchtune, describe how they are reflected in its model builders, training recipes, and distributed training stack, and evaluate the library across representative post-training settings. We compare against popular fine-tuning frameworks, including Axolotl and Unsloth, and show that torchtune provides strong performance and memory efficiency across many settings while remaining flexible enough for rapid research iteration. These results position torchtune as a practical foundation for reproducible LLMs post-training research.

</details>


### [170] [Approximation Theory for Neural Networks: Old and New](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21451)
*Soumendu Sundar Mukherjee,Himasish Talukdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经网络逼近理论，从经典的密度结果发展到现代定量理论，重点关注深度-宽度权衡、参数效率以及新兴的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(KANs)的逼近特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用逼近定理为神经网络的表达能力提供了数学解释。过去四十年中，这些定性的普适性结果已发展成为一个丰富的定量理论，需要系统性地综述逼近率、参数效率以及深度和宽度等架构特征的作用。

Method: 本综述回顾了单隐藏层神经网络的经典密度结果，以及将逼近误差与网络规模和目标函数光滑性假设相关联的定量界限。重点分析了深度-宽度权衡，以及深架构在结构化函数类中实现优越参数效率的结果。此外，还回顾了Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(KANs)的最新发展。

Result: 综述揭示了在温和的激活函数条件下，前馈神经网络在紧集上的连续函数、$L^p$空间或Sobolev空间等广泛函数类中具有稠密性。定量分析表明，更深的架构可以在结构化函数类中实现更好的参数效率。KANs作为替代架构范式，其逼近理论特性已开始受到重要的理论关注。

Conclusion: 神经网络逼近理论已从定性密度结果发展为涵盖逼近率、参数效率和架构特征的丰富定量理论。深度架构在参数效率方面表现出优势，而KANs等新架构为逼近理论提供了新的研究方向，展现了该领域的持续发展和深化。

Abstract: Universal approximation theorems provide a mathematical explanation for the expressive power of neural networks. They assert that, under mild conditions on the activation function, feedforward neural networks are dense in broad function classes, such as continuous functions on compact subsets of $\mathbb{R}^d$, $L^p$ spaces, or Sobolev spaces. Over the past four decades, these qualitative universality results have evolved into a rich quantitative theory addressing approximation rates, parameter efficiency, and the role of architectural features such as depth and width. This survey presents several glimpses into this theory. We review classical density results for single-hidden-layer networks, as well as quantitative bounds that relate approximation error to network size and smoothness assumptions on target functions. Particular emphasis is placed on depth--width trade-offs and on results demonstrating that deeper architectures can achieve superior parameter efficiency for structured function classes. In addition to standard feedforward neural networks, we also review recent developments on Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs), which offer an alternative architectural paradigm and whose approximation-theoretic properties have begun to attract significant theoretical attention.

</details>


### [171] [Agent JIT Compilation for Latency-Optimizing Web Agent Planning and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21470)
*Caleb Winston,Ron Yifeng Wang,Azalia Mirhoseini,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agent即时编译（JIT）方法，将任务描述直接编译为可执行代码，相比现有方法实现了10.4倍加速和28%准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用代理（CUA）采用顺序的获取-截图-执行循环，每次迭代都需要调用LLM，导致高延迟和频繁的工具使用错误。

Method: 提出即时编译方法，包含三个组件：(1) JIT-Planner生成多个代码计划，验证并选择最小成本候选；(2) JIT-Scheduler通过蒙特卡洛代价估计探索并行化策略；(3)不变量强制工具协议指定前置和后置条件状态要求，减少错误工具使用率。

Result: 在5个Web应用上，JIT-Planner相比Browser-Use实现10.4倍加速和+28%准确率提升；JIT-Scheduler相比OpenAI CUA实现2.4倍加速和+9%准确率提升。

Conclusion: Agent即时编译方法通过将任务描述编译为可执行代码并优化并行化策略，显著提升了计算机使用代理的执行速度和准确率。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUA) automate tasks specified with natural language such as "order the cheapest item from Taco Bell" by generating sequences of calls to tools such as click, type, and scroll on a browser. Current implementations follow a sequential fetch-screenshot-execute loop where each iteration requires an LLM call, resulting in high latency and frequent errors from incorrect tool use. We present agent just-in-time (JIT) compilation, an alternative that compiles task descriptions directly into executable code that is free to include LLM calls, tool calls, and parallelization. Our approach comprises three components: (1) JIT-Planner, which generates multiple code plans, validates each against tool specifications, and selects the minimum-cost candidate; (2) JIT-Scheduler, which explores parallelization strategies via Monte Carlo cost estimation from learned latency distributions; and (3) an invariant-enforcing tool protocol specifying precondition and postcondition state requirements that reduce the rate of generating plans with incorrect tool use. Across 5 web applications, JIT-Planner achieves $10.4\times$ speedup and $+28\%$ accuracy over Browser-Use, while JIT-Scheduler achieves $2.4\times$ speedup and $+9\%$ accuracy over OpenAI CUA.

</details>


### [172] [Quantifying Hyperparameter Transfer and the Importance of Embedding Layer Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21486)
*Dayal Singh Kalra,Maissam Barkeshli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现μP相对于标准参数化(SP)在AdamW训练中的主要优势源于嵌入层学习率的最大化，通过提高SP中嵌入层学习率可显著改善训练稳定性和超参数迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数迁移对于大语言模型训练至关重要，但现有理论无法充分解释为何μP能提供高质量的学习率迁移，因此需要建立量化框架并深入分析其内在机制。

Method: 建立包含三个指标的量化框架：(1)缩放定律拟合质量、(2)对外推误差的鲁棒性、(3)参数化选择导致的渐近损失惩罚；通过系统性的消融实验研究μP相对于SP的优势来源。

Result: μP相对于SP的主要优势在于最大化嵌入层学习率；在SP中，嵌入层学习率是导致训练不稳定的瓶颈，将其提高width倍可显著改善训练和超参数迁移；权重衰减改善缩放定律拟合，但在固定token-per-parameter设置下会损害外推鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 标准参数化中的嵌入层学习率限制了超参数迁移性能，通过简单调整即可获得与μP相当的效果，这一发现为理解和改进超参数迁移提供了新的视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer allows extrapolating optimal optimization hyperparameters from small to large scales, making it critical for training large language models (LLMs). This is done either by fitting a scaling law to the hyperparameters or by a judicious choice of parameterization, such as Maximal Update ($μ$P), that renders optimal hyperparameters approximately scale invariant. In this paper, we first develop a framework to quantify hyperparameter transfer through three metrics: (1) the quality of the scaling law fit, (2) the robustness to extrapolation errors, and (3) the asymptotic loss penalty due to choice of parameterization. Next, we investigate through a comprehensive series of ablations why $μ$P appears to offer high-quality learning rate transfer relative to standard parameterization (SP), as existing theory is inadequate. We find that the overwhelming benefit of $μ$P relative to SP when training with AdamW arises simply from maximizing the learning rate of the embedding layer. In SP, the embedding layer learning rate acts as a bottleneck that induces training instabilities; increasing it by a factor of width to match $μ$P dramatically smooths out training while improving hyperparameter transfer. We also find that weight decay improves the scaling law fits, while, in the fixed token-per-parameter setting, it hurts the robustness of the extrapolation.

</details>


### [173] [A Deployment Audit of Release-Side Risk in Conformal Triage under Prevalence Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20956)
*Chengze Li,Xiao Liu,Hanrong Zhang,Haiyang Peng,Yanghao Ruan,Huanhuan Ma,Chunyu Miao,Qichao Zhou,Xiangrong Qi,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对保形分诊（conformal triage）的泄漏感知部署审计方法，用于在流行率偏移下检测被直接释放的事件阳性患者的安全风险，并发现NSCLC试点项目中较低的审查率具有误导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流行率偏移（prevalence shift）情况下，传统的边际覆盖率和人工审查率摘要无法回答一个关键的安全问题：真正经历目标事件（如疾病阳性）的患者是否在未经审查的情况下被直接释放（released）。这一缺口可能导致不安全的部署决策。

Method: 引入一种泄漏感知部署审计（leakage-aware deployment audit）方法，将目标受试者分配到三个非重叠的角色：流行率校正（prevalence correction）、保形校准（conformal calibration）和保留释放安全评估（held-out release-safety evaluation）。这种分离机制使得审计能够直接评估释放安全性，包括被无审查释放的事件阳性患者数量、校准所需的事件标签是否充足，以及安全-审查权衡的变化。

Result: 在对非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）回顾性试点项目的应用中发现：经过流行率校正后，合并保形分支（pooled conformal branch）虽然降低了审查率，但通过释放更多患者实现，其中包括部分事件阳性患者，揭示了低审查率的误导性；类条件分支（classwise branch）作为稀缺性诊断工具，显示该试点项目的事件标签过少，无法认证安全的低审查释放。

Conclusion: 该审计方法能够有效揭示流行率偏移下保形分诊部署中的安全隐患，证明单纯的审查率降低可能掩盖对事件阳性患者的错误释放风险，并为判断数据是否足以支持安全部署提供了诊断手段。

Abstract: Conformal triage converts predictive scores into deployment actions that either release a case, flag it for urgent attention, or defer it to human review. Under prevalence shift, however, the usual summaries of marginal coverage and human-review rate can miss the safety-critical question of whether patients who truly experience the target event are released without review. To address this gap, we introduce a leakage-aware deployment audit for release-side conformal triage. It first assigns target subjects to three non-overlapping roles: prevalence correction, conformal calibration, and held-out release-safety evaluation. This separation then lets the audit evaluate release directly: how many event-positive patients are cleared without review, whether the pilot has enough event labels for calibration, and how the safety-review trade-off shifts. Applying this audit to a retrospective NSCLC pilot shows why lower review can be misleading: after prevalence correction, the pooled conformal branch lowers review by releasing more patients, some of whom are event-positive. Within the audit, the classwise branch acts as a scarcity diagnostic: the pilot has too few event labels to certify safe low-review release.

</details>


### [174] [Variance Reduction for Expectations with Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21489)
*Jesse Bettencourt,Xindi Wu,Matan Atzmon,James Lucas,Jonathan Lorraine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CARV框架，通过分层蒙特卡洛估计器降低预训练扩散模型下游任务的梯度方差，在text-to-3D任务中实现2-3倍有效计算提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型作为冻结教师模型为下游流程（如text-to-3D、单步蒸馏、数据归因）提供梯度，但这些梯度是噪声水平和高斯噪声样本上的蒙特卡洛期望，其估计器方差主导了计算成本，因为每次采样都需要昂贵的上游计算（渲染、仿真、编码）。

Method: 提出CARV框架，一种计算感知的方差计算框架，采用分层蒙特卡洛估计器：在廉价的扩散噪声重采样上分摊昂贵的上游计算，并通过时间步重要性采样和分层逆CDF构造进行增强。

Result: 在text-to-3D蒸馏和归因实验中，CARV在不改变目标函数的情况下实现了2-3倍有效计算乘数（主要来自分摊重用；约25%来自重要性采样+分层）；在单步蒸馏中，相同技术将梯度方差降低了一个数量级，但未改善下游FID。

Conclusion: CARV成功降低了扩散模型下游任务的梯度估计方差并提升了计算效率，但单步蒸馏实验结果揭示了MC方差不再是瓶颈的计算机制，为实际应用提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models serve as frozen teachers feeding downstream pipelines such as text-to-3D, single-step distillation, and data attribution. The teacher gradients these pipelines consume are Monte Carlo (MC) expectations over noise levels and Gaussian noise samples; their estimator variance dominates compute cost because each draw requires expensive upstream work (rendering, simulation, encoding). We introduce CARV, a compute-aware variance-accounting framework that motivates a hierarchical MC estimator: amortize the expensive upstream computation over cheap diffusion-noise resamples, sharpened by timestep importance sampling and a stratified-inverse-CDF construction. In our text-to-3D distillation and attribution experiments, CARV delivers 2-3x effective compute multipliers (most from amortized reuse; ~25% additional from IS+stratification) without changing the objective; in single-step distillation, the same techniques cut gradient variance by an order of magnitude but do not improve downstream FID, marking the regime where MC variance is no longer the bottleneck.

</details>


### [175] [Choose Wisely and Privately: Proactive Client Selection for Fair and Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20975)
*Adda Akram Bendoukha,Heber Hwang Arcolezi,Nesrine Kaaniche,Aymen Boudguiga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种主动式客户端选择框架，通过潜在联邦损失在训练前寻找最优客户端子集，在保证差分隐私的前提下提升非独立同分布数据场景下的联邦学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于平均的联邦学习方法在非独立同分布数据上存在收敛速度慢和模型精度低的问题。传统替代方案效率低下，包含噪声或高度异构数据的客户端所贡献的昂贵梯度计算往往被丢弃或大幅降权，这种被动式方法浪费计算资源、增加通信轮数并导致不必要的隐私暴露。

Method: 提出主动式客户端选择框架，从差分隐私列联表中计算互信息来量化联合数据集中跨特征相关性；引入潜在联邦损失(PFL)平衡两个目标：最大化集体数据效用，同时确保公平的跨特征相关性以防止群体不公平；将客户端选择建模为PFL目标下的最优子集搜索问题，在强差分隐私保证下使用模拟退火算法求解。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，即使在采用最先进的自适应聚合或采样策略的情况下，通过该方法找到的最优联邦训练出的模型比均匀采样更快、更公平、更准确。

Conclusion: 主动式客户端选择策略通过在训练开始前识别最优客户端联盟，有效解决了非独立同分布数据带来的挑战，实现了更快的收敛速度、更好的公平性和更高的模型精度，证明了在训练前进行客户端筛选比被动式聚合策略更具优势。

Abstract: Federated Learning enables collaborative model training across decentralized data sources without data transfer. Averaging-based FL is limited by the presence of non-IID data, which negatively impacts convergence speed and final model accuracy. Conventional alternatives suffer from significant inefficiency. Clients with noisy or highly heterogeneous data contribute expensive gradient computations that are either discarded or heavily down-weighted before aggregation. These reactive approaches waste computational resources, require more communication rounds and result in unnecessary privacy exposure. In this paper, we propose a proactive client selection framework that aims to find an optimal federation of clients whose combined data match utility and fairness requirements before training begins. Our method relies on mutual information computed from differentially private contingency tables to quantify the relevance of cross-feature correlations in the union dataset. We introduce a Potential Federation Loss (PFL) over the set of fixed-size federations, which balances two objectives. Maximizing collective data utility while ensuring fair cross-features correlations to prevent group unfairness. Client selection is expressed as an optimal subset search problem over the PFL objective, which we solve using simulated annealing under strong differential privacy guarantees for clients' local statistics. Experimental results on four benchmarks show faster, fairer, and more accurate models trained on optimally found federations, compared to uniform sampling, even when state-of-the-art adaptive aggregation or sampling strategies are employed.

</details>


### [176] [Point Cloud Sequence Encoding for Material-conditioned Graph Network Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20978)
*Philipp Dahlinger,Balázs Gyenes,Niklas Freymuth,Luca Geminiani,Tobias Würth,Johannes Mitsch,Nadja Klein,Luise Kärger,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PEACH框架，通过在点云上进行上下文学习，使图网络模拟器（GNS）能够在推理时适应未知的物理属性，无需依赖显式的材料参数或网格结构，从而实现了高效的零样本仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图网络模拟器（GNS）作为复杂物理仿真的代理模型，虽然具有可微性和显著的速度优势，但通常假设已知底层的材料参数（如刚度或粘度）。在现实实验环境中获取这些参数非常困难。虽然现有的元学习方法尝试从网格轨迹推断属性，但从观测场景重建网格本身极具挑战性，限制了GNS在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出了Point Cloud Encoding for Accurate Context Handling (PEACH)框架。该方法的核心包括：(1) 一个新颖的时空点云序列编码器，用于处理点云数据；(2) 引入两种形式的辅助监督信号以提升仿真保真度；(3) 采用上下文学习（in-context learning）策略，使模型能够在推理阶段适应未见过的物理属性，无需显式推断材料参数。

Result: 在具有挑战性的动态场景实验中，PEACH成功实现了精确的零样本仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移。在仿真场景的测试中，PEACH在预测精度上甚至超越了基于网格的基线方法，同时在实际部署中具有更强的实用性和可行性。

Conclusion: PEACH框架有效地解决了GNS对已知材料参数的依赖问题，通过点云表示和上下文学习实现了对未知物理属性的快速适应。该方法不仅在预测精度上优于传统的网格基线，还显著提升了在真实世界场景中的部署便利性，为数据驱动的物理仿真提供了更加实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Network Simulators (GNSs) have emerged as powerful surrogates for complex physics-based simulation, offering inherent differentiability and orders-of-magnitude speedups over traditional solvers. However, GNSs typically assume access to the underlying material parameters, such as stiffness or viscosity, severely limiting their utility in realistic experimental settings. While recent meta-learning approaches address the parameter dependency by inferring properties from mesh trajectories, reconstructing a mesh from an observed scene is challenging. In this work, we introduce Point Cloud Encoding for Accurate Context Handling (PEACH), a novel framework that applies in-context learning on point clouds to adapt a learned simulator to unseen physical properties during inference. Our approach relies on a novel spatio-temporal point cloud sequence encoder, as well as two forms of auxiliary supervision to help improve simulation fidelity. We demonstrate that PEACH is capable of accurate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a challenging, dynamic scene. Experiments on simulation scenes show that PEACH even outperforms mesh-based baselines on prediction accuracy, while being much more practical for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [177] [Modeling Temporal scRNA-seq Data with Latent Gaussian Process and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20989)
*Mehmet Yigit Balik,Harri Lähdesmäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯过程和最优运输的生成框架，用于从单细胞RNA测序的静态快照数据中推断动态生物过程，在插值、外推和扰动轨迹预测任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞RNA测序技术虽然能够以单细胞分辨率测量基因表达，但只能提供静态快照数据，无法直接观测细胞随时间的动态变化过程。现有基于神经微分方程和流模型的方法容易过拟合，且未能充分考虑生物变异性，因此需要一种能够从静态数据中准确推断时间动态过程的方法。

Method: 提出一个生成框架，使用潜在异方差高斯过程（GP）并通过Hilbert空间方法进行近似来建模群体趋势。为解决缺乏真实细胞轨迹数据的问题，采用最优运输（OT）目标函数来对齐生成分布和观测分布。方法通过引入细胞特异性潜在时间和细胞类型条件，显式捕获生物异质性，从而解耦时间异步性和不同细胞类型的轨迹。

Result: 在复杂的插值和外推基准测试中达到了最先进的性能表现，并引入了一种新颖的基于梯度的策略来推断扰动轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了从静态单细胞数据推断动态过程的关键挑战，通过显式建模生物异质性和利用最优运输理论，为理解细胞动态过程和预测扰动效应提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing provides insights into gene expression at single-cell resolution, yet inferring temporal processes from these static snapshot measurements remains a fundamental challenge. Current approaches utilizing neural differential equations and flows are sensitive to overfitting and lack careful considerations of biological variability. In this work, we propose a generative framework that models population trends using a latent heteroscedastic Gaussian process (GP) approximated by Hilbert space methods. To address the absence of genuine cell trajectories, we leverage an optimal transport (OT) objective that aligns generated and observed population distributions. Our method explicitly captures biological heterogeneity by incorporating cell-specific latent time and cell type conditioning to disentangle temporal asynchrony and trajectories to different cell types. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on complex interpolation and extrapolation benchmarks and introduce a novel gradient-based strategy for inferring perturbation trajectories.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond the Bellman Recursion: A Pontryagin-Guided Framework for Non-Exponential Discounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20996)
*Hojin Ko,Jeonggyu Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PG-DPO框架，通过结合庞特里亚金最大值原理与蒙特卡洛采样，成功解决了非指数折扣下传统贝尔曼递归失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于价值函数和演员-评论家的强化学习方法依赖贝尔曼递归，但这种递归在人类偏好和生存过程中常见的非指数折扣情况下会失效。本文揭示了这种失效的结构性原因：指数折扣处于乘法性和时间齐次性的脆弱交汇点，违反任一性质都会破坏标准动态规划方法。

Method: 提出Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO)变分框架，放弃递归方法，通过伴随-蒙特卡洛投影(Adjoint-MC projection)将庞特里亚金最大值原理与蒙特卡洛轨迹相结合，强制执行逐点哈密顿量最大化。

Result: 在多维双曲折扣和生存折扣基准测试中，PG-DPO在方程驱动求解器和基于评论家的基线方法出现发散的情况下，提高了准确性和稳定性。

Conclusion: PG-DPO成功克服了非指数折扣下传统强化学习方法的局限性，为处理符合人类偏好和生存过程的非标准折扣问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Most value-based and actor--critic reinforcement learning methods rely on Bellman-style recursions, yet these recursions collapse under non-exponential discounting common in human preferences and survival processes. We show the breakdown is structural: exponential discounting sits at a fragile intersection of multiplicativity and time homogeneity, and violating either property breaks standard dynamic programming. To overcome this, we propose Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO), a variational framework that abandons recursion and couples the Pontryagin Maximum Principle with Monte Carlo rollouts via an Adjoint-MC projection enforcing pointwise Hamiltonian maximization. Across multi-dimensional hyperbolic and survival-discount benchmarks, PG-DPO improves accuracy and stability where equation-driven solvers and critic-based baselines diverge.

</details>


### [179] [Efficient Banzhaf-Based Data Valuation for $k$-Nearest Neighbors Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21033)
*Guangyi Zhang,Lutz Oettershagen,Lixu Wang,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对k近邻分类器，提出了高效的Banzhaf值计算算法，通过动态规划将时间复杂度从指数级降至伪多项式甚至线性级别，有效解决了数据估值中的计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据估值旨在量化单个数据点对模型性能的贡献。博弈论方法如Banzhaf值虽提供了公平的估值框架，但面临指数级计算复杂度的瓶颈，难以在实际中应用，因此亟需高效的计算方法。

Method: 作者首先证明了该问题在理论上属于#P难问题。随后利用kNN分类器的局部性特性，提出了动态规划框架：针对加权kNN提出了时间复杂度为O(Wkn²)的伪多项式算法；针对无加权kNN提出了时间复杂度为O(nk²)的专用算法（线性于数据点数量）。此外还提出了高效的蒙特卡洛估计方法。

Result: 理论分析证明了问题的#P难性质。所提算法在保证精确性的同时大幅降低了计算复杂度，特别是无加权kNN算法实现了线性时间复杂度。在真实数据集上的广泛实验验证了方法的实际高效性及其在数据估值应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 尽管Banzhaf值计算在理论上具有高计算难度，但通过挖掘kNN分类器的结构特性，可以设计出高效的精确算法和估计方法，为数据估值任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Data valuation, the task of quantifying the contribution of individual data points to model performance, has emerged as a fundamental challenge in machine learning. Game-theoretic approaches, such as the Banzhaf value, offer principled frameworks for fair data valuation; however, they suffer from exponential computational complexity. We address this challenge by developing efficient algorithms specifically tailored for computing Banzhaf values in $k$-nearest neighbor ($k$NN) classifiers. We first establish the theoretical hardness of the problem by proving that it is \#P-hard. Despite this intractability, we exploit the locality properties of $k$NN classifiers to develop practical exact algorithms. Our main contribution is a dynamic programming framework that achieves significant computational improvements: we present a pseudo-polynomial algorithm with $O(Wkn^2)$ time complexity for weighted $k$NN classifiers, where $W$ is the maximum sum of top-$k$ weights, and a specialized algorithm for unweighted $k$NN that achieves $O(nk^2)$ time complexity, that is, linear in the number of data points. We also offer efficient Monte Carlo estimation methods. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the practical efficiency of our approach and its effectiveness in data valuation applications.

</details>


### [180] [A Dialogue between Causal and Traditional Representation Learning: Toward Mutual Benefits in a Unified Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21058)
*Yan Li,Yuewen Sun,Shaoan Xie,Gongxu Luo,Yunlong Deng,Kun Zhang,Guangyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架连接因果表征学习与传统表征学习，通过任务组件和约束组件的组合实验发现因果约束的有效性高度依赖于其配对任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表征学习与传统表征学习发展轨迹分离：前者侧重理论可识别性，后者侧重应用与经验目标。这种差异导致两领域在术语、问题表述和评估方面存在鸿沟，限制了交流并造成研究脱节或冗余。

Method: 提出统一表述框架，将表征学习分解为两个组件：任务组件（规定学习表征需保留的信息）和约束组件（规定潜在空间施加的结构）。在此基础上实验研究不同任务组件下因果方法的行为表现。

Result: 在CausalVerse上的实验表明，因果约束的有效性强烈依赖于与之配对的任务，不同任务与约束的组合产生显著不同的效果。

Conclusion: 因果表征学习与传统表征学习应被视为对话关系而非分离范式。CRL为理解结构化潜在约束的必要性提供理论工具，传统表征学习为任务设计和目标选择提供实践见解，两者优势互补可推动领域共同发展。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) and traditional representation learning have largely developed along different trajectories. Traditional representation learning has been driven mainly by applications and empirical objectives, whereas CRL has focused more on theoretical questions, particularly identifiability. This difference in emphasis has created a gap between the two fields in terminology, problem formulation, and evaluation, limiting communication and sometimes leading to disconnected or redundant efforts. In this paper, we argue that these two fields should be brought into dialogue rather than treated as separate paradigms. To this end, we introduce a unified formulation in which the representation learning is characterized by two components: a task component, which specifies what information the learned representation is required to preserve, and a constraint component, which specifies what structure is imposed on the latent space. Under this formulation, the benefits run in both directions. CRL provides theoretical tools for understanding when structured latent constraints are useful or necessary, while traditional representation learning offers practical insights on task design and objective choice that can improve the development of CRL methods. To illustrate this interaction, we experimentally study how different task components affect the behavior of CRL methods under different structured constraints.
