Results on CausalVerse show that the effectiveness of causal constraints depends strongly on the tasks with which they are paired.

</details>


### [181] [Robust Personalized Recommendation under Hidden Confounding in MNAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21066)
*Zongyu Li,Wanting Su,Tianyu Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PUID框架，通过估计用户-项目级别的敏感性边界来解决推荐系统中未观测混淆变量的问题，避免了传统RCT实验的高成本和全局敏感性边界方法的局限性，在三个真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统依赖的观测数据存在选择偏差，现有方法（逆倾向权重、双稳健估计器）在存在隐藏混淆变量时失效。传统RCT方法成本高昂，而全局敏感性边界方法假设未观测混淆变量对倾向的影响具有均匀边界，忽略了用户-项目交互的异质性。

Method: 提出个性化未观测混淆感知交互去混淆器（PUID）框架，估计用户-项目级别的敏感性边界，放宽了全局敏感性边界中隐含的均匀性假设。为保证鲁棒性和预测准确性，开发了对抗优化策略，并提出基准引导变体（BPUID），利用预训练模型作为稳定参考。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上进行的大量实验表明，该方法在存在隐藏混淆变量的情况下显著优于全局方法，且无需RCT数据。

Conclusion: PUID框架成功解决了推荐系统中隐藏混淆变量的问题，通过个性化敏感性边界克服了现有方法的局限性，为实际推荐系统提供了一种无需RCT数据的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recommender systems often rely on observational user--item interaction data, which is prone to selection bias due to users' selective interactions with items. Inverse propensity weighting and doubly robust estimators effectively mitigate selection bias under observed confounding, but are unreliable in the presence of hidden confounders. Existing approaches relying on randomized controlled trials (RCTs) or global sensitivity bounds are constrained in practice: RCTs demand costly experimental data, while global sensitivity bounds presume a uniformly bounded effect of unmeasured confounders on propensities through sensitivity analysis, thereby neglecting heterogeneity across user--item interactions. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel framework, which estimates user--item level sensitivity bounds, thereby substantially relaxing the homogeneity assumption inherent in global sensitivity bounds named Personalized Unobserved-Confounding-aware Interaction Deconfounder (PUID). To ensure both robustness and predictive accuracy, we further develop an adversarial optimization strategy and propose a benchmark-guided variant (BPUID) that incorporates pre-trained models as stabilizing references. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms global methods under hidden confounding, without requiring RCT data.

</details>


### [182] [Towards Understanding Self-Pretraining for Sequence Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21070)
*Omar Coser,Loredana Zollo,Paolo Soda,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统消融实验揭示了自预训练(SPT)提升Transformer性能的机制：标签监督难以从随机初始化学习有效的注意力模式，而掩码重建通过引入邻近性偏置克服了这一瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Amos等人(2024)发现自预训练(SPT)能显著提升Transformer在序列分类任务上的性能，但其背后的优化机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究：(1)SPT如何驱动优化过程找到更优解？(2)为何标准监督训练会在Transformer中失败？

Method: 复现Amos等人(2024)的实验结果并进行系统性消融研究。通过最小化实验设置，分析SPT各个组件的贡献，并在简化理论框架下分析标签监督与掩码重建的差异。

Result: 发现核心瓶颈在于标签监督难以从随机初始化学习有用的查询-键注意力模式。识别出SPT的关键改进来源：学习邻近性交互，即将绝对位置编码转化为具有邻近性偏置的注意力分数。理论分析表明标签监督对某些注意力分数方向存在局部盲区，而掩码重建能够检测到这些方向。

Conclusion: SPT的有效性源于其能通过掩码重建目标学习标签监督无法有效捕获的注意力模式，特别是通过引入邻近性偏置来克服标准监督训练在注意力模式学习方面的局限性。

Abstract: Amos et al. (2024) showed that the accuracy of Transformer models in sequence classification can be significantly improved by first pretraining with a masked token prediction objective without external data or augmentation, a procedure referred to as self-pretraining (SPT). While the primary objective of Amos et al. (2024) was to showcase that Transformers can achieve strong performance on the Long-Range Arena (LRA), their pipeline raises more fundamental questions: How does SPT drive optimization to better solutions? Why can standard supervised training fail in Transformers? To better understand this, we replicate and systematically ablate the findings of Amos et al. (2024). Our ablations suggest that a central bottleneck in the studied settings is not depth or generalization alone, but the ability of label supervision to learn useful query-key Attention patterns from random initialization. With a minimal setup, we identify learning proximity interactions - turning absolute positional encodings into proximity-biased Attention scores - as a key source of the improvements brought by SPT. Finally, in a simplified theoretical setup, we show that label supervision can be locally blind to certain Attention-score directions that are instead detectable through masked reconstruction.

</details>


### [183] [Reviving Error Correction in Modern Deep Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21088)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Kien Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种与架构无关的通用误差校正器(UEC-STD)，通过季节-趋势分解来缓解深度时间序列预测中的自回归误差累积问题，在无需重新训练原有模型的情况下显著提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习模型在长期时间序列预测中存在自回归推理导致的误差累积问题，而传统的误差校正机制在深度学习模型中的应用仍受限或无效，亟需研究如何在新背景下有效应用误差校正机制。

Method: 提出通用误差校正器(UEC-STD)，将预测显式分解为趋势和季节分量，分别对每个分量进行校正调整。该方法具有架构无关性，可与任何现有预测模型集成且无需重新训练原模型。

Result: 在4种骨干网络和10个数据集上的实验表明，UEC-STD显著提高了校正精度和鲁棒性，验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为增强时间序列预测提供了实用工具，同时为缓解深度时间序列模型中的自回归误差提供了新的见解和方法。

Abstract: Modern deep-learning models have achieved remarkable success in time-series forecasting. Yet, their performance degrades in long-term prediction due to error accumulation in autoregressive inference, where predictions are recursively used as inputs. While classical error correction mechanisms (ECMs) have long been used in statistical methods, their applicability to deep learning models remains limited or ineffective. In this work, we revisit the error accumulation problem in deep time-series forecasting and investigate the role and necessity of ECMs in this new context. We propose a simple, architecture-agnostic error correction model that can be integrated with any existing forecaster without requiring retraining. By explicitly decomposing predictions into trend and seasonal components and training the corrector to adjust each separately, we introduce the Universal Error Corrector with Seasonal-Trend Decomposition (UEC-STD), which significantly improves correction accuracy and robustness across 4 backbones and 10 datasets. Our findings provide a practical tool for enhancing forecasts while offering new insights into mitigating autoregressive errors in deep time-series models. Code is available at https://github.com/DA2I2-SLM/UEC-STD.

</details>


### [184] [UOTIP: Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Unpaired Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21094)
*Donggyu Lee,Taekyung Lee,Jaewoong Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UOTIP，一种基于非平衡最优传输的非配对图像逆问题求解器，通过松弛边际约束在多种噪声场景下实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非配对图像逆问题这一挑战性设置（训练数据中仅有独立的噪声测量集合和干净目标信号集合，缺乏成对数据），现有方法难以处理多级噪声、类别不平衡和多样化噪声类型等问题，需要一种更鲁棒、适应性更强的解决方案。

Method: 提出非平衡最优传输逆问题求解器（UOTIP），将重建任务（从噪声测量预测干净信号）表述为学习从噪声测量分布到干净信号分布的非平衡最优传输映射。通过引入基于似然的代价函数，并松弛精确边际约束，使模型具备对多级观测噪声的鲁棒性、对类别不平衡的适应性以及对多样化噪声场景的泛化能力。理论上证明引入二次代价项可通过满足扭曲条件确保传输映射的存在性和唯一性。

Result: UOTIP在非配对图像逆问题基准测试上取得了最先进的性能，实验涵盖线性和非线性逆问题，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 非平衡最优传输框架为非配对逆问题提供了一种有效解决方案，通过松弛边际约束和引入适当的代价函数，在保证理论性质的同时实现了优异的实证性能，为处理现实世界中缺乏成对训练数据的逆问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: We investigate unpaired image inverse problems, a challenging setting where only independent, non-paired sets of noisy measurements and clean target signals are available for training. We propose a novel inverse problem solver based on Unbalanced Optimal Transport, called Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Inverse Problems (UOTIP). Our method formulates the reconstruction task, predicting clean target signals from noisy measurements, as learning a UOT Map from noisy measurement distribution to clean signal distribution by incorporating a likelihood-based cost function. By relaxing the exact marginal constraint, the UOT framework provides key advantages to our model: robustness to multi-level observation noise, adaptability to class imbalance between noisy and clean datasets, and generalizability to diverse noise-type scenarios. Furthermore, we theoretically demonstrate that incorporating a quadratic cost term ensures the existence and uniqueness of the transport map by satisfying the twist condition, even for ill-posed inverse problems. Our experiments demonstrate that UOTIP achieves state-of-the-art performance on unpaired image inverse problem benchmarks, across linear and nonlinear inverse problems.

</details>


### [185] [A Typed Tensor Language for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21103)
*Theofilos Mailis,Kalliopi-Christina Despotidou,Konstantinos Filippopolitis,Yannis Foufoulas,Thanasis-Michail Karampatsis,Andreas Ktenidis,Evdokia Mailli,Theodore Papamarkou,Yannis Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个类型化张量语言，形式化描述联邦学习与分析的统一结构，证明了单轮程序可通过固定维度的共享状态进行分解，并建立了与可表达分解的双射关系，从而形式化了编码-合并-解码的计算范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习与分析通常被视为独立协议的集合，尽管它们共享相同的数学形式（客户端本地张量计算、可合并聚合、共享后处理）。现有研究缺乏对这种统一结构的正式描述，需要一种形式化框架来刻画这类计算的共同本质和表达能力边界。

Method: 引入类型化张量语言，区分联邦张量（记录沿被跟踪记录轴跨客户端分区）和共享张量（全局可用）。通过虚拟全局张量定义语义，建立共享状态分解理论，证明类型化单轮程序可分解为独立于客户端和记录数量的固定维度共享状态。同时证明逆表示性结果，并扩展至迭代程序。开发了可微分片段用于学习，将全局梯度表示为联邦梯度张量的记录轴求和。

Result: 证明了类型化单轮程序通过固定维度共享状态的分解定理，该状态大小独立于客户端和记录数量，可从客户端本地张量表达式计算并跨客户端合并。证明了逆表示性结果：语言中可表达的编码器和解码器分解可由类型化单轮程序实现。将对应关系扩展至跨轮状态为共享的迭代程序。对于可微分片段，证明如果逐记录损失及其梯度由客户端本地张量表达式表示，则全局梯度由联邦梯度张量的记录轴求和表示。

Conclusion: 该框架形式化刻画了一类广泛的联邦学习计算，其通信通过固定维度共享状态进行，为服务器端梯度下降和共享线性代数二阶更新提供了类型化迭代程序，建立了编码-合并-解码计算范式的理论基础。

Abstract: Federated learning and analytics are often described as collections of separate protocols, even when they share the same mathematical form: client-local tensor computation, mergeable aggregation into shared state, and shared-only post-processing. We introduce a typed tensor language that formalizes this structure. The language distinguishes federated tensors, whose records are partitioned across clients along a tracked record axis, from shared tensors, which are available globally. Its semantics are defined by comparison with a virtual global tensor, used only as a reference object. The main result is a shared-state factorization theory. We show that typed one-round programs factor through fixed-dimensional shared state whose size is independent of the number of clients and records, computed from client-local tensor expressions and merged across clients. We also prove a converse representability result; factorizations whose encoders and decoders are expressible in the language are realized by typed one-round programs, and the correspondence extends to iterative programs whose cross-round state is shared. This gives a formal account of the computations in the language that can be expressed as encode, merge, and decode procedures. We then develop a differentiable fragment for learning. If a per-record loss and its per-record gradient are represented by client-local tensor expressions, the global gradient is represented by record-axis summation of the federated gradient tensor. This yields typed iterative programs for server-side gradient descent and shared-linear-algebra second-order updates. The framework characterizes a broad class of federated learning computations whose communication passes through fixed-dimensional shared state.

</details>


### [186] [HORST: Composing Optimizer Geometries for Sparse Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21104)
*Tom Jacobs,Rohan Jain,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HORST优化器，通过组合算子将自适应方法的稳定性与双曲镜映射的L1稀疏性偏置结合，显著提升了Transformer稀疏训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准优化器无法同时促进稀疏性和保持训练稳定性。有效的自适应优化器表现出有利于稳定性的隐式L∞偏置，但稀疏性需要L1偏置，二者存在矛盾。

Method: 将优化器步骤组合建模为非交换算子，以原则性方式分析和组合其优化几何。提出HORST优化器，通过双曲镜映射诱导L1稀疏性偏置，同时继承自适应方法的稳定性。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务的Transformer稀疏训练实验中，HORST在所有稀疏度水平上均显著优于AdamW基线，在高稀疏度下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: HORST成功解决了Transformer稀疏训练中稳定性与稀疏性之间的矛盾，为稀疏训练提供了一种有效的模块化优化器解决方案。

Abstract: Sparsifying transformers remains a fundamental challenge, as standard optimizers fail to simultaneously encourage sparsity and maintain training stability. Effective adaptive optimizers exhibit an implicit $L_{\infty}$ bias favoring stability, yet, sparsity requires an $L_1$ bias. To integrate sparsity, we propose a composition of optimizer steps, which we cast as non-commutative operators to analyze and combine their optimization geometry in a principled way. This yields HORST (Hyperbolic Operator for Robust Sparse Training), a modular optimizer that inherits stability from adaptive methods while inducing $L_1$ sparsity bias through a hyperbolic mirror map. Our experiments demonstrate its utility for sparse training of transformers on both vision and language tasks. HORST consistently and significantly outperforms AdamW baselines across all sparsity levels, with large gains at higher sparsity.

</details>


### [187] [Improved Guarantees for Constrained Online Convex Optimization via Self-Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21107)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的投影算法，在约束在线凸优化中实现了显著改进：对于强凸损失，同时达到O(log T)遗憾和O(log T)累积约束违反（CCV），相比已有结果在CCV上实现了指数级提升；对于凸损失，在保持最优O(√T)遗憾的同时将CCV改进至O(√T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有对抗性约束的约束在线凸优化（COCO）问题，目标是在实现较小静态遗憾的同时控制累积约束违反（CCV）。现有最优算法对于强凸损失达到O(log T)遗憾和O(√T log T) CCV，对于凸损失达到O(√T)遗憾和O(√T log T) CCV，CCV边界仍有较大改进空间。

Method: 提出一种简单的基于投影的算法，关键技术突破在于运用了关于自收缩曲线的最新几何结果，这一结果可能具有独立的研究价值。

Result: 对于强凸损失，算法同时达到O(log T)遗憾和O(log T) CCV，在CCV上相比现有结果实现指数级改进；对于凸损失，在保持最优O(√T)遗憾的同时将CCV改进至O(√T)。

Conclusion: 该研究在约束在线凸优化领域取得了重要突破，特别是对强凸损失情形实现了CCV的指数级改进，且算法设计简洁有效，所使用的自收缩曲线几何技术具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We consider Constrained Online Convex Optimization (COCO) with adversarially chosen constraints. At each round, the learner chooses an action before observing the loss and constraint function for that round. The goal is to achieve small static regret against the best point satisfying all constraints while also controlling cumulative constraint violation ($\mathsf{CCV}$). For strongly convex losses, state-of-the-art algorithms achieve $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T \log T})$ $\mathsf{CCV}.$ The corresponding best-known bounds for convex losses is $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$. In this paper, we give a simple projection-based algorithm that simultaneously achieves $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$ for strongly-convex losses, yielding an exponential improvement in the $\mathsf{CCV}$. For the convex losses, our algorithm improves the $\mathsf{CCV}$ to $O(\sqrt{T})$ while maintaining the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret. The key to our improvement is a recent geometric result for self-contracted curves, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [188] [A Unified Framework for Uncertainty-Aware Explainable Artificial Intelligence: A Case Study in Power Quality Disturbance Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21114)
*Yinsong Chen,Samson S. Yu,Zhong Li,Chee Peng Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了不确定性感知相关性归因算子（UA-RAO）框架，用于量化贝叶斯神经网络中的解释分布，通过均值、方差等统计量捕捉归因的不确定性，在电能质量扰动分类任务上验证了其有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后 hoc 可解释AI方法通常产生确定性的归因图，而贝叶斯神经网络会在解释上产生分布。捕捉这种分布的变异性对于不确定性感知决策至关重要，但目前缺乏系统的方法来量化和利用这种解释不确定性。

Method: 1. 将解释分布形式化为BNN后验通过Lipschitz连续归因算子的推进测度；2. 提出不确定性感知相关性归因算子（UA-RAO）框架，使用均值、方差、变异系数、分位数和集合论聚合度量来总结解释分布；3. 提供Monte Carlo可达性和Wasserstein近似界的理论支持；4. 在15类电能质量扰动分类基准上评估，比较三种BNN近似方法与三种归因算子的组合。

Result: 1. 深度集成方法配合均值UA-RAO相比确定性基线提高了定位性能；2. 其他UA-RAO摘要揭示了点估计归因中不存在的不确定性模式；3. 在实测信号上的定性结果表明这些模式能够泛化到合成训练分布之外；4. 使用相关性质量精度和交并比作为定位度量进行量化评估。

Conclusion: 该框架是一个领域无关的方法，可以应用于任何BNN与Lipschitz连续归因算子的组合，为不确定性感知决策提供了系统性的解释不确定性量化工具，在理论上有Monte Carlo可达性和Wasserstein近似界的保证，在实验上证明了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Post-hoc explainable AI (XAI) methods typically produce deterministic attribution maps, whereas Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) induce a distribution over explanations. Capturing the variability of this distribution is important for uncertainty-aware decision-making. This paper formalises the \emph{explanation distribution} as the push-forward measure of the BNN posterior through any Lipschitz-continuous attribution operator. It further proposes the uncertainty-aware relevance attribution operator (UA-RAO), a general family of operators that summarises the explanation distribution using the mean, variance, coefficient of variation, quantiles, and set-theoretic aggregation measures. Theoretical support is provided through Monte Carlo accessibility and Wasserstein approximation bounds. The framework is evaluated on a 15-class power quality disturbance (PQD) classification benchmark, comparing three BNN approximations paired with three attribution operators using relevance mass accuracy and intersection-over-union as localisation metrics. Results show that deep ensembles with the mean UA-RAO improve localisation over the deterministic baseline, while other UA-RAO summaries reveal uncertainty patterns absent from point-estimate attributions. Qualitative results on measured signals further suggest that these patterns generalise beyond the synthetic training distribution. The framework is domain-agnostic and can be applied to any BNN paired with a Lipschitz-continuous attribution operator.

</details>


### [189] [Advantage Collapse in Group Relative Policy Optimization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21125)
*Xixiang He,Qiyao Sun,Ao Cheng,Xingming Li,Xuanyu Ji,Hailun Lu,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了优势崩塌率(ACR)指标来诊断GRPO算法中的优势崩塌问题，并提出AVSPO算法通过注入虚拟奖励样本来解决该问题，在数学推理任务上显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO算法在强化学习框架中表现优异，但存在优势崩塌问题：当组内奖励同质化时（全部正确或全部错误），会导致优势值接近零和梯度消失，严重影响训练效果。目前缺乏对该问题的量化诊断和有效解决方案。

Method: 提出优势崩塌率(ACR)作为诊断指标，量化训练批次中无效梯度的比例；基于ACR设计自适应虚拟样本策略优化(AVSPO)算法，通过实时ACR监控引导虚拟奖励样本注入，使模型能够从同质化组中学习，且无需额外的模型展开。

Result: ACR能够有效预测训练停滞和最终性能；在0.5B到14B参数规模的数学推理基准测试中，AVSPO相比GRPO将优势崩塌率降低58-63%，准确率提升4-6个百分点，同时在域外任务上保持泛化能力。

Conclusion: ACR为GRPO的优势崩塌问题提供了可靠的诊断工具，AVSPO作为轻量级扩展算法有效解决了该问题，在多个模型规模上取得显著性能提升，为强化学习框架下的LLM推理能力改进提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a prominent algorithm within the Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) framework, has achieved strong results in improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, GRPO is prone to advantage collapse, a failure mode where homogeneous rewards within a group (e.g., all correct or all incorrect answers) yield near-zero advantages and vanishing gradients. To address this, we introduce the Advantage Collapse Rate (ACR), the first diagnostic metric quantifying the proportion of training batches with ineffective gradients. Across models from 0.5B to 14B parameters on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we show that ACR strongly predicts training stagnation and final performance. We then propose Adaptive Virtual Sample Policy Optimization (AVSPO), a lightweight extension of GRPO that injects virtual reward samples, guided by real-time ACR monitoring, to enable learning from homogeneous groups without additional model rollouts. AVSPO reduces advantage collapse by 58-63% relative to GRPO and yields consistent accuracy gains of 4-6 percentage points across all model scales, while maintaining generalization on the evaluated out-of-domain task. Code and datasets are available at https://qingyonghu.github.io/AVSPO.

</details>


### [190] [Reasoning-Trace Collapse: Evaluating the Loss of Explicit Reasoning During Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21127)
*Lukas Twist,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 显式推理模型在仅包含最终答案的指令数据上微调时，会出现推理链崩塌现象（保持答案正确但丢失有效推理过程）。简单的损失掩码策略可以有效缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式推理模型在下游任务微调时，常使用不包含推理过程的普通指令-响应数据。作者旨在探究这种数据不匹配是否会导致模型丧失其核心的显式推理能力，以及传统的仅评估最终答案的方法是否能有效检测这一问题。

Method: 1. 提出一个结构化评估框架，将答案正确性与推理链有效性分离，定义了有效、空缺、缺失和截断四种推理状态，并测量条件于有效推理的任务性能。
2. 对四个开源权重推理模型进行标准监督微调（SFT）实验。
3. 测试简单的损失掩码（loss-masking）策略能否在不依赖教师模型生成推理轨迹的情况下缓解崩塌。

Result: 1. 标准监督微调会迅速抑制有效的推理轨迹，导致推理链崩塌。
2. 仅关注最终答案的指标会严重掩盖这一失败：在多个设置中，尽管有效推理率急剧下降，但条件于有效推理的性能仍保持较高水平。
3. 简单的损失掩码策略可以大幅缓解崩塌，无需教师生成的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 对微调后的推理模型进行评估时，除最终答案性能外，必须报告结构性推理可靠性指标，特别是在适配数据不包含显式推理轨迹的情况下，以确保模型保留了其推理能力。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning models are trained to produce intermediate reasoning traces before final answers, but downstream fine-tuning is often performed on ordinary instruction-response data that contains no such traces. We show that this mismatch can induce reasoning-trace collapse: a fine-tuned model continues to produce plausible final answers while losing the structurally valid explicit reasoning traces that made it a reasoning model in the first place. We introduce a structural evaluation framework that separates answer correctness from reasoning-trace validity, measuring valid, empty, missing, and truncated reasoning alongside reasoning-conditioned task performance. Using this framework, we study four open-weight reasoning models and find that standard supervised fine-tuning can rapidly suppress valid reasoning traces, and that answer-only metrics can substantially obscure this failure: in several settings, performance conditional on valid reasoning remains high while the rate of valid reasoning falls sharply. We further show that simple loss-masking strategies can substantially mitigate collapse without requiring teacher-generated reasoning traces. These results suggest that evaluations of fine-tuned reasoning models should report structural reasoning reliability metrics in addition to final-answer performance, especially when adaptation data does not contain explicit reasoning traces.

</details>


### [191] [SMoA: Spectrum Modulation Adapter for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21147)
*Yongkang Liu,Xing Li,Mengjie Zhao,Shanru Zhang,Zijing Wang,Qian Li,Shi Feng,Feiliang Ren,Daling Wang,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMoA（频谱调制适配器），通过频谱分块和Hadamard调制在低参数预算下增强LoRA的表示能力，在多个任务上优于LoRA及其变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法存在秩与表示能力的权衡问题：低秩限制了表示容量，理论上LoRA只能收敛到预训练权重矩阵的前r个奇异值方向；提高秩虽能改善性能但会增加可训练参数和计算成本。

Method: SMoA将层划分为多个对齐的频谱块，对每个对角块应用一个块内Hadamard调制的低秩分支，从而在较小的参数预算下扩展频谱感知更新的可访问族，实现对预训练频谱方向的更广覆盖。

Result: 理论分析证明SMoA能够覆盖更多预训练频谱方向。在多个任务的实验中，SMoA在当前低预算设置下的平均性能优于LoRA及竞争性LoRA类基线方法。

Conclusion: SMoA成功解决了LoRA在低秩设置下的表示能力限制问题，通过频谱分块和调制机制实现了在有限参数预算下的性能提升，为参数高效微调提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: As the number of model parameters increases, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become the go-to choice for tailoring pre-trained large language models. Low-rank Adaptation (LoRA) uses a low-rank update method to simulate full parameter fine-tuning, which is widely used to reduce resource requirements. However, decreasing the rank encounters challenges with limited representational capacity. Theory suggests that LoRA fine-tuning with rank r converges toward the top r singular values of the pre-trained weight matrix. As the rank increases, more principal singular directions are preserved, which generally improves the model's performance. However, a larger rank also introduces more trainable parameters, leading to higher computational cost. To overcome this dilemma, we propose SMoA, a \textbf{S}pectrum \textbf{Mo}dulation \textbf{A}dapter that enlarges the accessible family of spectrum-aware updates under a smaller parameter budget. SMoA partitions the layer into multiple aligned spectral blocks and applies one in-block Hadamard-modulated low-rank branch to each diagonal block, yielding broader coverage of pretrained spectral directions. We provide theoretical analysis and empirical results on multiple tasks. In our experiments, SMoA improves average performance in the current lower-budget setting over LoRA and competitive LoRA-style baselines.

</details>


### [192] [Learning First Integrals via Backward-Generated Data and Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21160)
*Jingfeng Zhong,Zhengxiang Liu,Zhijie Wang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FISolver，一个基于大语言模型的首次积分求解器。通过反向生成算法构建大规模数据集缓解数据稀缺问题，结合监督微调和强化学习，在较低计算成本下显著优于大型数学LLM和Mathematica等商业求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现首次积分对于理解动力系统中的守恒定律至关重要，但现有的符号计算工具和大语言模型在此任务上仍存在局限性，主要瓶颈在于高质量训练数据的稀缺以及成功求解往往依赖于数学直觉。

Method: 1. 提出“反向生成”算法，通过从采样的积分反向推导微分方程，系统性构建大规模（微分方程，首次积分）数据集，缓解数据稀缺问题；2. 对紧凑的数学模型进行监督微调，并通过基于Levenshtein距离的塑形奖励进行强化学习以提升性能；3. 设计数据合成和混合策略，支持从稀疏样本有效适应困难问题族。

Result: 实验表明，FISolver在显著降低计算成本的同时，在具有挑战性的基准测试中显著优于更大的数学LLM和Mathematica等商业求解器。

Conclusion: FISolver为首次积分的自动发现开辟了一条新的数据驱动途径，证明了通过创新的数据生成方法和针对性的训练策略，紧凑模型可以在特定数学问题上超越通用大模型和传统商业工具。

Abstract: The discovery of first integrals is of fundamental scientific importance for understanding conservation laws in dynamical systems. However, existing symbolic computation tools and Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited on this task because high-quality training data are scarce and successful solutions often depend on mathematical intuition. This paper presents FISolver, an LLM-based solver developed to address this challenge. First, we introduce a "Backward Generation" algorithm that systematically builds large-scale datasets of (differential equation, first integral) pairs by deriving differential equations from sampled integrals, thereby alleviating the data scarcity bottleneck. Second, we apply supervised fine-tuning to a compact mathematical model and further improve its performance through reinforcement learning with a Levenshtein Distance-based shaped reward. In addition, we design data synthesis and blending strategies that support effective adaptation to difficult problem families from sparse examples. Experiments show that FISolver, while requiring substantially lower computational cost, significantly outperforms larger mathematical LLMs and commercial solvers such as Mathematica on challenging benchmarks, indicating a new data-driven route for automated discovery of first integrals.

</details>


### [193] [Q-SYNTH: Hybrid Quantum-Classical Adversarial Augmentation for Imbalanced Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21164)
*Adam Innan,Mansour El Alami,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique,Mohamed Bennai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-SYNTH框架，利用参数化量子电路作为生成器、经典神经网络作为判别器，生成少数类欺诈样本，在分布保真度和下游欺诈检测性能之间取得了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈检测面临极端类别不平衡问题，欺诈交易稀少但至关重要。这种不平衡导致监督学习模型偏向合法交易类别，虽然整体准确率高，但欺诈类别的召回率和F1分数较低。

Method: 提出Q-SYNTH混合经典-量子生成对抗框架，其中参数化量子电路作为生成器，经典神经网络作为判别器，专门用于表格数据中的少数类欺诈样本合成。评估包括两个维度：与真实欺诈样本的统计保真度，以及下游欺诈检测性能。使用基于Kolmogorov-Smirnov统计和Wasserstein距离的分布相似性度量、AUC-ROC衡量的真假样本可检测性，以及量子和经典分类器的下游分类性能进行评估。

Result: Q-SYNTH相比经典GAN基线减少了边际分布不匹配，同时保持具有竞争力的下游欺诈检测性能。虽然SMOTE在特征相似性方面最强，经典GAN在部分设置中下游性能最高，但Q-SYNTH在分布保真度和下游性能之间提供了有利的折中。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持混合量子增强在不平衡欺诈检测中的可行性，Q-SYNTH为信用卡欺诈检测中的类别不平衡问题提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Credit card fraud detection is fundamentally challenged by extreme class imbalance, where fraudulent transactions are rare yet operationally critical. This imbalance often biases supervised learners toward the legitimate class, leading to high overall accuracy but weaker fraud-class recall and F1-score. This paper introduces Q-SYNTH, a hybrid classical--quantum generative adversarial framework in which a parameterized quantum circuit serves as the generator and a classical neural network serves as the discriminator. Q-SYNTH is designed for minority-class fraud synthesis in tabular data and is evaluated along two dimensions: statistical fidelity to real fraud samples and downstream performance for fraud detection. To this end, generated samples are assessed using distributional similarity measures based on Kolmogorov-Smirnov statistics and Wasserstein distances, real-vs-synthetic detectability measured by AUC-ROC, and downstream classification performance across both quantum and classical classifiers. Under the reported protocol, Q-SYNTH reduces marginal distribution mismatch relative to a classical GAN baseline while maintaining competitive downstream fraud-detection performance. Although SMOTE achieves the strongest feature-wise similarity and the classical GAN attains the highest downstream performance in several settings, Q-SYNTH offers a favorable compromise between distributional fidelity and downstream performance, supporting the feasibility of hybrid quantum augmentation for imbalanced fraud detection.

</details>


### [194] [ChunkFT: Byte-Streamed Optimization for Memory-Efficient Full Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21177)
*Yongkang Liu,Zijing Wang,Mengjie Zhao,Ercong Nie,Mingyang Wang,Qian Li,Feiliang Ren,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChunkFT框架，通过动态激活工作集实现内存高效的全参数微调，在有限GPU资源下实现与全参数微调相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调需要大量GPU内存，限制了在资源受限环境下的应用。现有内存高效方法往往在性能上有所妥协。需要一种既能降低内存消耗，又能保持全参数微调优化质量的方法。

Method: 提出ChunkFT框架，核心思想是将全参数微调重新表述为围绕动态激活工作集进行。该方法能够在不修改网络架构的情况下计算任意子张量的梯度，为优化任意子网络提供了算法基础，同时避免了标准的稠密梯度计算。论文提供了确定性设置下的理论收敛性分析。

Result: 在单张RTX 4090-24GB GPU上成功微调Llama 3-8B，在2×H800-80GB GPU上微调Llama 3-70B。7B模型1K输入长度的全参数微调仅需13.72GB GPU内存。在语言理解、数学推理和MT-Bench等下游任务评测中，ChunkFT持续优于现有内存高效基线方法，性能达到甚至超越全参数微调。

Conclusion: ChunkFT成功实现了内存高效与优化质量的平衡，显著降低了全参数微调的硬件门槛，使大模型微调更加普及可行，为资源受限场景下的大模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents \textsc{ChunkFT}, a memory-efficient fine-tuning framework that reformulates full-parameter fine-tuning around a dynamically activated working set. \textsc{ChunkFT} enables gradient computation for arbitrary sub-tensors without modifying the network architecture, providing an algorithmic foundation for optimizing arbitrary sub-networks while avoiding standard dense gradient computation. We provide a theoretical convergence analysis of \textsc{ChunkFT} in the deterministic setting. Empirically, we apply \textsc{ChunkFT} to fine-tune Llama 3-8B and Llama 3-70B using a single RTX 4090-24GB GPU and 2$\times$ H800-80GB GPUs, respectively. Full-parameter fine-tuning of a 7B model with a 1K input length requires only 13.72GB of GPU memory. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of \textsc{ChunkFT} in memory usage, running time, and optimization quality. Moreover, downstream evaluations on language understanding, mathematical reasoning, and MT-Bench show that \textsc{ChunkFT} consistently outperforms existing memory-efficient baselines. Notably, \textsc{ChunkFT} achieves performance comparable to, and in some cases exceeding, full-parameter fine-tuning. Our repository is on https://github.com/misonsky/chunk.

</details>


### [195] [Domain-Adaptable Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation with Dense Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21180)
*Erfan Aghadavoodi Jolfaei,Daniel Maninger,Abhinav Anand,Mert Tiftikci,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于强化学习的框架，通过近端策略优化（PPO）微调预训练的大语言模型，利用可定制的执行感知奖励机制，显著提升了代码生成在通用编程任务和机器人领域的正确性和领域适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自动化代码生成方面表现出强大潜力，但缺乏对正确性、质量、安全性和领域特定约束的保障。特别是在机器人领域，代码生成需要充分感知环境和物理约束，现有的LLM无法有效满足这些多样化需求。

Method: 提出了一个基于强化学习的微调框架，使用近端策略优化（PPO）算法。设计了可定制的执行感知奖励公式，涵盖语法、功能正确性、代码风格、安全性和模拟器可执行性。引入了token级别的奖励映射机制，能够将执行结果有效分配给生成的token，实现精确的信用分配。

Result: 在通用代码生成数据集MBPP/MBPP+和机器人程序合成数据集RoboEval上进行了评估。MBPP上的pass@1绝对提升19%，RoboEval上的执行失败率降低了51%。功能正确性和模拟器可执行性均获得了显著改善。

Conclusion: 结构化强化学习可以有效地将语言模型与正确的程序生成和领域特定需求进行对齐，证明了该方法在提升代码生成质量和满足领域约束方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models show strong potential for automated code generation, but lack guarantees for correctness, quality, safety, and domain-specific constraints. For instance in robotics, where code generation is increasingly being used for planning and executing actions, awareness of the environment and physical constraints is critical. To facilitate the adaption of code-generating LLMs to diverse requirements, including domain-specific ones, we present a reinforcement learning framework that fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs using proximal policy optimization. Our customizable execution-aware reward formula captures and optimizes syntax, functional correctness, code style, security, and simulator executability. A token-level reward mapping mechanism enables effective credit assignment from execution outcomes to generated tokens. The framework is evaluated on general-purpose code generation (MBPP/MBPP+) and robotic program synthesis (RoboEval). The results show substantial improvements in functional correctness and simulator executability, including an absolute pass@1 increase of 19% on MBPP and a reduction in execution failures by 51% on RoboEval. These findings demonstrate that structured reinforcement learning can effectively align language models to correct program generation and domain-specific requirements.

</details>


### [196] [Divide and Contrast: Learning Robust Temporal Features without Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21241)
*Abdul-Kazeem Shamba,Kerstin Bach,Gavin Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Di-COT，一种无需数据增强的高效时间序列自监督学习框架，通过在窗口内对比子结构而非单个时间步，在显著降低训练成本的同时达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列自监督学习方法通常计算成本高昂，或依赖于在多种时间动态下并不成立的假设，且往往依赖数据增强，限制了其泛化能力与效率。

Method: 提出Divide and Contrast (Di-COT)框架。核心方法是不使用数据增强，而是通过随机将窗口划分为重叠子块，在窗口内部对比这些信息丰富的子结构。同时采用了计算复杂度仅取决于批量大小和子块数量、独立于序列长度的对比目标函数，从而提升扩展性。

Result: 在六个大规模真实数据集以及UCR和UEA基准上进行了广泛实验，Di-COT在分类、聚类、kNN和跨数据集迁移任务上均取得了最先进的性能，并大幅减少了训练时间。

Conclusion: Di-COT通过避免数据增强和多编码器传递，利用窗口内子结构对比实现了高效且有效的时间序列表示学习，证明了语义结构化和可迁移性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning for time-series representation aims to reduce reliance on labeled data while maintaining strong downstream performance, yet many existing approaches incur high computational costs or rely on assumptions that do not hold across diverse temporal dynamics. In this work, we introduce Divide and Contrast (Di-COT), an unsupervised framework that avoids data augmentation and multiple encoder passes by contrasting informative substructures within a window rather than individual timesteps. Di-COT stochastically partitions each window into a small number of overlapping sub-blocks per iteration, enabling efficient and meaningful contrast while mitigating false positives during temporal transitions. To further improve scalability, we adopt a contrastive objective whose computation depends on the batch size and the number of sub-blocks, making loss computation independent of sequence length. Extensive experiments on six large-scale real-world datasets, as well as the UCR and UEA benchmarks, demonstrate that Di-COT learns semantically structured and transferable representations, achieving state-of-the-art performance on classification, clustering, $k$NN, and cross-dataset transfer, while substantially reducing training time. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sfi-norwai/Di-COT.

</details>


### [197] [Graph Navier Stokes Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21247)
*Zexing Zhao,Guangsi Shi,Yu Gong,Tianyu Wang,Shirui Pan,Hongye Cheng,Yuxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受Navier-Stokes方程启发，提出图Navier-Stokes网络（GNSN），通过在图结构中引入对流机制与扩散机制自适应平衡，有效缓解了过平滑问题，并在12个数据集上取得了优于SOTA的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图神经网络主要基于图信号处理和扩散方程进行消息传递，这种机制存在固有的过平滑问题，即随着网络深度增加，节点特征变得难以区分。为了突破传统基于扩散的消息传递的局限性，作者受到Navier-Stokes方程的启发，试图引入对流机制来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的架构——图Navier-Stokes网络（GNSN）。该方法超越了传统的基于扩散的消息传递，通过在图上定义动态速度场来控制对流，将对流机制引入图结构中。GNSN能够自适应地平衡对流和扩散过程，从而实现对不同同质性水平数据集的高效处理。

Result: 在12个真实世界数据集上的广泛评估表明，GNSN在分类准确率上一致优于最先进的基线方法。实验结果进一步强调了其在缓解过平滑问题方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将流体力学中的对流机制引入图神经网络，GNSN成功解决了传统扩散模型的过平滑瓶颈，实现了更高效直接的消息传播，为图神经网络的设计提供了新的物理启发视角。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a cornerstone of deep learning, with most existing methods rooted in graph signal processing and diffusion equations to model message passing. However, these approaches inherently suffer from the oversmoothing problem, where node features become indistinguishable as the network depth increases. Inspired by the Navier Stokes equations, we introduce Graph Navier Stokes Networks (GNSN), a novel architecture that transcends conventional diffusion-based message passing by incorporating convection into graph structures. GNSN defines a dynamic velocity field on the graph to govern convection, enabling more efficient and direct message propagation. By adaptively balancing convection and diffusion, GNSN is able to efficiently handle datasets with varying levels of homophily. Extensive evaluations across twelve real-world datasets demonstrate that GNSN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in classification accuracy. Moreover, experimental results further emphasize its effectiveness in alleviating the oversmoothing problem.

</details>


### [198] [On the Cost and Benefit of Chain of Thought: A Learning-Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21260)
*Yue Zhang,Zhiyi Dong,Tommaso Cesari,Yongyi Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个理解思维链（CoT）的学习理论框架，将CoT的风险分解为有益的“神谕轨迹风险”和有害的“轨迹失配风险”，证明了稳定性是控制误差累积和决定CoT成效的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链（Chain of Thought）在实践中表现出色，但目前缺乏一个统一的理论框架来解释其何时有效、何时失效。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过学习理论的视角，建立一个形式化的模型来量化和分析CoT推理过程中的风险与收益。

Method: 作者将CoT建模为答案映射与链式规则之间的交互，并定义了推理风险。通过理论推导，提出了一个紧致的规范分解，将该风险分解为两项：代表CoT收益的神谕轨迹风险（OTR）和代表因推理轨迹失配导致误差累积成本的轨迹失配风险（TMR）。

Result: 研究得出了两个核心结果：首先，若损失函数、答案映射或链式规则任一缺乏稳定性，轨迹失配风险可能无限大，即使假设非常接近真值；其次，在满足稳定性的前提下，文章证明了TMR的一个紧致上界，该界限由一个精确的放大因子控制，并识别了误差增长的有限、线性和指数三种模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个关于CoT的精确理论，阐明了CoT何时有帮助、何时有损害以及控制两者之间转换的关键因素。结论指出稳定性对于避免误差累积至关重要，决定了CoT推理的最终成败。

Abstract: We develop a learning-theoretic framework for understanding Chain of Thought (CoT). We model CoT as the interaction between an answer map and a chain rule that generates intermediate questions autoregressively, and define the reasoning risk of a hypothesis under this interaction. Our first result is a tight canonical decomposition of this risk into two terms with opposing roles: an oracle-trajectory risk (OTR), which captures the benefit of CoT and reduces to a target-domain risk in a domain adaptation problem, and a trajectory-mismatch risk (TMR), which captures the cost of CoT through error accumulation along mismatched reasoning trajectories. We then show that this cost is unavoidable without structure: if any one of the loss, the hypothesis answer map, or the chain rule lacks stability, the TMR can be arbitrarily large even when the OTR is zero and the hypothesis is uniformly close to the ground truth. Conversely, under stability, we prove a tight upper bound on the TMR governed by an exact amplification factor that identifies bounded, linear, and exponential error-growth regimes. Together, these results give a precise theory of when CoT helps, when it hurts, and what controls the transition between the two.

</details>


### [199] [Nonparametric Learning and Earning with One-Point Feedback under Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21263)
*Xiangyu Yang,Feng Xu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Jiaqiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非参数动态定价方法，通过基于收入的梯度近似和重启机制，解决了在非平稳市场环境下仅观测单期收入的动态定价问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在动态定价中面临两个核心挑战：一是每个时期只能观测到单一价格产生的收入，反馈信息有限；二是市场条件可能因客户偏好、竞争或外部冲击而发生渐变或突变。传统的参数化需求假设在非平稳环境下难以适用，因此需要一种能够适应未知市场变化的非参数学习框架。

Method: 作者提出了一个两层次的学习框架：（1）基础层：使用每期单次观测构建基于收入的梯度近似来更新价格，无需假设参数化需求函数；（2）重启机制：定期重置学习过程以淘汰过时信息，适应环境变化；（3）元学习层：在非平稳程度未知时，自适应地在多个重启策略间进行对冲。

Result: 算法的性能保证表明，相对于完全知情基准的累积收入损失取决于时间跨度和市场变化幅度。通过合成数据和真实数据的仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种能够在非平稳环境下进行有效学习与定价的框架，无需参数化需求假设，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其有效性，为企业面对不确定市场条件时的动态定价决策提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Firms increasingly rely on dynamic pricing to respond to evolving customer demand, yet in many applications they observe only the revenue generated by a single posted price in each period. At the same time, market conditions may shift gradually or abruptly due to changes in customer preferences, competition, or external shocks. These features create two intertwined challenges: learning the revenue--demand relationship from limited feedback and adapting pricing decisions to a changing environment. We study how a seller can learn and earn effectively under these constraints, without assuming a specific parametric form for demand. We develop a learning framework that updates prices using revenue-based gradient approximations constructed from one observation per period. To address environmental changes, we incorporate a restarting mechanism that periodically refreshes the learning process so that outdated information is discounted. When the degree of nonstationarity is unknown, we further introduce a meta-learning layer to adaptively hedge across multiple restarting schedules. We provide performance guarantees for our approach, showing how cumulative revenue loss relative to a fully informed benchmark depends on both the time horizon and the magnitude of market variation. Simulation experiments using synthetic and real-world data illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [200] [FedCoE: Bridging Generalization and Personalization via Federated Coordinated Dual-level MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21264)
*Penglin Dai,Fulian Li,Xincao Xu,Junhua Wang,Lixin Duan,Xiao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCoE框架，通过双重级混合专家机制解决联邦学习中的非IID参数分歧和冷启动问题，实现全局泛化与局部个性化的平衡，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法面临根本性挑战：传统基于平均的方法在非IID条件下参数分歧严重，而个性化联邦学习方法容易过拟合局部数据且难以泛化到新客户端（冷启动问题）。混合专家机制可自然地通过将异构数据路由到专门专家而非强制统一聚合来解决这个问题。

Method: 提出FedCoE（联邦协调双重级混合专家框架）：在服务器端维护多个独立的全局专家模型，采用共享门控网络在聚合过程中动态建模客户端-专家相关性，有效缓解专家漂移和门控不一致问题；引入自适应机制使新客户端能立即利用全局专家池而无需大量本地训练。

Result: 实验表明FedCoE平均达到78.00%全局准确率和89.32%个性化准确率，分别超越基线8.82%和29.19%；在冷启动场景中，无需本地微调即可达到77.27%准确率，超越基线12.54%以上。

Conclusion: FedCoE成功平衡了全局泛化与局部个性化需求，有效解决了非IID条件下的参数分歧问题，并通过自适应机制实现了冷启动场景下的即时部署能力，为联邦学习在实际应用中面临的异构性和新客户端问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for privacy-preserving distributed learning. However, existing FL methods face a fundamental challenge. Traditional averaging-based approaches suffer from parameter divergence under non-IID conditions, while personalized FL methods overfit to local data and fail to generalize to new clients (cold-start problem). Mixture-of-Experts naturally addresses this by routing heterogeneous data to specialized experts rather than forcing uniform aggregation. In this paper, we propose FedCoE, a Federated Coordinated dual-level mixture-of-Experts framework that effectively balances global generalization with local personalization. FedCoE maintains multiple independent global expert models on the server and employs a shared gating network to dynamically model client-expert correlations during aggregation, effectively mitigating expert drift and gating inconsistency. To address the cold-start challenge, we introduce an adaptive mechanism that enables new clients to immediately leverage the global expert pool without extensive local training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedCoE achieves 78.00% global accuracy and 89.32% personalized accuracy on average, outperforming the baseline by 8.82% and 29.19%, respectively. In cold-start scenarios, FedCoE delivers 77.27% accuracy without any local fine-tuning, outperforming baselines by over 12.54%.

</details>


### [201] [A Mechanistic Study of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21288)
*Marin Biloš,James T. Wilson,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对不同架构的表格基础模型进行了机制分析，揭示了它们采用不同的基于相似度的读取机制，解释了置换不变性的来源，并通过对抗性扰动验证了模型的故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同架构的表格基础模型在分类和回归任务中表现出相似的准确率，这引发了关于模型是否执行相同上下文算法、置换不变性来源以及模型鲁棒性等机制性问题的思考，而传统排行榜无法回答这些问题。

Method: 通过因果干预方法表征不同模型家族的相似度读取机制；识别导致置换不变性的特定位置参数并测试其移除效果；设计针对读取机制的对抗性扰动（hub和rank攻击）来验证模型故障模式。

Result: 发现不同模型实现质的不同的相似度读取机制（从注意力加权投票到类别条件均值读取）；表示坍塌不是实际问题；置换不变性源于特定位置参数，移除后保持准确率并将近似不变性变为精确不变性；对抗性扰动成功复现了预测的故障模式。

Conclusion: 该研究为当代表格基础模型提供了机制性解释，识别了支配模型准确率和特征失败的归纳偏置，加深了对表格基础模型工作原理的理解。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models with different architectures converge in accuracy across a range of classification and regression tasks. This raises questions a leaderboard cannot answer: (i) whether the models execute the same in-context algorithm, (ii) where row, column, and class-permutation invariances originate, and (iii) how robust they are under perturbations engineered against the inferred mechanism. We characterize all three. The model families realize qualitatively distinct similarity-based readouts: from an attention-weighted vote over context labels to a class-conditional mean readout, each confirmed by causal intervention. We find that the representation collapse highlighted in prior work is not a practical concern for them. Each model's permutation invariances trace to specific positional parameters whose removal preserves accuracy and makes approximate invariance exact. Perturbations engineered against each readout reproduce predicted failure modes; hub and rank attacks isolate them from refit baselines. Together these results give a mechanistic account of contemporary tabular foundation models and identify which inductive biases govern both their accuracy and characteristic failures.

</details>


### [202] [Automatic Discovery of Disease Subgroups by Contrasting with Healthy Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21301)
*Robin Louiset,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier,Antoine Grigis,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Deep UCSL方法，通过对比患者与健康对照组，利用深度学习识别仅由病理因素驱动的患者亚群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物医学亚群发现中，临床医生需要在患者群体中发现可解释且同质的亚群。现有方法面临的挑战是如何排除与健康受试者共有的变异因素，仅关注病理因素驱动的亚群。

Method: 提出了一种对比式亚群发现方法Deep UCSL。该方法假设健康对照组与患者共享无关的变异因素，通过将患者与对照组进行对比来识别亚群。技术上，使用深度特征提取器学习判别性表示空间，推导了一种基于潜在聚类和患者/对照标签的条件联合似然的新损失函数，采用期望最大化(EM)策略在亚群推断和特征编码器更新之间交替优化，并引入正则化项鼓励表示捕获疾病特异性变异。

Result: 与相关研究工作相比，该方法在MNIST示例和四个不同的真实医学影像数据集上，定量地提高了估计亚群的质量。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: Deep UCSL框架通过对比学习策略，能够有效识别仅由病理因素驱动的患者亚群，同时忽略与健康对照组共享的变异性，为生物医学领域的亚群发现提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In biomedical Subgroup Discovery, practitioners are interested in discovering interpretable and homogeneous subgroups within a group of patients. In this paper, assuming that healthy subjects (i.e., controls) share common but irrelevant factors of variation with the patients, we motivate and develop a Contrastive Subgroup Discovery method, entitled Deep UCSL. By contrasting patients with controls, Deep UCSL identifies subgroups driven solely by pathological factors, ignoring common variability shared with healthy subjects. Our framework employs a deep feature extractor to learn a discriminative representation space. Mathematically, we derive a novel loss based on the conditional joint likelihood of latent clusters and patient/control labels, optimized via an Expectation-Maximization strategy alternating between subgroup inference and feature encoder updates. A regularization term further encourages representations to capture disease-specific variability while ignoring variability shared with controls. Compared to previous related works, our approach quantitatively improves the quality of the estimated subgroups, as demonstrated on a MNIST example and four distinct real medical imaging datasets. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/rlouiset/deep_ucsl.

</details>


### [203] [A New Framework to Analyse the Distributional Robustness of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21313)
*Divij Khaitan,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于层级权重与激活交互的框架，通过建模伯努利分布并利用类间分离度作为诊断代理，成功量化并分析了深度神经网络对分布偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽然在多种任务上表现出色，但面对分布偏移时的脆弱性严重阻碍了其在现实世界中的部署，因此亟需一种方法来分析与量化这种分布鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种框架，通过研究层级权重与激活之间的交互作用来分析分布鲁棒性。具体使用伯努利分布对这些交互进行建模，并将类别间的分离度作为鲁棒性的诊断代理。实验在CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上进行。

Result: 提出的指标能够有效区分“记忆训练数据”的网络与“未记忆”的网络；在激活空间的对比实验表明相同属性不成立；在多种分布偏移下，基于路径的诊断显示分离度会降低。

Conclusion: 该框架能够提供有关表示结构和鲁棒性的有效模型级诊断，为理解和改进神经网络的分布鲁棒性提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have achieved impressive performance on a variety of tasks, but their brittleness to distributional shifts remains a significant barrier to real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose a framework to analyse and quantify the distributional robustness of neural networks by studying the interactions between layer weights and activations. We model these interactions using Bernoulli distributions, using the separation between classes as a diagnostic proxy for robustness. We demonstrate the usefulness of this framework through models trained on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet. We show that our proposed metrics can distinguish between networks that have memorised their training data and those that have not. We also perform analogous experiments in the activation space and find that the same properties do not hold up. Additionally, we investigate the behaviour of our metrics under various distribution shifts and show that these shifts reduce separation under our path-based diagnostics. Our results suggest that this framework provides useful model-level diagnostics of representation structure and robustness.

</details>


### [204] [CRAFT: Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21317)
*Ziqi Wang,Qiang Liu,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT框架，通过几何校正解决联邦学习中异构数据下的客户端更新冲突问题，在避免迭代计算开销的同时提升全局模型精度并减小客户端间性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构数据分布的联邦学习场景中，客户端更新之间存在冲突，简单的平均聚合虽然能改善全局目标，但会损害特定客户端的性能，这构成了联邦学习的核心瓶颈。

Method: 将聚合问题建模为几何校正问题，通过寻找距离参考方向最近且满足无冲突对齐约束的更新来求解，并推导出闭式解以避免迭代求解器的计算开销，同时采用分层适应机制处理不同特征粒度的冲突。

Result: 理论分析表明CRAFT通过投影几何机制促进共同下降结构并缓解冲突；在异构基准测试上的广泛实验显示，相比最先进基线方法，CRAFT提高了全局模型精度并降低了客户端间的性能差异。

Conclusion: CRAFT为联邦学习中的冲突聚合问题提供了一种高效且理论有据的解决方案，在保持计算效率的同时有效平衡了全局性能与客户端公平性。

Abstract: The aggregation of conflicting client updates remains a fundamental bottleneck in federated learning (FL) under heterogeneous data distributions. Naive averaging can produce a global update that improves the global objective while conflicting with specific clients, causing degradation for those clients. In this work, we propose CRAFT (Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training), a new aggregation framework that treats the global update as a geometric correction problem. We formulate aggregation as finding the update closest to a reference direction while satisfying conflict-free alignment constraints. We derive a closed-form expression for the constrained optimization problem, avoiding the computational overhead of iterative solvers. Furthermore, we use a layer-wise adaptation to address conflicts at varying feature granularities. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that CRAFT promotes a common-descent structure and mitigates conflicts through its projection geometry. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrate that CRAFT improves the accuracy of the global model while reducing performance disparity across clients compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The source code for CRAFT is available at https://github.com/tum-pbs/CRAFT.

</details>


### [205] [Optimized Federated Knowledge Distillation with Distributed Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21322)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon,Guilain Leduc,Sadi Alawadi,Feras M. Awaysheh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedKDNAS框架，通过结合客户端神经架构选择和服务器端知识蒸馏，在联邦学习中同时解决非独立同分布数据、系统异构性和通信效率问题，在准确率、计算资源和通信开销之间实现了更优的帕累托效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的联邦学习部署需要同时应对客户端数据的统计异构性（非IID）、设备能力的系统异构性以及通信效率挑战。现有方法虽然通过改进聚合、个性化或知识蒸馏缓解了这些问题，但普遍假设固定的客户端架构，限制了适应异构数据复杂度和硬件约束的能力，导致准确率与效率之间的权衡次优。

Method: 提出FedKDNAS框架，结合客户端神经架构选择与服务器协调的知识蒸馏：1）每个客户端在准确率-资源约束下自主选择轻量级模型；2）使用监督学习和知识蒸馏的混合目标进行本地训练；3）仅共享公共参考集上的预测结果；4）服务器聚合和平滑这些预测，可选地与教师模型结合，为下一轮生成稳定的蒸馏目标。

Result: 在六个数据集上与六个代表性联邦学习基线（FedAvg、Ditto、FedMD、FedDF、FedDistill、Local-KD）对比实验表明：在非IID条件下准确率提升最高达15%，客户端CPU使用量降低约28%，通信开销减少高达44倍，同时保持轻量级的基于logit的通信。

Conclusion: FedKDNAS通过解耦客户端架构选择与服务器端知识协调，成功打破了传统联邦学习中固定架构的限制，实现了准确率、计算效率和通信效率的帕累托最优权衡，为异构环境下的联邦学习部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing data. However, real-world deployments must simultaneously address statistical heterogeneity across client data (non-IID), system heterogeneity in device capabilities, and communication efficiency. Existing FL approaches mitigate these challenges through improved aggregation, personalization, or knowledge distillation, but they almost universally assume a fixed client architecture, limiting adaptability to heterogeneous data complexity and hardware constraints. This architectural constraint often leads to suboptimal trade-offs between accuracy and efficiency in real-world FL systems. This work introduces FedKDNAS, a distillation-driven FL framework that combines client-side neural architecture selection with distillation of server-coordinated knowledge. Each client autonomously selects a lightweight model under accuracy-resource constraints. It then trains it locally using a hybrid objective combining supervised learning and knowledge distillation and shares only predictions on a public reference set. The server then aggregates and smooths these predictions, optionally combining them with a teacher model, to produce stable distillation targets for the next round. Extensive evaluation on six datasets against six representative FL baselines (FedAvg, Ditto, FedMD, FedDF, FedDistill, Local-KD) demonstrates that FedKDNAS consistently achieves superior Pareto efficiency, improving accuracy by up to 15\% under non-IID conditions, reducing client CPU usage by approximately 28\%, and decreasing communication overhead by up to 44 times while maintaining lightweight logit-based communication.

</details>


### [206] [Fast and Stable Triangular Inversion for Delta-Rule Linear Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21325)
*Aleksandros Sobczyk,Gioele Gottardo,Christos K. Matzoros,Mirko De Vita,Filip Skogh,Anastasios Zouzias,Jiawei Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了线性注意力中三角矩阵求逆算法，提出了高效的数值稳定实现方案，在NPU上实现了最高4.3倍加速且保持模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力已被Qwen3.5/3.6、Kimi Linear、RWKV-7等先进模型采用，其中Delta-Rule涉及三角矩阵求逆操作。该操作既是性能瓶颈，又因对数值误差高度敏感而影响模型精度，亟需高效且数值稳定的实现方案。

Method: 系统分析了直接法和迭代法两类三角矩阵求逆算法，重点研究富含矩阵乘积的方法以充分利用现代硬件特性。从数值稳定性、计算复杂度、硬件效率和实际实现等多维度进行了理论分析与实验验证，特别关注低精度浮点表示场景。

Result: 在NPU上的性能基准测试表明，相较于SGLang的三角矩阵求逆实现，实现了高达4.3倍的加速；在整个层级上获得了显著性能提升，同时保持了完整的端到端模型精度。

Conclusion: 本研究为线性注意力架构中的三角矩阵求逆提供了高效、数值稳定的解决方案，在保证模型精度的前提下显著提升了计算性能，对高效长上下文架构的实现具有重要实践价值。

Abstract: Linear attention has emerged as a cornerstone for efficient long-context architectures, as evidenced by its integration into state-of-the-art open-source models including Qwen3.5/3.6, Kimi Linear, and RWKV-7. Models that incorporate linear attention layers with the so-called Delta-Rule involve the inversion of triangular matrices as a core sub-routine. This operation often forms a performance bottleneck, and, due to its high-sensitivity to numerical errors, it can significantly deteriorate end-to-end model accuracy if it is not carefully implemented. This work provides a systematic analysis of both direct and iterative triangular inversion algorithms, targeting methods that are rich in matrix products, and, therefore, have the potential to efficiently utilize modern hardware. To that end, our analysis covers a broad spectrum of mathematical and practical aspects, with a heavy focus on numerical stability, computational complexity, and, ultimately, hardware efficiency and practical considerations. We provide a rigorous experimental evaluation to verify these properties in practical scenarios, and in low-precision floating-point representations, highlighting the strengths and limitations of each method. Performance benchmarks on NPUs reveal up to $4.3\times$ speed-up against the state-of-the-art implementations of SGLang for triangular matrix inversion, leading to significant performance improvements on the entire layer level, while maintaining full end-to-end model accuracy.

</details>


### [207] [Classification of Single and Mixed Partial Discharges under Switching Voltage Using an AWA-CNN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21352)
*Md Rafid Kaysar Shagor,Zannatul Ferdousy Mouri,Farhina Haque,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于开关电压激励下的局部放电(PD)源分类的幅值-宽度-面积(AWA)模式表征方法。该方法将时域PD脉冲的幅值和面积作为坐标轴、宽度编码为颜色生成可视化模式，并利用CNN模型实现了超过96%的分类准确率，显著优于随机森林方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着快速开关功率电子设备的广泛应用，在开关电压激励下进行局部放电(PD)分析变得日益重要。然而，由于PD活动主要集中在电压转换期间，开关电压条件下的PD分析比正弦波条件更具挑战性，因此需要开发新的方法来进行有效的PD源分析。

Method: 提出幅值-宽度-面积(AWA)模式表征方法：首先用时域PD脉冲的幅值、宽度和面积三个特征进行表征，然后将幅值和面积映射为坐标轴，宽度通过颜色编码，形成可视化模式图。使用随机森林和两个CNN模型(InceptionV3和ResNet-18)对六种单一和混合PD源条件进行分类对比实验。

Result: AWA模式显示出明显的源依赖性分布特征。基于CNN的分类方法测试准确率超过96%，而随机森林基线方法仅为73.33%。实验验证了AWA模式能够有效表征开关电压激励下的PD脉冲特征，适用于多类PD源分类。

Conclusion: AWA模式为开关电压激励下的多类PD源分类提供了一种有效的可视化表征方法，配合深度学习模型能够实现高精度的PD源识别，为电力电子设备的PD检测与分析提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: The growing use of fast-switching power electronics has made partial discharge (PD) analysis under switching-voltage excitation increasingly important, yet more challenging than under sinusoidal conditions due to activity concentrated at voltage transitions. This work presents an Amplitude-Width-Area (AWA) pattern representation for source-oriented PD analysis under switching-voltage excitation. In the proposed method, time domain PD pulses are characterized using pulse amplitude, width, and area, and mapped into a visual pattern where amplitude and area define the coordinate axes and width is encoded by color. The generated AWA patterns are used to distinguish six single and mixed PD source conditions: corona, internal, surface, corona+internal, corona+surface, and internal+surface. To evaluate the classification capability of the proposed representation, a Random Forest baseline and two Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) models, InceptionV3 and ResNet-18, are compared. The AWA patterns show distinguishable source-dependent distributions, and CNN-based classification achieves testing accuracy above 96%, compared with 73.33% for Random Forest. The results indicate that AWA patterns provide a visual representation of PD pulses suitable for multi-class PD source classification under switching-voltage excitation.

</details>


### [208] [What Twelve LLM Agent Benchmark Papers Disclose About Themselves: A Pilot Audit and an Open Scoring Schema](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21404)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi,Faezeh Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对12篇LLM智能体基准测试论文进行了审计，发现智能体基准测试论文的平均披露得分仅为0.38/1.0，主要缺失成本报告和评估环境规范，揭示了当前智能体评估的可复现性危机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现在LLM智能体研究领域，不同论文对同一模型在同一基准测试上的结果经常出现分歧且原因不明（可能涉及脚手架、采样设置、数据子集或评估器版本等），而发表的文章通常缺乏足够的信息来判断差异来源，这促使作者对现有基准测试论文的评估披露情况进行系统性审计。

Method: 设计了一个包含五个字段的审计框架（基准身份、测试工具规范、推理设置、成本报告、失败细分），编写了包含边界情况的评分手册，对12篇经典论文（8篇智能体基准、4篇传统静态基准）进行单轮审计评分，评分聚焦于披露完整性而非结果正确性。

Result: 8篇智能体基准论文的平均审计得分为0.38/1.0，4篇传统静态基准论文为0.66/1.0。最大差距在于成本报告（8篇智能体论文均未披露任何形式的推理成本）和测试工具规范（无一篇完整披露评估环境的内容寻址容器镜像）。发布了审计框架（JSON Schema）、评分手册（Markdown）和原始评分表（CSV）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体基准测试论文的评估披露严重不足，威胁研究可复现性。审计框架为改进披露标准提供了基础工具，下一步应进行多评估者审计以提高可靠性。

Abstract: We read twelve well-known LLM agent benchmark papers and recorded, dimension by dimension, what each paper actually says about how its evaluation was run. The motivation came from a familiar frustration: two papers will report results on the same benchmark with the same model name and disagree, and you cannot tell why -- the scaffold, the sampling settings, the subset, or the evaluator version. In many cases the published artifact does not let you answer. This paper is an implementation report on the attempt. We designed a small audit schema (five fields: benchmark identity, harness specification, inference settings, cost reporting, failure breakdown), wrote a scoring codebook with the boundary cases we hit during pilot scoring, applied it to twelve canonical papers (eight agent, four classical static), and recorded what we saw. We score the disclosure of an agent run, not its correctness, and make no claim that disclosure implies a trustworthy result. The mean audit score across the eight agent-benchmark papers is 0.38 (out of 1.0), and across the four classical static benchmarks 0.66; the largest gap is on cost (none of the eight agent benchmark papers disclose inference cost in any form) and on harness specification (none fully disclose a content-addressed container image of the evaluation environment). We release the schema as a JSON Schema file, the codebook as a Markdown document, and the raw scoring sheet as a CSV. The scoring was performed by a single auditor in one pass; a multi-rater audit is the natural next step, and we discuss what we think it would change.

</details>


### [209] [Preference-aware Influence-function-based Data Selection Method for Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21422)
*Qihao Lin,Guanxu Chen,Dongrui Liu,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRISM方法，通过当前模型的偏好对目标示例加权，构建偏好感知的目标表示，从而更有效地选择训练数据，提升微调效率和安全性修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模扩大，训练效率越来越依赖于数据的有效利用。现有数据选择方法通常用目标示例表示目标行为，但将这些示例视为同等重要，忽略了它们与当前模型的相关性差异——接近当前模型行为的示例比远离的示例提供更具可操作性的指导。

Method: 提出PRISM（偏好感知的影响力函数数据选择方法）：1）使用当前模型的偏好对目标示例进行加权，构建偏好感知的目标表示；2）根据候选训练样本与该表示的对齐程度进行评分；3）将数据预算集中在更可能推动模型向目标行为移动的样本上。理论分析表明偏好加权为增加目标行为偏好提供了更有效的一阶方向。

Result: 在多个模型家族和规模上的实验表明，PRISM在高效微调和面向安全的有监督微调修复两个任务上都取得了改进，证明了精确的目标行为表征是预算高效数据选择的关键。

Conclusion: 精确的目标行为表征对于预算高效的数据选择至关重要，通过偏好感知的目标表示可以更有效地指导数据选择，从而提升大语言模型的训练效率。

Abstract: As LLMs continue to scale, improving training efficiency increasingly depends on using data more effectively. Data selection addresses this problem by allocating a limited training budget to samples that best promote a target behavior. Existing methods usually represent the target behavior with a set of target examples, but often treat these examples as equally important. This can be inefficient because target examples may differ in their relevance to the current model: examples closer to the model's current behavior provide more actionable guidance than those farther away. We propose PRISM (PReference-aware Influence-function-based Data Selection Method for Efficient Fine-Tuning), which uses the current model's preference to weight target examples and construct a preference-aware target representation. PRISM then scores candidate training samples by their alignment with this representation, concentrating the data budget on samples more likely to move the model toward the target behavior. Theoretical analysis shows that this preference weighting yields a more effective first-order direction for increasing target-behavior preference. Experiments across model families and scales show that PRISM improves both efficient fine-tuning and safety-oriented SFT repair, demonstrating that precise target-behavior characterization is key to budget-efficient data selection.

</details>


### [210] [Adaptive Signal Resuscitation: Channel-wise Post-Pruning Repair for Sparse Vision Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21426)
*Qishi Zhan,Ziheng Chen,Minxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应信号复苏（ASR），一种免训练的通道级修复方法，通过匹配修复粒度与损伤粒度，有效解决了全局幅度剪枝在高稀疏度下的精度崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全局幅度剪枝在高稀疏度下会导致严重的精度崩溃。作者发现这源于修复粒度的不匹配：同一层内可能同时存在近乎崩溃的通道和保留有效信息的通道，而现有的层级激活修复方法对整个层应用单一校正，会过度放大受损通道。

Method: 提出自适应信号复苏（ASR）方法，是一种免训练的通道级修复技术。ASR为每个输出通道估计方差匹配校正，并使用数据驱动的收缩规则进行稳定化处理，抑制信号微弱通道的不可靠校正，同时保留健康通道的校正。该方法仅需在小校准集上进行前向传播，无需重新训练，应用于BatchNorm重校准之前。

Result: 在三个数据集、四种卷积架构以及非结构化和结构化稀疏性设置上，ASR普遍优于层级修复方法，在高稀疏度场景下优势最为明显。在ResNet-50上以90%稀疏度测试，ASR在CIFAR-10上恢复了55.6%的top-1精度，而层级修复仅为41.0%，仅BatchNorm重校准为28.0%。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，简单的通道级方差匹配是不够的，收缩处理对稳定剪枝后修复至关重要。ASR通过将修复粒度与损伤粒度相匹配，有效解决了高稀疏度下的精度崩溃问题。

Abstract: One-shot magnitude pruning can cause severe accuracy collapse in the high-sparsity regime, even when the pruning mask preserves the largest weights. We argue that this failure reflects a granularity mismatch in post-pruning repair. Under global magnitude pruning, nearly collapsed channels can coexist with channels that retain informative activation variance within the same layer. Existing layer-wise activation repair methods apply a single correction to the whole layer, and can therefore over-amplify damaged channels while trying to restore the layer-level signal. We propose Adaptive Signal Resuscitation (ASR), a training-free channel-wise repair method that matches the granularity of repair to the granularity of damage. ASR estimates a variance-matching correction for each output channel and stabilizes it with a data-driven shrinkage rule, suppressing unreliable corrections for channels with weak post-pruning signal while preserving corrections for healthier channels. Applied before BatchNorm recalibration, ASR requires only forward passes on a small calibration set and no retraining. Across three datasets, four convolutional architectures, and both unstructured and structured sparsity settings, ASR generally improves over layer-wise repair, with the clearest gains in high-sparsity regimes. On ResNet-50 at 90% sparsity, ASR recovers 55.6% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10, compared with 41.0% for layer-wise repair and 28.0% for BatchNorm-only recalibration. Ablations show that naive channel-wise variance matching is insufficient, and that shrinkage stabilizes post-pruning repair.

</details>


### [211] [Polynomial-Time Robust Multiclass Linear Classification under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21428)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Giannis Iakovidis,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯分布下多类线性分类器的不可知学习问题，首次给出了维度独立误差保证的多项式时间鲁棒学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分类情形（k=2）已有成熟的理论，但多类情形（k≥3）的研究相对匮乏，现有算法在复杂度和表示规模上对精度的倒数呈指数依赖，需要开发高效且具有维度独立误差保证的学习方法。

Method: 提出成对不当学习框架，实现一般k类的误差$\tilde{O}(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+\epsilon$；发展基于定位的更精细框架，对k=3达到误差$O(\mathrm{opt})+\epsilon$，对几何正则的k类线性分类器达到$\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+\epsilon$。

Result: 标准多类感知机算法即使在高斯边缘分布和纯净标签下也需要超多项式样本和更新次数；新算法实现了完全多项式时间且维度独立的误差保证。

Conclusion: 多类线性分类器的不可知学习可以通过不当学习与定位技术实现高效算法，突破了二分情形方法的局限，为多类学习理论提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the task of agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under the Gaussian distribution. Given labeled examples $(x, y)$ from a distribution over $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$, with Gaussian $x$-marginal, the goal is to output a hypothesis whose error is comparable to that of the best $k$-class linear classifier. While the binary case $k=2$ has a well-developed algorithmic theory, much less is known for $k \ge 3$. Even for $k=3$, prior robust algorithms incur exponential dependence on the inverse of the desired accuracy in both complexity and representation size. In this work, we develop new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers and use them to design fully polynomial-time robust learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. Our first result shows that the standard multiclass perceptron algorithm requires super-polynomially many samples and updates, even with clean labels and Gaussian marginals, revealing a basic obstruction absent in the binary case. Our main positive result is a pairwise improper-learning framework which yields an efficient learner with error $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ for general $k$. Additionally, we develop a sharper localization-based framework which leads to error $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ for $k=3$, and error $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ for geometrically regular $k$-class linear classifiers.

</details>


### [212] [Gaussian Sheaf Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21435)
*André Ribeiro,Ana Luiza Tenório,Tiago da Silva,Diego Mesquita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了高维Sheaf神经网络（GSNNs），一种专门处理节点特征为高斯分布（均值和协方差）的图神经网络框架，通过推导新的Laplacian算子保留几何和代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络的消息传递机制适用于向量值节点特征，但当节点特征更适合用概率分布（特别是高斯分布）表示时，简单地将均值和协方差参数拼接为向量并应用标准消息传递，会丢失控制均值和协方差的几何与代数结构。

Method: 基于Cellular Sheaf理论，构建了一个新的框架GSNNs；推导了一个新的Laplacian算子，该算子将Sheaf Laplacian推广到节点特征为高斯分布的设置，并保留了其关键性质。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上的实验表明，GSNNs在处理高斯分布节点特征的图学习任务中具有实际价值和优越性。

Conclusion: GSNNs为具有概率分布特征的图数据提供了一个原理性的学习框架，成功地将几何先验融入图神经网络，拓展了GNNs在关系数据学习中的应用范围。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become the de facto standard for learning on relational data. While traditional GNNs' message passing is well suited for vector-valued node features, there are cases in which node features are better represented by probability distributions than real vectors. Concretely, when node features are Gaussians, characterized by a mean and a covariance matrix, naively concatenating their parameters into a single vector and applying standard message passing discards the geometric and algebraic structure that governs means and covariances. We propose Gaussian Sheaf Neural Networks (GSNNs), a principled framework that incorporates these inductive biases into graph-based learning. Building on the theory of cellular sheaves, we derive a new Laplacian operator that generalizes the sheaf Laplacian to this setting and preserves its key properties. We complement our theoretical contributions with experiments on synthetic and real-world data that illustrate the practical relevance of GSNNs.

</details>


### [213] [Mitigating Label Bias with Interpretable Rubric Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21455)
*Calvin Isley,Johann D. Gaebler,Sharad Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评分标准嵌入的表示框架，通过使用专家定义的标准特征替代黑盒嵌入，有效缓解了历史标签偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在招聘、大学录取和内容审核等难以获得真实标签的领域，模型通常基于历史人工评估进行训练。如果过去的评估存在对特定群体的不公正偏向，模型会继承这些偏差，导致算法决策的不公平性。

Method: 提出评分标准嵌入框架，使用从专家定义标准派生的特征替代标准黑盒嵌入，这些特征与潜在目标构念对齐，将预测锚定在语义上有意义的维度上，从而防止有偏代理信号的影响。

Result: 在大型硕士项目申请数据集上的实验表明，基于评分标准嵌入训练的模型能够减少群体差异，同时提高群体质量指标。理论和实证证据均证明该方法在合理条件下能缓解标签偏差。

Conclusion: 基于可解释的、领域扎根的表示进行预测，为在存在偏差标签的情况下进行学习提供了一种实用方法，能够在缓解偏差的同时保持或提升决策质量。

Abstract: Statistical decision algorithms are increasingly deployed in domains where ground-truth labels are hard to obtain, such as hiring, university admissions, and content moderation. In these settings, models are typically trained on historical human evaluations -- for example, using past hiring decisions as a proxy for true applicant quality. However, if past evaluations unjustly favor certain groups, models trained on these labels may inherit those biases. To address this problem, we propose basing predictions on rubric embeddings, a representation framework that replaces standard black-box embeddings with features derived from expert-defined criteria that align with the underlying construct of interest. By anchoring predictions to semantically meaningful dimensions, this approach guards against biased proxy signals. We provide both theoretical and empirical evidence that rubric embeddings mitigate label bias under plausible conditions. Empirically, we evaluate our method on a novel dataset of applications to a large master's program. We find that models trained on rubric embeddings reduce group disparities while improving measures of cohort quality. Our results suggest that basing predictions on interpretable, domain-grounded representations offers a practical approach to learning in the presence of biased labels.

</details>


### [214] [A Machine Learning Framework for Weighted Least Squares GNSS Positioning based on Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21461)
*Pin-Hsun Lee,Harry Leib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合机器学习与加权最小二乘（WLS）的GNSS定位框架，利用集成学习算法识别劣质信号并通过激活函数将其转化为权重，显著提升了城市峡谷环境下的定位精度，且具有良好的地理迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在城市峡谷环境中，高层建筑和狭窄街道会导致GNSS信号遮挡、非视距（NLOS）接收和多径效应，引入伪距测量误差。尽管多星座GNSS增加了可用卫星数量，但包含退化信号会导致严重的定位误差，因此需要一种有效方法来识别并降低劣质信号的影响。

Method: 提出一种机器学习框架，首先利用多种信号质量指标作为特征训练集成学习算法，为信号提供质量评分以识别劣质信号；随后采用激活函数（如Sigmoid函数）将机器学习预测的评分转换为加权最小二乘（WLS）算法的权重，从而优化定位解算。

Result: 基于香港和东京城市地区的真实数据集进行实验，结果表明Sigmoid激活函数在不同机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置下均能带来最大改进；该算法在单星座和多星座场景下均显著降低了定位误差，且当使用其他类似城市化程度区域的数据进行训练时，仍能保持相当的性能水平，显示出强地理迁移性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于机器学习的加权最小二乘定位算法有效提升了城市峡谷环境下的GNSS定位精度，Sigmoid激活函数表现最佳，算法具有良好的地理迁移能力，为城市环境下的高精度定位提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) are widely used to provide position, velocity, and timing (PVT) information for various applications, including transportation, location-based communication services, and intelligent agriculture. In urban canyons, high-rise buildings and narrow streets can cause signal obstruction, non-line-of-sight (NLOS) reception, and multipath effects that introduce errors in GNSS pseudorange measurements. Although multi-constellations GNSS effectively increase the number of available satellites, the inclusion of degraded signals can lead to severe positioning errors. This study proposes a machine learning framework for the weighted least squares (WLS) algorithm incorporating activation functions to enhance positioning accuracy. Several signal quality indicators are employed as training features for ensemble learning algorithms to identify poor quality signals by providing quality scores. Then, activation functions are employed to transform the machine learning predicted scores to appropriate weights for WLS positioning. To evaluate the performance of our approach, experiments are conducted using real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Tokyo urban areas. Comparative analysis of activation functions reveals that sigmoid functions consistently yield the greatest improvements with different machine learning algorithms and GNSS constellation configurations. The proposed algorithm demonstrates substantial reductions in positioning errors for both single- and multiconstellation scenarios. Furthermore, our results indicate that the proposed algorithm exhibits strong geographical transferability. The proposed algorithm maintains comparable level of performance when trained on data from other regions with similar levels of urbanization.

</details>


### [215] [DelTA: Discriminative Token Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21467)
*Kaiyi Zhang,Wei Wu,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DelTA方法，通过判别式Token信用分配来改进RLVR的训练动态，在数学推理基准上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习验证奖励（RLVR）方法在将响应级奖励转化为Token级概率变化时，其判别器由正负侧质心构成，容易被共享的高频模式（如格式化Token）主导，稀释了稀疏但具有判别性的方向，导致高奖励和低奖励响应的区分能力不足。

Method: 提出DelTA（判别式Token信用分配方法）：1）引入判别器视角分析RLVR更新方向；2）估计Token系数来放大侧特定的Token梯度方向，并降低共享或弱判别性方向的权重；3）通过这些系数重加权自归一化的RLVR代理目标，使有效的侧向质心更具对比性，从而重塑RLVR更新方向。

Result: 在七个数学基准测试中，DelTA在Qwen3-8B-Base和Qwen3-14B-Base上分别比最强同规模基线平均高出3.26和2.62个点。在代码生成、不同骨干网络和域外评估上的额外结果进一步证明了DelTA的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DelTA通过改进Token级信用分配，有效解决了RLVR中共享高频模式主导的问题，显著提升了模型在数学推理等任务上的性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central technique for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Despite its effectiveness, how response-level rewards translate into token-level probability changes remains poorly understood. We introduce a discriminator view of RLVR updates, showing that the policy-gradient update direction implicitly acts as a linear discriminator over token-gradient vectors and thereby determines which token probabilities are increased or decreased during learning. Under standard sequence-level RLVR, this discriminator is constructed from positive- and negative-side centroids formed by advantage-weighted averaging of token-gradient vectors. However, such centroid construction can be dominated by shared high-frequency patterns, such as formatting tokens, diluting sparse yet discriminative directions that better distinguish high-reward responses from low-reward ones. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{DelTA}$, a discriminative token credit assignment method that estimates token coefficients to amplify side-specific token-gradient directions and downweight shared or weakly discriminative ones. These coefficients reweight a self-normalized RLVR surrogate, making the effective side-wise centroids more contrastive and thereby reshaping the RLVR update direction. On seven mathematical benchmarks, DelTA outperforms the strongest same-scale baselines by 3.26 and 2.62 average points on Qwen3-8B-Base and Qwen3-14B-Base, respectively. Additional results on code generation, a different backbone, and out-of-domain evaluations further demonstrate the generalization ability of DelTA.

</details>


### [216] [You Only Need Minimal RLVR Training: Extrapolating LLMs via Rank-1 Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21468)
*Zhepei Wei,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Chengsong Huang,Jiaxin Huang,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习（RLVR）的参数轨迹具有极低秩特性，主要性能增益可由秩为1的近似捕捉。基于此提出的RELEX方法通过线性回归外推，仅需15%的训练步数即可达到或超越完整训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的主流范式，但其参数轨迹的几何特性尚未被充分探索。理解并利用这些特性有望带来更高效的训练方法。

Method: 提出RELEX方法：首先从短观察窗口中估计秩为1的参数变化子空间，然后通过线性回归外推未来检查点，无需学习额外模型。该方法将参数更新投影到秩为1的子空间上，起到去噪效果。

Result: 在三个模型（Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B、Qwen3-4B-Base、Qwen3-8B-Base）上，RELEX仅需完整RLVR训练15%的步数，在域内和域外基准上即可达到或超越RLVR性能。RELEX可外推至观察窗口10-20倍远（如观察前50步外推至1000步）且性能持续提升。增加子空间秩或使用非线性建模均未带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: RLVR权重轨迹呈现极低秩且高度可预测的特性，秩为1的近似足以捕捉主要性能增益。RELEX通过简单高效的线性外推实现了训练加速，验证了极简主义方法的充分性，为高效训练大语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a dominant paradigm for improving reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying geometry of the resulting parameter trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that RLVR weight trajectories are extremely low-rank and highly predictable. Specifically, we find that the majority of downstream performance gains are captured by a rank-1 approximation of the parameter deltas, where the magnitude of this projection evolves near-linearly with training steps. Motivated by this, we propose a simple and compute-efficient method RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation), which estimates the rank-1 subspace from a short observation window and extrapolates future checkpoints via linear regression, with no learned model required. Across three models (i.e., Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base), RELEX produces checkpoints that match or exceed RLVR performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, requiring as few as 15% steps of full RLVR training. Remarkably, RELEX is able to extrapolate far beyond the observation window at no training cost, predicting checkpoints up to 10-20$\times$ beyond the observed prefix with continued improvement (e.g., observe only the first 50 steps and extrapolate to 1000 steps). Our ablation analysis confirms the minimalist sufficiency of RELEX: neither increasing the subspace rank nor employing non-linear modeling yields further gains in extrapolation. Finally, we show that RELEX's success stems from a "denoising" effect: by projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace, the model discards stochastic optimization noise that would otherwise degrade performance during extrapolation. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX.

</details>


### [217] [Is Fixing Schema Graphs Necessary? Full-Resolution Graph Structure Learning for Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21475)
*Yi Huang,Qingyun Sun,Jia Li,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FROG框架，通过可学习的表角色建模实现全分辨率且可优化的图结构学习，解决了关系深度学习中固定图结构的局限性，在实验中取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关系深度学习（RDL）方法在图构建时普遍采用全分辨率原则以保持关系语义，这导致大多数方法依赖固定的图结构，限制了模型的表达能力和灵活性。

Method: 提出FROG框架，将关系结构学习形式化为可学习的表角色建模问题，允许表作为节点和边参与消息传递；设计角色驱动的消息传递机制以捕获关系语义，实现图结构与GNN表示的联合优化；引入函数依赖约束来正则化表级和实体级的表示，确保语义一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法优于现有方法，并揭示了表角色如何影响下游任务，为RDL中的图构建提供了新的见解。

Conclusion: FROG框架成功实现了全分辨率且可优化的图结构学习，为关系数据库的图建模提供了一种更灵活、更有效的方法，加深了对关系深度学习中图构建过程的理解。

Abstract: Relational prediction tasks are fundamental in many real-world applications, where data are naturally stored in relational databases (RDBs). Relational Deep Learning (RDL) addresses this problem by modeling RDBs as graphs and applying graph neural networks (GNNs) for end-to-end learning. However, the full-resolution property is commonly adopted as a design principle in graph construction for RDBs to preserve relational semantics, which leads most existing methods to rely on fixed graph structures.
