In this paper, we propose FROG, a Full-Resolution and Optimizable Graph Structure Learning} framework for RDL that formulates relational structure learning as a learnable table role modeling problem, allowing tables to contribute as nodes and edges in message passing. We further design role-driven message passing mechanisms to capture relational semantics, enabling joint optimization of graph structure and GNN representations. To ensure semantic consistency, we introduce functional dependency constraints that regularize representations across table and entity levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches and reveal how table roles impact downstream tasks, offering new insights into graph construction for RDL

</details>


### [218] [EvoStruct: Bridging Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Antibody CDR Design via Protein Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21485)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Sujin Lee,Umar Khayaz,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoStruct方法，通过冻结蛋白质语言模型(PLM)与E(3)-等变图神经网络(GNN)的跨注意力适配器，解决抗体CDR设计中的词汇崩塌问题，在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上相比最佳GNN基线序列恢复率提升16%，困惑度降低43%，氨基酸多样性提升2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变图神经网络(GNN)方法在抗体互补决定区(CDR)设计中存在严重的词汇崩塌问题，过度预测酪氨酸和甘氨酸等少数氨基酸，而忽略功能重要残基。根本原因在于GNN编码器从有限的结构数据中从头学习氨基酸分布，丢弃了进化数据库中编码的替代模式。

Method: 提出EvoStruct方法，通过跨注意力适配器将冻结的蛋白质语言模型(PLM)与来自E(3)-等变GNN的3D结构上下文桥接。针对CDR设计特有的词汇崩塌问题，采用渐进式PLM解冻和R-Drop一致性正则化策略。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，EvoStruct在多个抗体设计方法中实现了最高的氨基酸恢复率和最低的困惑度。相比最佳GNN基线，序列恢复率提升16%，困惑度降低43%，同时恢复2.3倍的氨基酸多样性，并与真实值达到最高的结合对相关性。

Conclusion: EvoStruct成功解决了抗体CDR设计中的词汇崩塌问题，通过结合预训练PLM的进化信息与结构信息，显著提升了设计质量和多样性，为抗体设计领域提供了有效的新方法。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural network (GNN) methods for antibody complementarity-determining region (CDR) design achieve the highest sequence recovery but suffer from severe vocabulary collapse. The current best GNN methods over-predict very few amino acids, such as tyrosine and glycine, while ignoring functionally important residues. We trace this failure to GNN encoders learning amino acid distributions de novo from limited structural data, discarding substitution patterns encoded in evolutionary databases. To resolve this, we propose EvoStruct, which bridges a frozen protein language model (PLM) with 3D structural context from an E(3)-equivariant GNN via a cross-attention adapter. Unlike prior PLM-structure adapters for general protein design, EvoStruct targets the vocabulary collapse problem specific to CDR design through progressive PLM unfreezing and R-Drop consistency regularization. On the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, EvoStruct achieves the highest amino acid recovery and lowest perplexity among several antibody design methods, improving sequence recovery by 16% and reducing perplexity by 43% relative to the best GNN baselines, while recovering 2.3x greater amino acid diversity and the highest binding-pair correlation with ground truth.

</details>


### [219] [Equilibrium Reasoners: Learning Attractors Enables Scalable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21488)
*Benhao Huang,Zhengyang Geng,Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平衡推理器（EqR），通过学习任务条件吸引子实现测试时计算的扩展，无需外部验证器即可在Sudoku-Extreme上将准确率从2.6%提升至99%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通过迭代更新潜在状态扩展测试时计算的方法虽有效，但其超越记忆模式实现泛化推理的内部机制尚不清晰。作者假设泛化推理能力源于学习到的任务条件吸引子，即潜在动力学系统中的稳定不动点对应于有效解。

Method: 提出平衡推理器（EqR）框架，该框架允许在无外部验证器或任务特定先验的情况下进行测试时扩展。EqR沿两个维度扩展内部动力学：深度（通过运行更多迭代次数）和广度（通过聚合来自多次初始化的随机轨迹）。

Result: 实验表明测试时扩展的增益与向解对齐吸引子的更强收敛性密切相关。简单案例在1-5次迭代内收敛，困难案例则受益于大规模测试时扩展。通过展开至等效40,000层，可扩展潜在推理在Sudoku-Extreme上的准确率从前馈模型的2.6%提升至99%以上。

Conclusion: 学习到的吸引子景观为理解迭代潜在模型中的可扩展推理提供了有用的机制视角，表明任务条件吸引子是实现泛化推理的关键机制。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute by iteratively updating a latent state has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning. Yet the internal mechanisms that enable these iterative models to generalize beyond memorized patterns remain unclear. We hypothesize that generalizable reasoning arises from learning task-conditioned attractors: latent dynamical systems whose stable fixed points correspond to valid solutions.
  We formalize this process through Equilibrium Reasoners (EqR), which enable test-time scaling without external verifiers or task-specific priors. EqR scales internal dynamics along two axes: depth, by running more iterations, and breadth, by aggregating stochastic trajectories from multiple initializations. Empirically, gains from test-time scaling are tightly coupled with stronger convergence toward solution-aligned attractors.
  This attractor perspective allows neural networks to adaptively allocate test-time compute based on task difficulty. While simple cases converge within 1 to 5 iteration steps, harder cases benefit from massive test-time scaling. By unrolling up to the equivalent of 40,000 layers, scalable latent reasoning boosts accuracy from 2.6% for feedforward models to over 99% on Sudoku-Extreme. These results suggest that learned attractor landscapes provide a useful mechanistic lens for understanding scalable reasoning in iterative latent models.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Clove: Object-Level CXL Memory Management in Managed Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20370)
*Sam Son,Zhihong Luo,Wen Zhang,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Scott Shenker*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出Clove系统，通过扩展现有托管语言运行时来实现CXL分层内存的对象级管理，相比页式系统将应用性能下降减少了22-84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对象级CXL内存管理研究不足，主要局限于非托管语言应用；而托管语言运行时已具备对象重定位等优化机制，但缺乏分层内存管理所需的特性，存在扩展空间。

Method: 提出Clove系统，在现有托管运行时中进行扩展，结合了基于剖析的对象热度追踪与对象重定位技术及策略。

Result: 基于JVM的原型系统实现了快速层级内存的高利用率并控制了运行时开销，相比页式系统将应用性能下降减少了22-84%。

Conclusion: 托管语言运行时是实现对象级CXL内存管理的有效设计点，通过合理扩展运行时可显著提升分层内存管理效率。

Abstract: Object-level management of tiered memory has been studied to address the inefficiencies in page-based systems. However, object-level management for CXL-tiered memory remains underexplored due to CXL's tight performance budget and load/store interface. As a result, existing approaches remain limited in scope, primarily targeting unmanaged-language applications with bespoke runtimes or compiler support.
  This paper identifies and explores a new design point for object-level CXL management: managed languages and their runtimes. The key observation is that existing managed runtimes already provide highly optimized mechanisms for problems closely related to object-level management, including object relocation and dynamic code generation. However, they still lack the features needed for tiered memory management, such as hotness tracking and relocation policies, and thus must be carefully extended to fully realize this direction.
  We present Clove, a system that extends existing managed runtimes to support object-level CXL management for managed-language applications. Clove combines profile-guided object hotness tracking with object relocation techniques and policies. Our JVM prototype demonstrates that this extension enables high utilization of fast-tier memory while bounding runtime overhead, reducing application slowdown by 22-84% compared to page-based systems.

</details>


### [221] [ParaCell: Paravirtualized Secure Containers with Lightweight Intra-Container Isolation and Intent-Driven Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20906)
*Yiyang Wu,Xunjie Wang,Jinyu Gu,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: ParaCell通过利用MPK硬件隔离和主动内存管理，显著提升了安全容器在传统云环境和新兴代理应用中的性能和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全容器设计面临隔离与性能的根本性权衡问题：嵌套云部署放大了VM exits和页表管理开销，新兴代理工作负载的突发内存需求需要细粒度弹性。根本原因在于：1) 缺乏轻量级容器内隔离原语来处理频繁的用户-内核态切换；2) 主机将容器内存管理视为黑盒，被迫采用被动的二级缺页和粗粒度大页映射。

Method: 提出ParaCell，一种半虚拟化安全容器运行时。包含两大创新：1) 使用基于MPK的XGates实现单地址空间内容器用户态和内核态隔离，将频繁的用户-内核切换转化为直接的域切换；2) 引入Pager机制，拦截分配和释放事件，批量主动完成GPA到HPA的绑定/解绑，避免被动的影子页表缺页，同时保持细粒度内存弹性。

Result: ParaCell作为RunV的即插即用替代方案实现。实验表明：在裸机和嵌套部署中，相比PVM延迟降低最高达57%和79%，相比RunV降低最高达33%和88%。在代理工作负载上，相比最先进的VM内存回收技术HyperAlloc，节省高达35.6%的内存。

Conclusion: ParaCell成功解决了安全容器的隔离-性能权衡问题，通过利用硬件隔离原语和暴露内存管理意图，在保持强隔离的同时显著提升了性能和内存效率，为传统云和新兴代理应用提供了高效的容器运行时解决方案。

Abstract: Secure containers isolate each container with its own kernel, mitigating shared-kernel attacks prevalent in traditional container systems. However, existing designs still face a fundamental isolation--performance trade-off. Nested-cloud deployments amplify the cost of VM exits and page-table management, while emerging agentic workloads expose bursty memory demand that requires fine-grained elasticity. We attribute this trade-off to two root causes. First, existing designs lack lightweight intra-container isolation primitives for frequent container user--kernel transitions. Second, the host treats container memory management as opaque, forcing reactive secondary faults and coarse-grained huge page mappings to amortize their cost.
  This paper presents ParaCell, a paravirtualized secure container runtime built on two insights. First, intra-address-space hardware protection primitives can provide lightweight intra-container isolation. ParaCell uses MPK-based XGates to isolate the container user and container kernel within a single address space, turning frequent user--kernel transitions into direct domain switches. Second, container kernel allocators already encode memory-management intent. ParaCell introduces Pager to interpose on allocation and free events, batch proactive GPA to HPA bindings and unbindings, and avoid reactive shadow page-table faults while preserving fine-grained memory elasticity.
  ParaCell is implemented as a drop-in replacement for RunV. Our experiments demonstrate that, across traditional cloud and emerging agent applications, ParaCell reduces latency by up to 57% and 79% over PVM, and by up to 33% and 88% over RunV, in bare-metal and nested setups, respectively. On agent workloads, ParaCell saves up to 35.6% memory compared with the state-of-the-art VM memory reclamation technique, HyperAlloc.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [ELSA: An ELastic SNN Inference Architecture for Efficient Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20802)
*Kang You,Chen Nie,Lee Jun Yan,Ziling Wei,Cheng Zou,Zekai Xu,Yu Feng,Honglan Jiang,Zhezhi He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出ELSA架构，通过细粒度流水线实现SNN的弹性推理，相比SOTA QANN加速器实现3.4倍加速和13.6倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN具有弹性推理特性，可渐进式输出结果以实现早期响应，但现有SNN加速器无法利用此优势：逐层设计需等待全部层完成，逐时间步设计采用粗粒度流水线需同步层内所有数据，导致结果无法立即传递，丧失弹性推理带来的延迟优势。

Method: 提出ELSA近SRAM数据流架构：1)细粒度spine/token级流水线，数据产生即转发；2)捆绑地址事件表示协议降低NoC通信流量；3)mini-batch spiking Gustavson乘积减少内存访问并利用固有稀疏性；4)映射与调度优化实现高效事件驱动计算。

Result: 在4-bit ResNet-50上，ELSA相比SOTA QANN加速器ANT实现3.4倍加速和13.6倍能效提升；相比SOTA SNN加速器PAICORE实现2.9倍加速和22.1倍能效提升；SNN在保持精度的同时超越量化人工神经网络性能。

Conclusion: ELSA成功实现了SNN真正的弹性推理，充分发挥了事件驱动计算的优势，证明了SNN在能效和延迟方面超越量化人工神经网络的潜力，为高效神经形态计算提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) exploit event-driven and addition-only computation to substantially improve efficiency for intelligent computation. A key temporal property of SNNs, elastic inference, allows outputs to emerge progressively, enabling responses to salient inputs much earlier than full evaluation. However, existing SNN-specific accelerators cannot capitalize on this property. Layer-by-layer designs emit outputs only after all layers are complete, while time-step-by-time-step designs rely on coarse-grained, layer-wise pipelines that require synchronizing all spines/tokens within a layer. This barrier prevents results from being forwarded immediately, delaying the earliest possible response and forfeiting the benefits of elastic inference.
  To address these challenges, we propose ELSA, a near-SRAM dataflow architecture that realizes true elastic inference through a fine-grained spine/token-wise pipeline and hardware optimizations tailored to SNNs. ELSA forwards each spine/token immediately upon production, forming a continuous streaming pipeline that substantially reduces the latency to the first response. To enhance this lightweight execution, ELSA introduces a bundled address event representation protocol to lower communication traffic of network-on-chip (NoC), and leverages mini-batch spiking Gustavson-product to cut memory access and exploit inherent sparsity. Combined with mapping and scheduling optimizations, ELSA achieves efficient, event-driven computation without compromising accuracy. Experiments show that SNNs can outperform quantized artificial neural networks (QANNs) while maintaining on-par accuracy. For a 4-bit ResNet-50, ELSA achieves 3.4$\times$ speedup and 13.6$\times$ higher energy efficiency over the SOTA QANN accelerator (ANT), and 2.9$\times$ speedup and 22.1$\times$ energy efficiency gains over the SOTA SNN accelerator (PAICORE).

</details>


### [223] [Supporting Dynamic Control-Flow Execution for Runtime Reconfigurable Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21203)
*Hassan Nassar,Rafik Youssef,Lars Bauer,Jörg Henkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文为运行时可重构处理器引入了动态控制流执行机制（支持循环、条件跳转和异常处理），在四个计算密集型应用中实现了相比通用处理器的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算能力提升方法面临瓶颈。专用硬件加速器虽能提升性能，但占用空间且常处于闲置状态。运行时可重构处理器通过动态切换加速器解决了这一问题，但其微码程序缺乏对动态控制流（如循环、条件跳转、异常处理）的支持，限制了计算密集型应用的执行效率。

Method: 为运行时可重构处理器的微码引入动态控制流执行机制，具体支持循环、条件跳转和异常处理功能。选取四个计算密集型应用（目标检测、海洋运动模拟、人工智能和安全领域）作为基准测试，验证动态控制流机制的有效性。

Result: 在四个不同领域的计算密集型应用上进行了基准测试。实验结果表明，相比在通用处理器上执行，支持动态控制流的可重构处理器能够实现显著的性能加速。

Conclusion: 动态控制流执行机制成功扩展了运行时可重构处理器的能力，使其能够高效执行计算密集型应用，同时保持了可重构架构的灵活性和资源利用优势。

Abstract: As the need for more computing power grows, traditional methods are hitting limits. To boost performance, we're expanding Central Processing Unit (CPU) capabilities and using specialized hardware accelerators. For example, mobile devices usually have cameras, video encoding, and audio accelerators. To perform the different tasks, these accelerators execute microcode programs. These accelerators, however, take up space and often sit idle. Reconfigurable processors offer a solution. They have a normal core connected to several accelerator slots. These accelerator slots can be filled during runtime to accommodate the application running. Once one application finishes and another application is running, the accelerators can be switched. For example, playing music after using the camera.
  In this work, we introduce dynamic control-flow execution for the microcode of runtime reconfigurable processors, i.e., support for loops, conditional jumps, and exception handling. We benchmark using four different applications from four domains (object detection, ocean movement simulation, artificial intelligence and security) that all are compute-intensive and would require the dynamic control-flow when executed on reconfigurable processors. We show that the dynamic control-flow allows different applications to be executed with significant speedup in comparison with execution on general-purpose processors.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [What Do Agents Communicate? Characterizing Information Exchange in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20548)
*Yong Jin Chun,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出CARA技术，通过在智能体间通信中强制包含推理与验证等关键信息，有效缓解多智能体系统中的错误传播问题，成功恢复了高达86.2%的失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的多智能体协作系统虽然通过多样化推理和迭代 refinement 提升了性能，但仍易受错误传播的影响，即早期阶段的信息失真会导致后续推理能力下降。为了解决这一问题，作者旨在通过系统性分析智能体间的通信，找出驱动系统性能的关键信息。

Method: 作者首先对智能体间通信进行了系统性分析，发现通信中缺失推理和验证信息会显著降低性能。基于此发现，提出了Category-Aware Recovery Augmentation (CARA) 技术，该方法通过在通信过程中强制补充这些关键缺失信息来增强协作质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，通信中缺乏推理与验证信息是多智能体系统性能下降的主要原因；应用所提出的CARA技术后，能够成功恢复高达86.2%的失败案例，验证了信息质量在多智能体协作中的核心作用。

Conclusion: 研究强调了高质量信息传递在多智能体协作中的关键作用。确保智能体间通信包含推理与验证等关键信息，对于构建鲁棒、高效的多智能体系统至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled collaborative Multi-Agent (MA) systems, where interacting agents improve performance through diverse reasoning and iterative refinement. However, these systems remain vulnerable to error propagation, where early-stage information degrades downstream reasoning. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis of inter-agent communication to identify which information drives MA performance. We find that the absence of reasoning and verification in inter-agent communication significantly degrades performance. Based on these insights, we propose Category-Aware Recovery Augmentation (technique), which enforces the presence of critical information during communication. recovers up to 86.2% of failed cases. Our results highlight the key role of information quality in effective MA collaboration. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cara_mas

</details>


### [225] [Multi-agent Collaboration with State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20563)
*Mengyang Liu,Taozhi Chen,Zhenhua Xu,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出STORM（面向状态管理）框架，通过在写入时检测和解决冲突，解决多智能体协作中共享代码库的并发冲突问题，在Commit0和PaperBench基准上分别比基线提升18.7和1.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于工作空间隔离（如git worktree）的多智能体系统在并发编辑共享代码库时会产生隐性冲突，导致集成失败；冲突解决被推迟到事后合并阶段，恢复成本高昂。

Method: STORM通过调解智能体与共享工作空间的交互来管理智能体状态，确保每个智能体在代码库的一致视图上操作，并在写入时检测和解决冲突编辑。

Result: 在Commit0-Lite上比git-worktree基线提升+18.7分，在PaperBench上提升+1.4分，同时保持相当或更好的成本效率；结合单智能体运行，在两个基准上分别达到87.6和78.2的最高分。

Conclusion: 显式状态管理是比工作空间隔离更有效的多智能体协作基础，STORM可以无缝集成到任何多智能体系统中。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-agent systems have shown great potential for solving complex tasks. However, when multiple agents edit a shared codebase concurrently, their changes can silently conflict and inconsistent views lead to integration failures. Existing multi-agent systems address this through workspace isolation (e.g., one git worktree per agent), but this defers conflict resolution to a post-hoc merge step where recovery is expensive. In this paper, we propose STORM, i.e., STate-ORiented Management for multi-agent collaboration. Specifically, STORM manages agent states by mediating their interactions with the shared workspace, ensuring that each agent operates on a consistent view of the codebase and that conflicting edits are detected and resolved at write time. We evaluate STORM on Commit0 and PaperBench across multiple LLMs. STORM outperforms the git-worktree-based multi-agent baseline by +18.7 on Commit0-Lite and +1.4 on PaperBench, while achieving comparable or better cost efficiency. Combined with single-agent runs, STORM reaches highest scores of 87.6 and 78.2 on the two benchmarks respectively, suggesting that explicit state management is a more effective foundation for multi-agent collaboration than workspace isolation. STORM can also be plugged into any multi-agent system seamlessly.

</details>


### [226] [ProCrit: Self-Elicited Multi-Perspective Reasoning with Critic-Guided Revision for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20867)
*Yingjia Xu,Jiulong Wu,Bowen Zhang,Baokui Guo,Siyuan Chai,Min Cao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProCrit框架，通过Proposal-Critic双智能体协作实现多模态讽刺检测的自发多视角推理，并利用合成过程级标注和互优化训练提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态讽刺检测方法依赖固定的、预定义的分析视角及手工路由规则，难以适应讽刺机制多样化的样本特性。模型缺乏自主生成所需分析视角并进行连贯推理的能力，且现有数据集缺乏过程级监督信号。

Method: 提出ProCrit框架，包含Proposal Agent和Critic Agent。首先，通过动态角色智能体展开合成过程级推理标注，利用强视觉语言模型生成多角色分析轨迹并扁平化为序列。其次，采用起草-批评-修正范式，由独立Critic识别推理缺陷并提供针对性自然语言反馈。最后，构建互优化训练框架，通过双阶段强化学习联合优化建议起草与反馈引导的修正过程。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的多模态讽刺检测基准数据集上的实验表明，ProCrit方法具有有效性。

Conclusion: ProCrit框架成功实现了样本自适应的多视角推理，通过合成过程级标注和双智能体协作机制，有效解决了现有多模态讽刺检测中的视角固定和缺乏过程监督的问题，为该领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires reasoning over cross-modal incongruities between literal expression and intended meaning, yet the specific analytical perspectives needed vary across samples due to the diversity of sarcastic mechanisms. While recent methods make this analytical process explicit, they still rely on fixed, predefined perspectives that operate independently under hand-crafted routing rules. We argue that multimodal sarcasm detection instead calls for self-elicited multi-perspective reasoning, where a model autonomously generates the perspectives needed for each sample and progressively integrates them into a coherent analysis. To realize this goal, we propose ProCrit, a Proposal-Critic two-agent framework with a proposal agent for multi-perspective reasoning and a critic agent for external evaluation and targeted revision guidance. First, to overcome the lack of process-level supervision in existing sarcasm datasets, ProCrit synthesizes process-level reasoning annotations through a dynamic-role agentic rollout: a strong vision-language model sequentially spawns analytical roles within a shared context, and the resulting multi-role trajectories are flattened into sequences that preserve cross-perspective dependencies while enabling efficient autoregressive generation. Second, to improve reasoning reliability, ProCrit adopts a draft-critique-revise paradigm in which an independent critic identifies reasoning deficiencies and provides targeted natural-language feedback for directed revision. Finally, we develop a mutual-refinement training framework that jointly optimizes proposal drafting and feedback-guided revision via dual-stage reinforcement learning, while refining the critic agent according to the actual effectiveness of its feedback. Experiments on three widely used benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ProCrit.

</details>


### [227] [Decoupling Communication from Policy: Robust MARL under Bandwidth Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21085)
*Alexi Canesse,Benoît Goupil,Jesse Read,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SLIM架构，通过解耦通信路径与策略潜在表示，在带宽受限的多智能体强化学习场景下实现了最先进的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体强化学习通信架构存在耦合瓶颈：共享潜在表示同时用于策略执行和智能体间通信。在带宽受限的实际应用（如无人机群搜救）中，减少消息大小会直接限制策略潜在空间，导致性能显著下降。

Method: 1. 引入β指标：标准化的单智能体带宽预算，将稀疏性、通信轮数和消息维度统一为可比较的约束条件；2. 提出SLIM架构：将通信路径与策略潜在表示解耦，使带宽限制独立于策略容量，同时保持同步通信优势。

Result: 在多个部分可观测MARL基准测试上达到最先进性能；在有限通信条件下展现出良好的可扩展性和鲁棒性；随着带宽减少，性能仅有边际退化。

Conclusion: 解耦通信与策略表示是解决带宽受限MARL通信问题的有效途径，SLIM架构成功实现了这一目标，在保持通信效率的同时避免了性能显著下降。

Abstract: Communication enables coordination in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but many real-world applications, e.g., search-and-rescue with drone swarms, operate under severe bandwidth constraints. Many communication architectures still expose a coupled bottleneck in which a shared latent representation is used for both policy execution and inter-agent communication. Consequently, reducing message size directly limits the policy's latent space, often leading to significant performance degradation. We address this with two contributions. First, we introduce $β$, a normalised per-agent bandwidth budget that unifies sparsity, rounds, and message dimension into a single comparable constraint. Second, we provide SLIM, a minimal architecture that decouples the communication pathway from the policy's latent representation, allowing us to isolate the effect of bandwidth from the effect of policy capacity while benefiting from in-step communication. We evaluate our method on several partially-observable MARL benchmarks, where communication is essential. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance and exhibits scalability and robustness under limited communication, with only marginal degradation as bandwidth is reduced.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Cloud-Native Operation of Roadside Infrastructure Enabling Demand-Driven Collective Perception via V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21145)
*Lukas Zanger,Fabian Thomsen,Guido Linden,Jean-Pierre Busch,Lennart Reiher,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kubernetes集群的云原生架构，用于分布式路侧基础设施的运营，并通过需求驱动的编排方法，仅在需要时部署资源密集型服务，从而实现节能和提高可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着路侧单元数量增长和功能范围扩展，智能路侧基础设施的可扩展和高效运营面临挑战，需要一种新的解决方案来优化资源利用和降低能耗。

Method: 设计了一个跨越路侧单元和云服务器的Kubernetes集群云原生架构，实现需求驱动的编排方法，以V2X集体感知应用为代表用例，仅在联网车辆附近时按需部署服务，并在亚琛测试场进行真实环境验证。

Result: 验证表明集体感知应用能够及时启动供车辆使用，无需求时应用保持非活动状态，减少了能耗、信道拥塞和硬件磨损；通过分析V2X记录估算了需求驱动运营的节能潜力。

Conclusion: 结果证明了云原生、需求驱动的路侧基础设施运营在实际中的可行性，并显示其在未来C-ITS部署中提升可扩展性和（能源）效率的潜力。

Abstract: Intelligent roadside infrastructure is a key enabler for cooperative intelligent transport systems (C-ITS), supporting vehicles equipped with automated driving systems (ADS), e.g., through enhanced environment perception. With a growing number and an expanding functional scope of roadside units, scalable and efficient operation becomes a challenge. This paper presents a cloud-native architecture for the operation of distributed roadside infrastructure based on a Kubernetes cluster spanning roadside units and a cloud server. Building on this architecture, a demand-driven orchestration approach is implemented to dynamically deploy resource-intensive services only when required. As a representative use case, a V2X-based collective perception application is deployed on-demand when a connected vehicle is nearby. The approach is validated in a real-world experiment in our test field in Aachen, demonstrating that the collective perception application starts in time for the vehicle to benefit from it. Without any demand, the application remains inactive, reducing energy consumption, channel congestion, and hardware wear. Beyond the primary evaluation, V2X recordings from the test field are analyzed to estimate the energy-saving potential of demand-driven operation. In summary, the results demonstrate the practical feasibility of cloud-native, demand-driven operation of roadside infrastructure and indicate its potential to improve scalability and (energy) efficiency in future C-ITS deployments.

</details>


### [229] [Hypergraph Partitioning on GPU with Distinct Incident Hyperedges and Size Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20497)
*Marco Ronzani,Cristina Silvano*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向GPU的多级超图划分算法，针对具有分区大小受限和入边互异约束的问题，相比串行算法平均加速380倍，连通性降低1.2-2.0倍，并在k-way平衡划分中比CPU方法快5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图划分是一个NP难问题，在工程领域频繁出现。其大规模高效求解依赖于并行计算。现有方法在处理深度嵌套遍历、并发决策以及特定约束（分区大小受限、入边互异）下的集合操作时面临挑战，需要针对GPU的层次化并行特性设计专门的算法。

Method: 设计围绕GPU层次化并行特性的算法：在形成分区时，物化超图的关联结构和唯一邻域以利用集合稀疏性，并在共享内存中批量计算节点配对得分；在优化分区时，将节点移动链接为改进路径和环，通过对移动进行并行归约累积集合大小变化来检查有效性，使得核心kernel的跨度与局部超图参数呈线性关系。

Result: 相比串行多级划分器，平均加速380倍，连通性降低1.2-2.0倍。在k-way平衡划分中，比CPU方法快5倍，k=2时质量损失约5%，与现有GPU划分器运行时间相当，且额外约束处理逻辑无显著开销。

Conclusion: 该GPU中心化算法成功解决了特定约束下的超图划分问题，在保持或略微牺牲划分质量的前提下，显著提升了计算速度，证明了针对GPU架构和问题特性协同设计算法的有效性。

Abstract: Hypergraph partitioning is a recurring NP-hard problem in engineering; its efficient solution at scale hinges on parallelism. This work proposes a GPU-centric algorithm for multi-level hypergraph partitioning aimed at a specific set of problem constraints: limited size and distinct inbound hyperedges per partition. Manipulating hypergraphs requires deeply nested traversals and concurrent decision-making; our constraints impose further set operations amidst that. In turn, we design algorithms around the GPU's hierarchical parallelism and our problem's specifics. When forming partitions, we materialize the hypergraph's incidence structure and unique neighborhoods in memory to exploit set sparsity and batch node-pairing scores in shared memory. Upon refining partitions, we chain node moves into improving paths and cycles, checking their validity via cumulative set size variations reduced in parallel over moves. Thus, our dominant kernels exhibit a span linear in local hypergraph parameters. Results show an average 380x speedup and a 1.2-2.0x reduction in connectivity compared to a sequential multi-level partitioner. With minor changes, we also support k-way balanced partitioning, running 5x faster than CPU methods with a ~5% quality loss for k=2, outperforming an existing GPU partitioner at comparable runtime, with no measurable overhead from the added constraints handling logic.

</details>


### [230] [Hybrid Edge-HPC Systems for Low-Latency Data-Driven Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20532)
*Liubov Kurafeeva,Ryan Hartung,Benjamin Carter,Alan Subedi,Avhishek Biswas,Michael Fay,Shantenu Jha,Chandra Krintz,Andre Merzky,Douglas Thainand Memet Can Vuran,Rich Wolski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出RBF架构，通过边缘轻量代理模型实现低延迟推理，异步整合HPC仿真驱动的高保真模型更新，解决了边缘响应需求与HPC批量调度延迟之间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴信息物理系统需要从流式传感器数据进行低延迟推理，同时模型需反映复杂演化的物理过程。然而模型更新依赖远程HPC系统上的高保真仿真和训练，其批处理调度特性与边缘所需的响应性存在根本性不匹配。

Method: RBF架构将低延迟边缘推理与异步仿真驱动的模型改进集成，在边缘部署轻量代理模型，异步整合HPC生成的改进模型。该架构重新诠释HPC回填机制，利用机会计算提升模型精度而非系统利用率，支持可插拔代理模型，编排跨边缘设备、私有5G、云和HPC资源的异构基础设施。

Result: 在真实数字农业部署中实例化RBF，将边缘感知与CFD仿真耦合以推断农业温室气流模式。评估量化了仿真延迟、训练成本、推理吞吐量以及延迟模型更新对预测精度的影响，结果表明RBF能在延迟和不规则模型更新情况下实现持续低延迟推理并随时间提升模型保真度。

Conclusion: RBF成功解决了仿真约束场景下边缘推理与HPC模型更新的矛盾，实现了持续低延迟推理与模型保真度随时间提升的统一，为需要高保真物理建模的边缘计算应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Emerging cyber-physical systems increasingly require low-latency inference from streaming sensor data while maintaining models that reflect complex and evolving physical processes. In many domains, however, model updates depend on high-fidelity simulations and training executed on remote high-performance computing (HPC) systems under batch scheduling. This creates a fundamental mismatch between the responsiveness required at the edge and the cost, throughput, and availability of simulation-driven model updates.
  We present RBF (Reverse Backfill), a hybrid edge-HPC learning and inference architecture that integrates low-latency edge inference with asynchronous, simulation-driven model improvement. RBF targets simulation-bounded settings in which model updates are constrained by simulation throughput and HPC scheduling delays, and reinterprets HPC backfilling by using opportunistic computation to improve model accuracy rather than system utilization. RBF decouples inference from simulation and training by deploying lightweight surrogate models at the edge while incorporating improved models asynchronously as they become available. The architecture supports pluggable surrogate models and orchestrates computation across heterogeneous infrastructure spanning edge devices, private 5G, cloud, and HPC resources.
  We instantiate RBF using a real-world digital agriculture deployment that couples edge sensing with computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations to infer airflow patterns in a large agricultural screenhouse. Our evaluation characterizes end-to-end system behavior under realistic constraints, quantifying simulation latency, training cost, inference throughput, and the impact of delayed model updates on prediction accuracy. Results demonstrate that RBF enables continuous, low-latency inference while improving model fidelity over time despite delayed and irregular model updates.

</details>


### [231] [Llamas on the Web: Memory-Efficient, Performance-Portable, and Multi-Precision LLM Inference with WebGPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20706)
*Reese Levine,Rithik Sharma,Nikhil Jain,Abhijit Ramesh,Zheyuan Chen,Neha Abbas,James Contini,Tyler Sorensen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LlamaWeb是一个基于WebGPU的llama.cpp浏览器后端，通过静态内存规划和可调优内核库实现高效的浏览器端LLM推理，内存占用降低29-33%，解码吞吐量提升45-69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在浏览器中运行语言模型具有构建高效、隐私保护和可移植AI应用的独特优势，但面临受限的内存可用性和异构硬件目标的挑战。现有浏览器端LLM框架存在内存开销大、跨设备性能差异显著等问题，亟需一种内存高效且性能可移植的解决方案。

Method: 提出LlamaWeb，一个面向llama.cpp的WebGPU后端。核心设计包括：(1) 通过静态内存规划和高效模型加载显著降低内存开销；(2) 通过可调优内核库解决跨设备变异性问题；(3) 引入模板化GPU内核支持多种量化格式的高性能实现，实现广泛的模型支持和格式可扩展性。

Result: 在来自8个厂商的16个设备上，使用10个语言模型和四种模型权重格式进行评估。与现有浏览器端LLM框架相比：(1) 内存占用减少29-33%（跨多种设备、浏览器和操作系统组合）；(2) 解码吞吐量提升45-69%（跨四个不同厂商的GPU）；(3) 与其他llama.cpp后端相比，在某些设备上具有竞争力甚至超越厂商特定后端性能。

Conclusion: LlamaWeb成功实现了浏览器端LLM推理的内存高效性和跨设备性能可移植性，为构建高效、隐私保护和可移植的浏览器AI应用提供了可行方案，证明了WebGPU作为浏览器端LLM推理平台的潜力。

Abstract: Running language models in the browser presents a unique opportunity to build efficient, private, and portable AI applications, but requires contending with constrained memory availability and heterogeneous hardware targets. To realize this opportunity, we present Llamas on the Web (LlamaWeb), a WebGPU backend for llama$.$cpp that enables memory-efficient and performance-portable LLM inference across a wide range of model weight formats in the browser. Our design significantly reduces memory overhead through static memory planning and efficient model loading, addresses cross-device variability through a tunable kernel library, and introduces templated GPU kernels that support performant implementations of numerous quantization formats, enabling broad model support and extensibility to new formats.
  We evaluate LlamaWeb on 16 devices from 8 vendors, collecting data from 10 language models and four model weight formats. We compare LlamaWeb against existing browser-based LLM frameworks and find that LlamaWeb requires 29-33% less memory across several combinations of device, browser, and operating system. We also evaluate LlamaWeb's performance against these frameworks and find that it increases decode throughput by 45-69% across four GPUs from separate vendors. In addition, we compare LlamaWeb's performance against other llama$.$cpp backends, where it is competitive with and even beats vendor-specific backend performance on some devices.

</details>


### [232] [Instant GPU Efficiency Visibility at Fleet Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20799)
*Connor Pedersen,Dong H. Ahn,Michel Migdal,Collin Neale,Nik Konyuchenko*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了OFU（Overall FLOP Utilization），一种基于硬件计数器、与精度无关的GPU效率指标，能够无需应用插桩即可在HPC系统中实时监测AI工作负载性能，实测与MFU相关性达0.78，预测误差≤2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的应用级MFU（Model FLOPs Utilization）指标需要应用插桩、依赖框架计算，难以在大规模GPU集群中进行统一、连续的效率监测，因此需要一种硬件级、通用且易部署的效率指标。

Method: 基于GPU片上性能计数器（Tensor Pipe Activity和SM时钟频率）推导出OFU指标；通过H100和GB200上的受控GEMM实验，系统表征了OFU近似的五个特性（分块量化、浮点精度缩放、时钟采样噪声、Tensor Core时钟域、非张量操作低估）；提出分块量化校正方法。

Result: 经分块量化校正后，OFU预测应用级MFU的误差≤2个百分点；在608个生产训练任务中，OFU与MFU的相关系数r=0.78；发现了两个框架级FLOPs计算错误；在实际部署中检测到2.5倍的效率退化，并追踪了混合精度训练中的精度相关利用率变化。

Conclusion: OFU是一种实用、部署就绪的硬件级效率指标，可作为应用级MFU的有效补充，适用于大规模GPU集群的连续效率监测。

Abstract: We present Overall FLOP Utilization (OFU), a hardware-level, precision-agnostic GPU efficiency metric for AI workloads on HPC systems, derived from two on-chip performance counters: Tensor Pipe Activity and SM clock frequency. OFU requires no application instrumentation and works across GPU generations and numeric precisions. We characterize five properties of the OFU approximation -- tile quantization, floating-point precision scaling, clock sampling noise, Tensor Core clock domains, and non-tensor undercounting -- through controlled GEMM experiments on H100 and GB200 across FP16, TF32, FP8, and NVFP4. After tile-quantization correction, OFU predicts application-level MFU to within <=2 percentage points. Against 608 production training jobs, OFU achieves r = 0.78 correlation with application-level MFU and surfaces two framework-level FLOPs miscalculations. Deployed across large-scale GPU fleets, OFU has detected a 2.5x efficiency regression and tracked precision-dependent utilization changes in mixed-precision pretraining. Our evaluation and operational experience suggest OFU is a practical, deployment-ready complement to application-level MFU for continuous fleet-wide efficiency monitoring.

</details>


### [233] [PlexRL: Cluster-Level Orchestration of Serviceized LLM Execution for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20863)
*Yiqi Zhang,Fangzheng Jiao,Tian Tang,Boyu Tian,Hangyu Wang,Qiaoling Chen,Guoteng Wang,Zhen Jiang,Peng Sun,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu,Ziming Liu,Menghao Zhang,Yanmin Jia,Yang You,Siyuan Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PlexRL，一种集群级运行时系统，通过跨RLVR作业复用统一LLM服务来消除结构性的空闲时间，最多可减少37.58%的用户GPU小时成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）训练效率极低：长尾展开、工具诱导停滞以及展开与训练间非对称资源需求导致大量空闲时间，且无法通过作业内优化（如同步流水线、异步展开）消除。作者认为这种低效是结构性的，不同作业间的空闲时间存在反相关性，可在集群层面加以利用。

Method: 提出PlexRL，一种集群级运行时系统，通过集中管理模型放置、状态转换和函数级调度，在严格亲和性约束下跨RLVR作业复用统一LLM服务，以时间切片方式填充空闲时段，同时避免昂贵的模型迁移开销。

Result: 实验表明，PlexRL显著提升了集群有效容量，在保持算法灵活性和引入最小单作业开销的前提下，最多减少37.58%的用户GPU小时成本。

Conclusion: RLVR训练的低效性可在集群层面通过跨作业复用LLM服务来解决，PlexRL提供了一种高效、低成本且算法无关的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently unlocked strong reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), triggering rapid exploration of new algorithms and data. However, RLVR training is notoriously inefficient: long-tailed rollouts, tool-induced stalls, and asymmetric resource requirements between rollout and training introduce substantial idle time that cannot be eliminated by job-local optimizations such as synchronous pipelining, asynchronous rollout, or colocated execution.
  We argue that this inefficiency is structural. While idle gaps are unavoidable within individual RLVR jobs, they are largely anti-correlated across jobs and therefore exploitable at the cluster level. Leveraging this observation, we present PlexRL, a cluster-level runtime for multiplexing unified LLM services across RLVR jobs. By centrally managing model placement, state transitions, and function-level scheduling under strict affinity constraints, PlexRL time-slices LLM execution across jobs to fill otherwise idle periods without expensive model migration. Our implementation and evaluations demonstrate that PlexRL significantly improves effective cluster capacity and reduces user GPU hour cost by maximum 37.58% while preserving algorithmic flexibility and introducing minimal per-job overhead.

</details>


### [234] [Ark: Offchain Transaction Batching in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20952)
*Pim Keer,Matteo Maffei,Marco Argentieri,Andrew Camilleri,Zeta Avarikioti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ark，一种兼容比特币的提交链方案，允许用户无需预先锁定资金即可接收链下支付，且状态更新仅需相关用户参与，实现了高效的链下扩容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为市值最大的加密货币，其共识算法的可扩展性限制阻碍了广泛采用。现有Layer-2方案（如闪电网络）存在部署门槛高（需锁定资金、复杂的辅助协议）的问题，而支付池、侧链和Rollup等方案受限于比特币脚本功能或需所有参与者更新状态。因此，需要一种易部署、非托管且高效的比特币链下扩容方案。

Method: 提出Ark协议，一种比特币兼容的提交链（commit-chain）。Ark通过不可信运营商将虚拟UTXO（VTXO）聚合为简洁的链上承诺，实现链下交易。用户无需预先锁定资金即可接收链下支付，且状态更新仅需相关用户参与。论文形式化定义了Ark协议并证明其安全性，同时发现并修复了测试网实现中的两种攻击。

Result: 实验评估表明，Ark能够以约200 vB的恒定大小在链上提交任意数量的VTXO批次。协作退出每用户仅需添加一个输出，单边退出对于n个VTXO的批次需要O(log n)笔交易，每个VTXO约150 vB。已在测试网发现并修复两种攻击，修复方案已集成至主网实现。

Conclusion: Ark作为首个比特币兼容的提交链，成功解决了现有Layer-2方案的部署复杂性和资金锁定问题，实现了高效、灵活的链下交易，为比特币扩容提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Bitcoin is the cryptocurrency with the largest market capitalisation, but its widespread adoption is fundamentally limited by the scalability constraints of its consensus algorithm, which requires every transaction to be confirmed onchain. To address this, several Layer-2 scalability solutions have been proposed to move payments offchain -- most notably, the Lightning Network. However, their deployment remains hindered by cumbersome setup requirements: users must lock funds onchain to participate and engage in complex auxiliary protocols (e.g., for channel rebalancing, top-ups, and routing). Other solutions, like payment pools, sidechains and rollups, cannot be implemented in a non-custodial way on Bitcoin due to its limited scripting capabilities, or require all protocol participants to update the offchain state.
  In this work, we present Ark, the first Bitcoin-compatible commit-chain. Ark enables offchain transactions of virtual UTXOs (VTXOs), through an untrusted operator who aggregates them into succinct onchain commitments. A distinctive feature of Ark is its ease of deployment: users can receive offchain payments without locking any funds beforehand and Ark state updates can be performed only requiring the users involved in that update.
  We formally define the Ark protocol and prove its security. During this process, we identified two attacks affecting the testnet implementation, which we responsibly disclosed and proposed fixes for, which have been now integrated into the mainnet implementation. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that Ark can commit onchain to batches of arbitrarily many VTXOs with a constant-sized footprint of approximately 200 vB. Cooperative exits add one output per user, while unilateral exits require $\mathcal{O}(\log n)$ transactions of roughly 150 vB per VTXO for a batch of $n$ VTXOs.

</details>


### [235] [Diagnosing Overhead in Dispatch Operations: Cross-architecture Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20982)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Koestler,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文挑战了MoE专家并行中AlltoAll优化的两个核心假设，发现路由不平衡是模型内在特性而非系统层可修正的问题，并识别出五种MoE架构可划分为两个稳定负载带，为互连和分发设计提供了正确的工作负载输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AlltoAll分发瓶颈的四类优化方案（预测性样本放置、自适应专家重布局、分层集合通信、EP感知拓扑）均基于两个假设：路由不平衡可由系统层修正，以及模拟token基准测试能真实代表生产环境路由行为。本文旨在检验这两个假设的有效性。

Method: 提出DODOCO框架，对五种MoE检查点（涵盖MLA、MHA、GQA、Mamba-2、GDN五种序列混合器设计）进行插桩分析。在H100上采用5×6网格数据条件，配合4到32 rank的EP扫描进行系统测试，对比真实文本与模拟token的路由行为。

Result: 两个核心假设均失效：(1) EP扩展在各架构可测范围内对每专家最大/平均token比率影响不超过5%，路由瓶颈源于模型路由决策而非专家在rank上的分布，是内在特性；(2) 模拟token高估路由基尼系数达2.35倍，并伪造了批处理规模扩展趋势。意外发现：五种架构分化为两个稳定负载带——MHA和Mamba-2为数据弹性型（基尼系数0.105-0.150），MLA和GDN为持续集中型（基尼系数>0.24），GQA为中间案例。

Conclusion: 路由不平衡是模型内在的路由决策特性，而非可通过系统层修正的问题。AlltoAll感知的互连和分发设计应以架构负载带而非EP程度或模拟数据特征为正确的工作负载输入，这为未来MoE系统优化指明了方向。

Abstract: AlltoAll dispatch is the dominant bottleneck of MoE expert parallelism, and the interconnect community has responded with four families of mitigations: predictive sample placement, adaptive expert relayout, hierarchical collectives, and EP-aware topology. All four rest on two assumptions about the workload. The first is that routing imbalance is correctable by the system layer. The second is that the mock-token benchmarks evaluating them faithfully represent production routing. We introduce DODOCO to test both assumptions.
  We instrument five MoE checkpoints spanning five sequence-mixer designs (DeepSeek-V2-Lite MLA, DeepSeek-MoE-16B MHA, Qwen3-30B GQA, Nemotron-30B Mamba-2, Qwen3.5-35B GDN) under a 5 by 6 grid of data conditions plus a matched EP scan from 4 to 32 ranks on H100s; both assumptions fail. Scaling EP changes the per-expert max/mean token ratio by at most 5% within every architecture's measurable range: the straggler is intrinsic to the routing decision the model makes, not to how its experts land on ranks. Mock tokens overestimate routing Gini by up to a factor of 2.35 and fabricate a batch-size scaling trend that vanishes the moment real text replaces random IDs.
  A third pattern, unexpected, emerges from the same matrix: the five architectures cleave into two stable bands. MHA and Mamba-2 (data-resilient) drop to Gini 0.105 and 0.150 on wikitext. MLA and GDN (persistently concentrated) stay above 0.24 on every real-text condition and reach 0.29 to 0.38 on mock. GQA is the intermediate case. These bands, not the EP degree or the mock-data profile, are the right workload input to AlltoAll-aware interconnect and dispatch design.

</details>


### [236] [NanoCP: Request-Level Dynamic Context Parallelism for Data-Expert Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21100)
*Jiefei Chen,Binbin Lin,Jinming Ma,Jiangfei Duan,Haojie Duanmu,Hao Liu,Qinxiu Cheng,Xiuhong Li,Zhilin Pei,Hui Wang,Xingcheng Zhang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出MoE系统，通过动态上下文并行（DCP）解耦MoE通信与KV缓存放置，根据请求KV足迹动态分配并行度，显著提升吞吐量并降低尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE服务系统将请求的注意力计算、MoE通信和KV缓存绑定在单一实例上，由于注意力延迟随KV缓存大小增长，MoE通信延迟随批大小增长，这种绑定无法同时平衡两者，导致EP落后者和KV内存碎片化；现有上下文并行采用统一并行度，通信和注意力开销过高。

Method: 提出DCP动态上下文并行，根据请求的KV足迹动态分配上下文并行度：长请求将注意力分布到多个实例，短请求保持本地化；引入提前时间图引擎和基于路由的自定义通信后端，将DCP与静态执行桥接。

Result: 在严格的TPOT SLO约束下，请求吞吐量提升1.88×-3.27×；显著缓解落后者问题，P99尾部延迟降低1.79×-2.12×。

Conclusion: DCP有效实现了KV缓存在集群间的"液化"，平衡了每实例KV缓存占用和批大小，避免了不必要的负载均衡开销，证明了动态并行策略在MoE服务系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Modern serving systems for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models adopt hybrid data-expert parallelism: expert parallelism (EP) shards experts across GPUs to scale capacity, while data parallelism (DP) replicates attention layers across instances to process independent requests. Existing systems bind each request's attention, MoE communication, and KV cache to a single instance. Because attention latency scales with KV cache size while MoE communication latency scales with batch size, this binding cannot balance both simultaneously, producing EP stragglers; it also fragments KV memory across instances, inflating tail latency under long contexts. While existing context parallelism (CP) mitigates these constraints, its uniform parallelism degree incurs prohibitive communication and attention-side overheads.
  We present \work, which decouples MoE communication from KV cache placement and achieves dual balance through dynamic context parallelism (DCP). DCP assigns each request a context-parallel degree sized to its KV footprint: long requests distribute attention across multiple instances; short requests remain local.
  This dynamic parallelism effectively liquefies the KV cache across the cluster, balancing both the per-instance KV cache occupancy and batch sizes without unnecessary load-balancing costs. To bridge DCP with static execution, \work introduces an ahead-of-time (AOT) graph engine paired with a custom routing-based communication backend. Experimental results show that \work maintains up to $1.88\times$--$3.27\times$ higher request rates under strict time-per-output-token (TPOT) service level objectives (SLOs). Furthermore, \work significantly mitigates stragglers, reducing P99 tail latency by up to $1.79\times$--$2.12\times$.

</details>


### [237] [Automated Byzantine-Resilient Clustered Decentralized Federated Learning for Battery Intelligence in Connected EVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21115)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Abdelaziz Amara Korba,Yacine Ghamri-Doudane*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ABC-DFL框架，这是一个用于网联电动汽车的自动化拜占庭容错聚类去中心化联邦学习系统。该框架利用区块链和oracle节点替代中心服务器，并通过核心算法FLECA有效抵御恶意攻击，在保证良性条件下性能的同时，显著提升了对抗场景下的安全性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电动汽车电池数据联邦学习框架大多依赖中心化聚合方案，这在安全性和信任机制方面存在关键缺陷，容易受到单点故障和恶意攻击的影响。因此，迫切需要一种既能保护隐私又能有效抵御拜占庭攻击的去中心化解决方案。

Method: 提出ABC-DFL框架，采用开放许可链替代中心服务器，引入动态Quorum拜占庭容错(QBFT)协议和基于oracle的聚合层。核心算法FLECA采用分层聚合机制：各EV通过基于参考模型更新的自适应阈值过滤恶意更新；Oracle节点使用鲁棒聚类方法隔离可信EV群组并进行跨组聚合。此外，设计了激励机制以确保系统的公平性。

Result: 实验表明，FLECA在良性条件下收敛性能与FedProx相当；在自适应对抗场景下，攻击影响分数低于0.10，显著优于现有防御方案。多任务模型学习实验验证了激励机制的有效性和公平性。链上和链下基准测试证实了ABC-DFL的实用性。

Conclusion: ABC-DFL成功解决了电动汽车联邦学习中的中心化信任和安全问题，通过去中心化架构和鲁棒的聚合协议实现了对拜占庭攻击的有效防御，为智能交通系统中的隐私保护数据分析提供了一个安全、高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for managing electric vehicle (EV) battery data in intelligent transportation systems (ITS), enabling privacy-preserving tasks such as anomaly detection and capacity estimation. However, most existing frameworks rely on centralized aggregation schemes, which pose critical limitations in terms of security and trust. To address these challenges, we propose ABC-DFL, an automated Byzantine-resilient clustered decentralized federated learning (C-DFL) framework for connected EVs. The proposed incentive-driven C-DFL system replaces the central server with an open-permissioned blockchain, featuring a new dynamic Quorum Byzantine Fault Tolerance (QBFT) protocol and an oracle-based aggregation layer, to enhance trust, security, and automation. At the core of ABC-DFL lies FLECA (Filtered Layered Enhanced Clustering Aggregation), a robust hierarchical aggregation protocol that mitigates Byzantine attacks by having each EV filter malicious updates using an adaptive threshold based on deviations from its reference model update. Oracle nodes, responsible for inter-group aggregation, employ robust clustering to isolate and aggregate model updates from trustworthy EV groups. Comprehensive experimental evaluations demonstrate that FLECA matches FedProx convergence under benign conditions and significantly outperforms existing defenses with attack impact scores below 0.10 in adaptive adversarial scenarios. Furthermore, several learning experiments with multitask models confirm the effectiveness and fairness of the incentive mechanism. Finally, on-chain and off-chain benchmarks validate the practicality of ABC-DFL.

</details>


### [238] [Frontier: Towards Comprehensive and Accurate LLM Inference Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21312)
*Yicheng Feng,Xin Tan,Yangtao Deng,Yimin Jiang,Yibo Zhu,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Frontier，一个面向现代LLM推理服务的离散事件模拟器，通过解耦抽象和细粒度建模，将端到端延迟预测误差从51.7%降低至2.6%，吞吐量预测误差低于4%，支持千卡规模模拟与新兴工作负载验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务系统已从同构单体架构演变为解耦执行、复杂并行和有状态工作负载的异构组合。现有模拟器采用单体副本抽象，无法适配解耦服务架构；且使用平均情况分析代理会导致SLA预测失真甚至得出相反的优化结论。设计空间探索亟需架构完整且具备决策级精度的模拟工具。

Method: 提出Frontier离散事件模拟器，核心采用解耦抽象：建模协同部署、Prefill-Decode解耦(PDD)和Attention-FFN解耦(AFD)，使用角色特定的集群worker；在调度器-批次-引擎循环中集成CUDA Graphs、推测解码等运行时优化；支持推理、智能体、RL等有状态请求；实现对计算、通信、内存成本的精确可泛化预测。

Result: 在16-H800 GPU测试平台上，吞吐量预测平均误差低于4%；相较于SOTA模拟器，协同部署场景下端到端延迟误差从44.9%降至6.4%，解耦场景下从51.7%降至2.6%；可在商用CPU上扩展至1000+ GPU规模；支持SLA依赖的Pareto前沿探索、异构解耦分配、智能体推理调度验证、RL后训练重配置等新场景。

Conclusion: Frontier通过解耦抽象和对现代服务系统结构与动态的细粒度建模，首次实现了面向现代LLM解耦服务架构的高精度、大规模模拟，为复杂服务场景下的设计空间探索和决策优化提供了可靠的模拟基础设施。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving is no longer homogeneous or monolithic. Production systems now combine disaggregated execution, complex parallelism, runtime optimizations, and stateful workloads such as reasoning, agents, and RL rollouts. Simulation is attractive for exploring this growing design space, yet existing simulators lack the architectural completeness and decision-grade fidelity it demands. Their monolithic-replica abstractions are ill-suited to disaggregated serving, while average-case analytical proxies can distort SLA predictions and even reverse optimization conclusions.
  We present Frontier, a discrete-event simulator for modern LLM inference serving. Frontier features a disaggregated abstraction. It captures the structure and dynamics of modern serving systems by modeling co-location, Prefill-Decode Disaggregation (PDD), and Attention-FFN Disaggregation (AFD) with role-specific cluster workers, incorporating key runtime optimizations (e.g., CUDA Graphs, speculative decoding) within the scheduler-batch-engine loop, and supporting stateful requests for emerging workloads. It further provides accurate and generalizable predictions of computation, communication, and memory costs across diverse serving scenarios with complex workload compositions. On 16-H800 GPU testbed, Frontier achieves an average throughput error below 4%. Compared with state-of-the-art simulators, it reduces end-to-end latency error from 44.9% to 6.4% under co-location and from 51.7% to 2.6% under disaggregation. It scales to over 1K GPUs on commodity CPUs and enables new use cases such as SLA-dependent Pareto frontier exploration, heterogeneous disaggregated allocation, agentic reasoning scheduling validation, and RL post-training reconfiguration.

</details>
